### [1] [Token Merging for Multilingual Speech Recognition: A Systematic Study Across Model Scale and Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13151)
*Dylan Luke Holyoak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了 token merging 在 Whisper 多语言语音识别模型上的效果：在16种语言、3种模型规模下，合并 token 可提升推理效率且几乎不损失转录准确率，并与 DoRA 微调兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Whisper 等大规模多语言语音识别模型无需语言特定训练即可转录低资源语言，但部署时计算开销高昂。Token merging 通过动态合并冗余特征、缩短推理序列长度来降低开销且无需重训练，但其在多语言低资源场景及微调后的效果尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在 Whisper 模型家族上系统评估 token merging，覆盖16种不同语言和3种模型规模；并测试 token merging 与低资源语言微调方法 DoRA 的交互作用。

Result: 合并 token 提升了计算效率，在大多数低资源语言和模型规模下转录准确率几乎无损失；即使模型经过微调后依然有效。

Conclusion: Token merging 是一种使多语言语音识别更快、更易低成本部署的高实用性方法。

Abstract: Leading multilingual speech recognition models like Whisper transcribe diverse, low-resource languages without language-specific training but are computationally expensive to deploy. Token merging mitigates this inefficiency by dynamically combining redundant features, shortening the sequence length during inference without requiring retraining. In this paper, we systematically evaluate token merging on the Whisper model family across sixteen diverse languages and three different model sizes. We also test how token merging interacts with fine-tuning (DoRA) on low-resource languages. Our findings show that merging tokens increases computational efficiency with almost no loss in transcription accuracy across most low-resource languages and model sizes, and it works even after the model has been fine-tuned. Our results demonstrate that token merging is a highly practical method for making multilingual speech recognition faster and cheaper to deploy.

</details>


### [2] [PhysMent: An Interactive Approach For LLM Reasoning In Physics Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13152)
*Joseph Chan,Utkarsh Jha,Xiyin Yang,Abhinav Jarajapu,Anik Sahai,Eddie Hu,Robin Jeshua Deepak,Stefano Saravalle,Aditya Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhysMent：通过与 MuJoCo 模拟器进行迭代、工具中介交互来评估大语言模型物理推理能力的基准，包含 105 个经典力学场景，结果显示模型在定性任务上表现尚可，但在需要精确多步实验的定量任务上性能大幅下降，瓶颈在于程序性工具使用而非概念理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态科学基准虽能反映 LLM 的部分能力，但无法衡量模型通过主动实验来推理物理世界的能力。静态基准直接提供所有已知量，回避了信息发现与多步实验设计这一关键环节，因此需要一个新的、基于主动交互的评测框架来揭示模型在真实物理推理中的局限。

Method: 提出 PhysMent 基准，要求模型与 MuJoCo 物理模拟器进行迭代、工具中介交互：通过施力、查询物体状态、推进时间、修改场景几何来主动发现信息后再作答。基准涵盖 105 个经典力学场景，按四种难度范式（Easy/Hard × Single/Multi）、三种场景模态（标准、物体创建、隐藏物体）以及一个场景操纵类别组织，并采用六维评分框架进行评估。

Result: 当前模型在定性单概念任务上表现较好（最高约 80% 准确率），但在需要精确、多步实验流程的定量任务上显著退化，多数模型在最难的单一概念类别上低于 30%。七个模型的总体准确率介于 25% 至 67% 之间。失败原因主要在于过早提交答案、探索效率低下以及与模拟器反馈的对接不一致，而非概念性知识缺口。

Conclusion: LLM 在物理推理上的主要瓶颈是程序性的——即自适应多步工具使用能力，而非概念负荷。PhysMent 表明，主动实验型评测能够揭示静态基准所掩盖的能力短板，未来改进应聚焦于多步实验规划、探索效率与可靠的模拟器反馈利用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform strongly on static science benchmarks, yet their ability to reason about the physical world through active experimentation remains poorly understood. We introduce PhysMent, a benchmark that evaluates LLM physical reasoning via iterative, toolmediated interaction with a MuJoCo physics simulator. Unlike static benchmarks that supply all quantities upfront, PhysMent requires models to discover information by applying forces, querying object states, advancing time, and modifying scene geometry before answering. The benchmark comprises 105 scenes of classical mechanics, organized across four difficulty regimes (Easy/Hard and Single/Multi), three scene modalities (standard, object creation, hidden objects), and a scene-manipulation category, evaluated with a six-dimensional scoring framework. Results show that current models perform reasonably well on qualitative single-concept tasks (up to 80% accuracy) but degrade substantially on quantitative tasks that demand precise, multi-step experimental procedures: most models fall below 30% on the hardest single-concept category, where the bottleneck is procedural (adaptive multi-step tool use) rather than conceptual load. Across the seven models, accuracy ranges from 25% to 67%, with failures due to premature answer submission, inefficient exploration, and inconsistent grounding in simulator feedback rather than conceptual gaps.

</details>


### [3] [Lexical Prompt Compression for Large Language Models: A Training-Free, Deterministic Pipeline with Empirical Pareto Analysis Across Eleven Task Categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13154)
*Shamin Chokshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一套免训练、完全确定性、仅CPU的经典词汇级NLP提示压缩流水线（11种可开关变换、15种配置），在1242条英文提示上评测，最激进配置平均减少40.3% token（BERTScore-F1=0.876），仅停用词配置减少29.6%（0.913），并刻画了各任务类别的压缩-保真度帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM提示日益冗长（数千token），推高推理成本与延迟；现有学习式压缩方法（LLMLingua、Selective Context）需辅助语言模型且非确定性，故探索经典词汇NLP在免训练、确定性、纯CPU条件下的压缩上限。

Method: 将11种可开关的词汇变换（停用词移除、填充短语删除、缩略与缩写替换、基于词性的剪枝、词形还原、WordNet同义替换、命名实体保护等）组装为可配置流水线；在六大数据源共1242条英文提示（覆盖11个自动推导的任务类别）上评测15种配置，生成18,630对GPT-4o-mini配对补全；用BLEU、ROUGE-1/2/L、BERTScore-F1、SentenceBERT余弦相似度衡量输出保持度。

Result: 最激进配置平均token减少40.3%（σ=9.2），对应BERTScore-F1为0.876；仅停用词配置减少29.6%，BERTScore-F1达0.913；按任务类别刻画了压缩-保真度帕累托前沿，发现常识推理在激进压缩下是系统性失效模式。

Conclusion: 经典词汇级、免训练、确定性的CPU压缩流水线可在质量显著下降前实现可观压缩（约30%–40%），但收益与保真度存在任务相关的权衡，尤其常识推理任务对激进压缩敏感；全部代码、提示与逐单元结果已开源以保证可复现。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made prompts increasingly large and complex. Techniques such as chain-of-thought reasoning (Wei et al., 2022) and in-context learning (Brown et al., 2020) frequently push real-world prompts past several thousand tokens, increasing inference cost and latency. Learned compression methods such as LLMLingua (Jiang et al., 2023) and Selective Context (Li et al., 2023) achieve high compression ratios but require auxiliary language models and are non-deterministic. We ask a complementary question: how far can a training-free, fully deterministic, CPU-only pipeline based on classical lexical NLP be pushed before output quality degrades significantly? Eleven toggleable lexical transformations - stopword removal, filler-phrase deletion, contraction and abbreviation substitution, part-of-speech-based pruning, lemmatization, WordNet-driven synonym shortening, and named-entity preservation - are assembled into a configurable pipeline. Fifteen configurations are evaluated on 1,242 English-only prompts from six sources (Dolly-15k, LMSYS-Chat-1M, WildChat-1M, MMLU, GSM8K, HellaSwag), spanning eleven automatically derived task categories, yielding 18,630 paired GPT-4o-mini completions. Output preservation is measured using BLEU, ROUGE-1/2/L, BERTScore-F1, and SentenceBERT cosine similarity. The most aggressive configuration achieves a mean token reduction of 40.3% (sigma = 9.2) at a BERTScore-F1 of 0.876 against the original-prompt output; a stopword-only configuration achieves 29.6% reduction at 0.913. The compression-versus-fidelity Pareto frontier is characterized per task category, with commonsense reasoning a systematic failure mode under aggressive compression. All code, prompts, and per-cell results are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [4] [TestHallVQA: Exploring LVLMs' Document-Level Reasoning under Redundant Contexts from Scientific Exams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13158)
*Yongqi Yu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出TestHallVQA多图像VQA基准，兼具文档级规模与人类考试难度，并给出量化冗余鲁棒性的新指标F1-R²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平面媒体VQA基准存在割裂：长文档理解类推理深度不足，复杂视觉推理类又局限于单页无噪声场景；同时无关视觉token对性能的负面影响虽被理论分析证实，却缺乏系统量化。

Method: 构建TestHallVQA多图像VQA基准，覆盖文档级规模与考试级难度、任务覆盖全面，并支持可控注入多级上下文冗余；在此基础上提出F1-R²指标，联合衡量LVLM的计算推理能力与对文档级冗余的证据检索鲁棒性。

Result: 对主流LVLM的大量实验与分析揭示了其在多个维度上的潜在缺陷，给出了具体洞见与未来研究方向；数据集、代码及完整理论推导已开源。

Conclusion: TestHallVQA与F1-R²为评估LVLM在文档级冗余场景下的推理与检索鲁棒性提供了统一框架，指明了后续研究改进方向。

Abstract: Large Vision--Language Models (LVLMs) are increasingly expected to perform visual question answering (VQA) over planar media. However, existing planar VQA benchmarks typically emphasize isolated challenges: some emphasize long-document understanding with limited reasoning depth, while others require complex visual reasoning but remain restricted to single-page, noise-free settings. Moreover, through theoretical analysis, we identify the impact of irrelevant visual tokens, which leads to measurable performance degradation but has received little attention with respect to systematic quantification. To address these limitations, we introduce TestHallVQA, a multi-image VQA benchmark that simultaneously embodies document-level scale and the difficulty of human examinations, while providing comprehensive task coverage. Leveraging TestHallVQA's ability to controllably inject multi-level contextual redundancy, we further propose a novel metric, F1-R\textsuperscript{2}, which jointly quantifies LVLMs' computational reasoning capability and their evidence retrieval robustness against document-level redundancy. Extensive experiments and analyses on mainstream LVLMs reveal their latent deficiencies across multiple dimensions, offering concrete insights and directions for future research. The associated datasets, code, and complete theoretical derivations are available at https://github.com/yqyu2317/TestHallVQA-benchmark.

</details>


### [5] [Clinical Reasoning Under a Partially Observed Objective in Cone Beam CT Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13238)
*Ajo Babu George,Govind Arun,Sidharth N Krishna,Uma Ranjan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究评估了锥形束CT颌面部报告生成任务：复合目标（80%大模型事实蕴含判断+20%词面重叠）能纠正纯词面指标的偏差，可见的BLEU-4/METEOR倾向奖励"口述惯例"而非解剖事实；所交付系统在未见中心的50例上METEOR达0.3542。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有颌面部CBCT报告生成多依赖BLEU-4、METEOR等词面重叠指标，而开发阶段只能看到词面部分（20%权重）。作者怀疑词面指标奖励的是口述/采集惯例而非影像解剖，因此需要引入以事实蕴含为主的复合评价来检验并优化生成质量。

Method: 1) 用纯Python复现评分器的BLEU-4与METEOR例程，与参考实现达到机器精度一致；2) 构建离线蕴含代理模型（判别报告是否属于同一患者，AUC 0.987），使复合目标可低成本直接优化（80%蕴含+20%词面）；3) 在622例公开数据上对比"按可见词面排序选报告"与"按复合目标选报告"；4) 用2900万参数编码器在公开数据上微调，做985条陈述的患病率加权折外AUC评估；5) 用图像头文件中的9个数值做覆盖度与句子选择的预测对照；6) 交付系统基于极性、侧别与牙位一致性约束，输出8条无条件陈述+5条几何门控陈述。

Result: 可见词面排序选出的报告得分0.2909，复合目标选出为0.4122；追求n-gram重叠使蕴含精确率由0.522降至0.266。29M编码器在985条陈述上的折外AUC仅0.486，与语料先验无区别；而9个头文件数值对下颌骨覆盖度达0.945、髁突覆盖度0.872，且仅用采集中心预测句子选择达0.718，高于图像模型的0.663。交付系统在未见中心的50例上METEOR为0.3542。

Conclusion: 词面指标主要奖励口述惯例而非影像解剖，故以蕴含为主导的复合目标是必要的优化方向；图像端模型几乎无区分力，说明该任务当前更难由影像驱动，数据集与代码已开源。

Abstract: Maxillofacial report generation from cone beam computed tomography is scored here by a composite objective placing 80% of its weight on a large language model judgement of factual entailment and 20% on lexical overlap, of which only the lexical fifth is visible during development. The grader's BLEU-4 and METEOR routines are reproduced in pure Python and match the reference to machine precision, and an offline entailment surrogate, which tells a report written for one patient from one written for another at an area under the curve of 0.987, makes the composite objective cheap enough to optimise directly. Over the 622-case public release, a report selected against the visible lexical ranking scores 0.2909, whereas one selected against the composite objective scores 0.4122, because pursuing n-gram overlap drives entailment precision from 0.522 down to 0.266. A 29 million parameter encoder fine-tuned on the release reaches a prevalence-weighted out-of-fold area under the curve of 0.486 over 985 statements, indistinguishable from the corpus prior, while nine numbers read from the image header reach 0.945 for mandible coverage and 0.872 for condyle coverage, and acquisition centre alone predicts sentence choice at 0.718 against 0.663 for the image-derived model, identifying dictation convention rather than anatomy as the quantity the lexical metrics reward. The delivered system emits eight unconditional statements and five gated on header geometry under polarity, laterality and tooth-level consistency constraints, and reaches METEOR 0.3542 over 50 held-out cases from an unseen centre. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/GIND123/CBCT-Clinical-Reasoner

</details>


### [6] [RFCLLM: Evaluating LLMs' Reasoning Ability of Network Protocol State Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13389)
*Anqi Chen,Dan Goldwasser,Cristina Nita-Rotaru*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估LLM能否正确理解协议规范中的有限状态机（FSM）语义。作者为16种协议设计4类任务、共1482条查询，将LLM由自然语言隐含构建的状态转移系统与人工标注的ground-truth模型对比，并考察评判偏差、任务难度差异、4种上下文类型及协议特征的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络安全与测试中，LLM生成的文本规范到形式化表示的映射常被默认为对规范有完美理解，但这一假设在实践中未必成立；因此需要实证评估LLM对协议规范FSM语义的解读正确程度。

Method: 以自然语言描述的有限状态转移系统为对象，构建人工ground-truth模型；针对16种协议设计4类任务、1482条查询；比较LLM的隐式表示与ground-truth的一致性；并分析不同评判者偏差、任务间固有难度差距、4种上下文类型以及协议特征的影响。

Result: 观察到不同judge bias存在差异，任务之间具有固有难度梯度，4种上下文类型与协议特征均对LLM的FSM推理表现产生影响。

Conclusion: 该工作为检验LLM在协议规范FSM推理中是否真正可信迈出了一步，表明不能默认LLM已完美理解规范。

Abstract: Mapping textual specifications into formal representations is essential for ensuring the correctness of protocol designs and implementations. LLM-generated mappings, used for networking security or testing, are assumed to capture a perfect understanding of the specification, which may not hold in practice. The goal of this paper is to assess the extent to which LLMs can interpret the specification correctly. We examine the degree to which an LLM's implicit representation of a finite-state transition system-defined via natural language descriptions-aligns with a manually generated ground-truth model. We designed 4 tasks and 1482 task queries for 16 protocols. We evaluated different judge biases, observed the inherent difficulty gaps between tasks, looked into the effect of 4 context types, and the influence of protocol characteristics. Our work contributes to a step toward verifying whether LLMs can really be trusted in FSM (Finite State Machine) reasoning of protocol specifications.

</details>


### [7] [CVSS-X: A Multilingual Speech-to-Speech Translation Corpus for 28 Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13413)
*Lucas Rafael Stefanel Gris,Alef Iury Siqueira Ferreira,Frederico Santos de Oliveira,Augusto Seben da Rosa,Alexandre Costa Ferro Filho,Arlindo Rodrigues Galvão Filho,Anderson da Silva Soares*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CVSS-X，一个通过反转翻译方向来扩展 CVSS 的大规模合成语音到语音翻译语料库，支持从英语翻译到 28 种目标语言，规模约为 CVSS 的八倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CVSS 语料库仅支持从 21 种语言翻译为英语，方向单一，限制了双向及多语言语音到语音翻译研究的需求。

Method: 构建 CVSS-X 语料库，涵盖 12 个语系、28 种目标语言，每种语言约 24 万对平行语音，总计超过 16,000 小时。提供两种变体：CVSS-X-C（每种语言两个规范音色）与 CVSS-X-T（跨语言语音克隆），均完全合成生成。

Result: 评估显示翻译质量与 CVSS 相当，且在类型学多样的语言间表现一致。

Conclusion: CVSS-X 与 CVSS 结合可实现双向和多语言语音到语音翻译研究，代码与数据集均已公开。

Abstract: We introduce CVSS-X, a large-scale synthetic speech-to-speech translation corpus that extends CVSS by reversing the translation direction. While CVSS translates from 21 languages into English, CVSS-X enables translation from English into 28 target languages spanning 12 language families. The corpus comprises approximately 240,000 parallel speech pairs per language, totaling over 16,000 hours, eight times larger than CVSS. We provide two variants: CVSS-X-C with two canonical voices per language, and CVSS-X-T with cross-lingual voice cloning, both fully generated. Evaluation shows comparable translation quality to CVSS with consistent performance across typologically diverse languages. Combined with CVSS, this enables research on bidirectional and multilingual speech-to-speech translation. The code is available at https://github.com/ErmisAI/XVSS-X and the dataset under CC-BY-NC 4.0 license at https://huggingface.co/datasets/lgris/XVSS-X.

</details>


### [8] [Causal Analysis and Mitigation of Spurious Onsets in Full-Duplex Speech LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13445)
*Kento Nishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 探究全双工语音LLM在长时间静音下产生"伪语音起始"的成因，并提出一种无需重训练的推理时抑制方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Moshi、PersonaPlex等全双工语音LLM可同时听与说，但在用户长时间静音（数字零输入）时会发生不当起始说话：Moshi 12/40、PersonaPlex 11/40的五分钟续写中出现语音起始，需查明成因并加以抑制。

Method: 提出两个假设：(1)重复采样在持续低起始概率下选中语音；(2)对模型自身非语音输出的条件化导致起始概率突增。通过分析每帧起始概率验证假设。进而提出因果反事实判据：若将前序用户输入静音后，模型下一词分布变化极小，则判定该起始并非对用户语音的响应，从而抑制之。方法为推理时干预，无需重训练。

Result: 在每个观测到的起始处，语音概率在单个80 ms帧内跃升超过九个数量级，支持第二假设。在每模型40次留出试验（含真实麦克风噪声）中，抑制了Moshi 13/13、PersonaPlex 9/9的伪起始，同时保留每模型40/40的真实响应；95百分位决策时间低于61 ms，在80 ms帧预算内。

Conclusion: 伪语音起始源于对模型自身非语音输出的条件化导致的概率骤增，而非采样噪声；基于因果反事实的推理时抑制方法可实时、无重训练地消除伪起始并保留真实响应。

Abstract: Speech-to-speech LLMs like Moshi, and its derivative PersonaPlex, can listen and speak concurrently through full-duplex generation. However, they can begin speaking inappropriately during prolonged user silence: under digital-zero input, Moshi and PersonaPlex initiate speech in 12/40 and 11/40 five-minute continuations, respectively. What causes this spurious speech? We investigate two hypotheses: either repeated sampling selects speech despite persistently low onset probabilities, or conditioning on the model's nonspeech outputs causes an abrupt spike in onset probability. We find that, at every observed onset, speech probability spikes by over nine orders of magnitude in one 80-ms frame, supporting the latter hypothesis. Then, to suppress these onsets without blocking genuine responses, we ask a causal counterfactual question: is the model responding to user speech, or would its next-token distribution remain similar if the preceding user input were muted? Accordingly, we suppress onsets whose distributions change little under this intervention. Across 40 held-out trials per model with realistic microphone noise, our method suppresses 13/13 Moshi and 9/9 PersonaPlex spurious onsets, while preserving 40/40 genuine responses per model. Our inference-time method requires no retraining and runs in real-time, with 95th-percentile decision time below 61 ms, within the 80-ms frame budget. Our code is available at https://github.com/KentoNishi/icassp27-spurious-onsets.

</details>


### [9] [Hindsight Bias in Clinical Temporal Reasoning: How Future Data Exposure Affects Large Language Model Judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13454)
*Misaki Matsuura,Sayantan Kumar,Ojas Kadam,Jeremy C. Weiss*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个配对基准，用于衡量临床时间推理中与后见之明偏差一致的结果条件性偏移。基准包含171份病例报告，配有前瞻性参考答案与结果一致的后见之明陷阱问题，并评估Acc、HTR、AIR、HBR四项指标。实验显示完整时间线暴露会导致模型出现稳定的后见之明敏感偏移，而时间遮蔽可在不降低准确率的情况下减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策本质上是前瞻性的，但临床语言模型常基于揭示最终诊断、治疗反应和结局的回顾性病历进行评估。这种评估可能奖励模型利用未来信息，而非在决策时点的不确定性下进行推理，从而诱发后见之明偏差。

Method: 构建一个配对基准，包含来自PubMed Central开放获取子集的171份病例报告（40例脓毒症、131例GLP-1/糖尿病），每例同时表示为文本叙述以及人工标注和LLM生成的文本时间序列（TTS）。每个病例的问题绑定到临床有意义的截断点，并配对前瞻性参考答案与结果一致的后见之明陷阱。模型分别使用截断于该时点的TTS或完整时间线作答，附加条件变化叙述来源（原始或合成）与TTS标注来源（人工或LLM）。采用准确率（Acc）、后见之明陷阱率（HTR）、答案不稳定率（AIR）和后见之明偏差率（HBR）进行评测。

Result: 在GPT 5.6 Sol、Gemma 4、GLM 5.2和Opus 5上，完整时间线暴露会产生一致的后见之明敏感偏移；时间遮蔽可减少偏差且不降低准确率。

Conclusion: 临床语言模型评估应关注决策时点的不确定性，避免因暴露完整时间线而诱发后见之明偏差；时间遮蔽是一种在不牺牲准确率前提下缓解该偏差的有效策略。

Abstract: Clinical decisions are prospective, but clinical language models are often evaluated on retrospective records that reveal the final diagnosis, treatment response, and outcome. Such evaluations may reward the use of future information rather than reasoning under the uncertainty present at the decision point. We introduce a paired benchmark for measuring outcome-conditioned shifts consistent with hindsight bias in clinical temporal reasoning. It contains 171 case reports from the PubMed Central Open Access Subset---40 sepsis and 131 GLP-1/diabetes cases---represented as both textual narratives and human-annotated and LLM-generated textual time series (TTS). For each case, questions are tied to a clinically meaningful cutoff and paired with a prospective reference answer and an outcome-consistent \emph{hindsight trap}. Models answer each question using either a TTS truncated at the cutoff or the complete timeline; additional conditions vary the narrative source (original or synthetic) and TTS annotation source (human or LLM). We evaluate accuracy (Acc), hindsight trap rate (HTR), answer instability rate (AIR), and hindsight bias rate (HBR), each of which captures different signals of hindsight bias. Across GPT 5.6 Sol, Gemma 4, GLM 5.2, and Opus 5, full timeline exposure produces consistent hindsight-sensitive shifts, while temporal masking reduces bias without lowering accuracy.

</details>


### [10] [A Hybrid Hierarchical 1D-CNN-BiLSTM Framework for Extractive Summarization of Biomedical and Clinical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13481)
*Saad Bin Ather,Muhammad Saif,Ali Hassan Khan,Manzer Abbas,Hajra Waheed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文将抽取式摘要建模为句子选择任务，提出混合分层CNN-LSTM摘要器，通过从源文直接复制句子规避生成式幻觉，在PubMed上优于单独CNN/LSTM基线，并在MIMIC数据集上呈现场景依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的摘要虽流畅，但可能产生事实幻觉，在生物医学与临床领域风险严重。作者希望通过取消生成环节，将摘要重构为抽取式句子选择，从而在结构上保证事实忠实性。

Method: 采用混合分层CNN-LSTM结构：先用堆叠多核卷积将句子级嵌入组合为更丰富的句间表示，再以双向LSTM建模文档长程依赖；轻量评分头输出每句重要性分数，并以oracle抽取标签通过二元交叉熵端到端训练。推理时使用动态均值加标准差阈值，并设top-3回退策略，直接从原文选句并按时间顺序重排成摘要。

Result: 在PubMed上，该架构优于单独CNN与LSTM基线；消融实验显示更宽的卷积感受野可提升句子打分。在MIMIC-CXR与MIMIC-IV BHC上，模型对非结构化叙述表现良好，但在高度模板化报告上退化为依赖位置基线。

Conclusion: 结构约束可作为通向事实有据摘要系统的可靠路径，使系统在设计上可信，而非依赖事后纠错。

Abstract: Large language models have made abstractive summarization remarkably fluent, but generated summaries can hallucinate facts, posing serious risks in biomedical and clinical domains. We address this by removing generation from the pipeline and framing summarization as extractive sentence selection. Our Hybrid Hierarchical CNN-LSTM Summarizer uses stacked multi-kernel convolutions to compose sentence-level embeddings into richer inter-sentence representations, followed by a bidirectional LSTM to model long-range dependencies across the document. A lightweight scoring head assigns per-sentence importance scores and is trained end-to-end with binary cross-entropy against oracle extractive labels. At inference, a dynamic mean-plus-standard-deviation threshold with a top-3 fallback selects sentences directly from the source and chronologically reorders them into the final summary. Since every output sentence is copied from the input, the model avoids generation-induced factual drift. On PubMed, our architecture outperforms isolated CNN and LSTM baselines, while ablations show that wider convolutional receptive fields improve sentence scoring. On MIMIC-CXR and MIMIC-IV BHC, the model performs well on unstructured narratives but defaults toward positional baselines on highly templated reports. These results suggest that structural constraints can provide a reliable path toward factually grounded summarization systems that are trustworthy by design rather than by correction.

</details>


### [11] [Forty Shades of Blue: Quality-Diversity Alignment via Mode-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14896)
*Jiayi Yuan,Hangoo Kang,James Jihao Liu,Yejin Choi,Vikram Iyer,Liwei Jiang,Natasha Jaques*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MoDA 是一种基于多智能体强化学习思想的后训练 RL 算法，通过角色条件化策略与质量门控机制，在提升生成质量的同时显著增强输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 对齐训练会导致模式崩溃，即输出多样性逐渐丧失，削弱模型在科学创意、创意写作等需要开放探索和多元视角任务中的表达能力。现有方法难以在质量与多样性之间取得良好平衡。

Method: MoDA（Mode-conditioned Diversity Alignment）是一种在线后训练 RL 算法，借鉴多智能体强化学习中的协调视角，联合优化生成质量与多样性。它训练单一共享 LLM 策略，以抽象编号角色为条件，每个角色作为智能体竞争产生彼此不同的输出，从而探索高质量输出空间中的互补区域，无需手工设计人格或修改架构。同时引入提示自适应质量门控机制，校准参考质量阈值，仅对达到阈值的回复给予多样性奖励，防止损害质量的奖励黑客行为。

Result: 在覆盖七项通用能力任务和四项领域特定多样性任务（科学创意与创意写作）的基准上评估。MoDA 在 Infinite-Chat 留出提示上将 SBERT 多样性提升 265%，相比 Qwen3-8B 基线平均通用能力 pass@1 提升 10.3%。相比最强的 DivPO 基线，SBERT 多样性从 0.274 提升至 0.482（+75.9%），E-Vendi 从 2.86 提升至 4.4（+53.8%），平均通用能力 pass@1 提升 7.0%。

Conclusion: MoDA 可作为标准后训练方法的即插即用替代方案，在提升生成质量的同时保持并扩展模型的表达性输出空间。

Abstract: A notable byproduct of LLM alignment training is mode collapse: the progressive loss of output diversity that narrows a model's expressivity at inference time. This degradation is especially limiting for applications requiring open-ended exploration and pluralistic perspectives, such as scientific ideation and creative writing. We present MoDA (Mode-conditioned Diversity Alignment), an online post-training RL algorithm that jointly optimizes generation quality and diversity, inspired by the coordination perspective in multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). MoDA trains a single shared LLM policy conditioned on abstract numbered roles, where each role acts as an agent competing to produce outputs distinct from the others. This formulation encourages mode-conditioned agents to explore complementary regions of the high-quality output space without requiring hand-crafted personas or architectural modifications. MoDA employs a prompt-adaptive quality gating mechanism that calibrates a reference quality threshold and grants diversity rewards only to responses that meet the threshold, preventing reward-hacking behaviors that compromise response quality. To study quality-diversity tradeoffs, we evaluate MoDA on a comprehensive suite of benchmarks spanning seven general capability tasks and four domain-specific diversity tasks in scientific ideation and creative writing. MoDA improves SBERT diversity by 265% on the Infinite-Chat held-out prompts, while increasing average general capability pass@1 by 10.3% over the Qwen3-8B baseline. Compared with the strongest DivPO baseline, MoDA improves SBERT diversity from 0.274 to 0.482 (+75.9%) and E-Vendi from 2.86 to 4.4 (+53.8%), while improving average general capability pass@1 by 7.0%. Overall, MoDA provides a drop-in alternative to standard post-training methods that preserves and expands the model's expressive output space while improving quality.

</details>


### [12] [From Token Probabilities to Semantic Constraints: Towards Declarative Probabilistic Evaluation of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13520)
*Kyle Richardson,Cullen Anderson,Pranav Balakrishnan,Takuto Ban,Daksha Ladia,Ankita Gupta,Marisa Hudspeth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ModelLog，一个用于预训练评估的声明式概率框架，将评估目标表示为词元级预测上的符号约束，并度量模型分布对这些约束的满足程度，从而把评估与学习通过共享语义统一起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽快速进步，但如何评估其获得的知识与推理能力、以及这些评估与预训练学习信号之间的关系等基本问题仍未解决；传统词元似然或答案准确率难以刻画系统性失效。

Method: 提出ModelLog声明式概率框架：以符号约束形式显式指定评估目标，作用于词元级预测，并量化模型分布对这些约束的满足强度；设计针对否定、互斥性与一致性的新任务套件；将评估分数解释为损失，分析其梯度对逻辑强度、信息量与变量级敏感性的反映。

Result: 在否定、互斥性与一致性任务上发现系统性失效，这些失效难以仅通过词元似然或答案准确率刻画；评估分数可视为损失，其梯度体现逻辑强度、信息量和变量级敏感性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过共享语义将评估与学习联系起来，既可诊断模型行为，也有助于澄清学习的语义结构。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have improved rapidly, many fundamental questions remain about how to evaluate the knowledge and reasoning abilities they acquire, and how such evaluations relate to the learning signals used in pre-training. In this paper, we propose ModelLog, a declarative probabilistic framework for pre-training evaluation that makes the semantic structure of model behavior explicit and provides new formal tools for relating evaluation to learning. ModelLog specifies evaluation targets as symbolic constraints over token-level predictions and measures how strongly a model's distribution satisfies those constraints. We explore the framework through a new suite of tasks targeting negation, mutual exclusivity, and consistency, finding systematic failures that are difficult to characterize through token likelihood or answer accuracy alone. We further show that these evaluation scores can also be interpreted as losses, whose gradients reflect logical strength, informativeness, and variable-level sensitivity. This links evaluation and learning through a shared semantics, suggesting evaluation methods that diagnose model behavior while also helping to clarify the semantic structure of learning.

</details>


### [13] [Translating the Translator: Decomposing the Cost of English-Forced Inter-Agent Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15079)
*Kushagra Agrawal,Yuming Feng,Man-Fai Leung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM架构（如LangChain、AutoGen）默认以英语作为智能体间通信语言。本文在四种类型学差异大的语言（印地语、汉语、西班牙语、阿拉伯语）上，使用Aya-23-8B模型，比较原生语言流水线与强制英语流水线（含回译步骤），发现强制英语通信会带来显著的"英语强制税"，准确率下降13.0至30.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM框架默认以英语作为智能体间通信的通用语，即使最终用户任务为非英语。这一假设尚未被系统评估，其代价不明。

Method: 构建双智能体"抽取-回答"核心流程，并在英语强制条件下增加回译智能体。使用Aya-23-8B模型，在印地语、汉语、西班牙语、阿拉伯语四种语言上各取300个样本，对比原生语言流水线与强制英语流水线（含英译回用户语言的最终回译步骤）。

Result: 发现统计显著（通过严格Bonferroni校正）的"英语强制税"，将英语路由成本与一般多智能体编排开销分离。强制英语通信使精确匹配准确率相对原生语言多智能体执行下降13.0个百分点（西班牙语）至30.6个百分点（印地语）。以chrF分数衡量英语参考词元重叠度，发现重叠度越低与流水线失败关联越强，表明翻译损失是性能下降的重要成因。

Conclusion: 当源语言与目标语言类型学距离较远时，智能体框架采用原生语言路由具有充分理由，可减少复合的翻译税。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM architectures, such as LangChain and AutoGen, largely assume English as the lingua franca for internal inter-agent communication, even when the end-user task is non-English. We fill this gap by evaluating a two-agent extraction-answer core, with an additional back-translation agent in the English-forced condition, across four typologically diverse languages (Hindi, Chinese, Spanish, Arabic; n = 300 per language) using the Aya-23-8B model. We compare a native-language pipeline to an English-forced one (which incorporates a final back-translation step from English to the user's language). We discover a statistically significant English-Forcing Tax (surviving a strict Bonferroni correction) that isolates the cost of English routing from general multi-agent orchestration overhead. Forcing inter-agent communication through English reduces Exact Match accuracy by 13.0 percentage points (Spanish) up to 30.6 percentage points (Hindi) compared to native-language multi-agent execution. Using chrF scores as a diagnostic measure of English-reference lexical overlap, we find that lower overlap is strongly associated with pipeline failure, consistent with translation loss being an important contributor to the observed performance drop. These findings suggest a compelling case for native-language routing in agent frameworks when the source and target languages are typologically distant, reducing a compounding translation tax.

</details>


### [14] [Harmfulness Propagation Dynamics: Layer-wise Trajectories of Adversarial Intent in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13534)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Uluğ Bayazıt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现有害提示的末token隐藏状态沿模型深度的投影呈单调上升，良性提示则平坦或振荡，据此提出轻量级输入审核方法HERALD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有有害提示检测依赖单层表征快照，忽视了有害意图是随深度逐步"解析"的语义属性——表层形式早出现，语用意图晚固化，因此单层信息不足以刻画危害性。

Method: 先定义并验证"危害性传播动力学"（HPD）：用逐层学习的LDA危害方向对末token隐藏状态做投影，得到跨层投影序列；再提取斜率、曲率、单调性、起始层等七维特征，送入仅288参数的MLP分类。方法无需梯度训练，每层仅存一个d维方向（32层、d=4096时约262KB），推理仅增2.6e-6 prefill FLOPs。

Result: 八个提示有害性基准、四个模型家族上，OLMo2-7B平均F1达89.3；对抗越狱检测F1为98.4，超过所有被测guard模型（96.9）；在每个backbone上比既有潜空间方法高2.3–4.1个F1点。逐层LDA方向在随机划分下余弦相似度>0.97。

Conclusion: 危害性体现为渐进解析的语义属性，其跨层轨迹形状比单层快照更具判别力；HERALD以极低开销实现更强的有害提示与越狱检测，并提供可机读的逐实例审计轨迹，具备可解释性优势。

Abstract: We identify \textbf{Harmfulness Propagation Dynamics (HPD)}: for harmful prompts, the projection of the last-token hidden state onto a learned harm direction rises monotonically with transformer depth, whereas benign prompts remain flat or oscillatory. This cross-layer signature reflects harmful intent as a \emph{progressively resolved} semantic property: surface form appears early, while pragmatic intent consolidates later, making the \emph{trajectory shape} more informative than any single-layer snapshot. Moreover, LDA-based harm directions, learned per layer, remain stable across random splits (pairwise cosine similarity $>0.97$), supporting the projection sequence as a reproducible structured signal. Building on HPD, we introduce \textbf{\herald{}} (\textbf{H}armful \textbf{E}ncoding \textbf{R}ecognition via \textbf{A}ctivation \textbf{L}ayer \textbf{D}ynamics). This lightweight input moderator extracts a seven-dimensional feature record, slope, curvature, monotonicity, onset layer, and related statistics from the cross-layer projection sequence and classifies it with a 288-parameter MLP. \herald{} stores one $d$-dimensional direction per layer ($262$\,KB for a 32-layer, $d{=}4096$ model), requires no gradient computation during training, and adds only $2.6{\times}10^{-6}$ prefill FLOPs at inference. Across eight prompt-harmfulness benchmarks and four model families, \herald{} achieves an average F1 of $89.3$ on OLMo2-7B, surpassing all tested guard models on adversarial jailbreak detection ($98.4$ vs.\ $96.9$ F1) and outperforming prior latent-based methods by $2.3$-$4.1$ F1 points on every backbone. Per-instance trajectories provide machine-readable audit records that reveal \emph{when} and \emph{how} harmfulness emerges, offering an interpretability advantage over single-layer approaches.

</details>


### [15] [HypoEvolve: Genetic Algorithms Enable Multi-Agent LLMs to Discover Scientific Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15938)
*Jieyuan Liu,Mengzhou Hu,Jefferson Chen,JungHo Kong,Pratibha Jagannatha,Yiming Gao,Dexter Pratt,Hsin-Yuan Lee,Zhiting Hu,Trey Ideker,Wei Wang,Eric P. Xing,Zhen Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HypoEvolve，通过世代式遗传算法显式建模 LLM 科研智能体之间的协作，将科学判断与新假设提案对假设种群的迭代更新过程制度化，从而可检验协作形式对假设质量的影响；在 34 种癌症的药物重定位任务上，以 DepMap 与 Open Targets 外部证据为指标，HypoEvolve 在两项度量上均超越六个基线，验证了多智能体协作相对于单次生成的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学智能体系统虽已结合进化搜索，通过批评、比较与修订来演化假设，但"不同形式的智能体协作如何影响假设质量"仍未解决。回答该问题需将智能体的科学能力效应与其协作效应分离，而现有框架难以做到，因此需要一个既保留智能体科学角色、又支持假设组合、修订与保留规则的框架。

Method: 提出 HypoEvolve，以假设种群的连续更新使协作过程显式化。采用世代式（generational）遗传算法协调专门化的 LLM 智能体，各智能体分别承担整合机制性论证、重新审视前提假设、评估证据与可检验性等科学职能。每一代明确规定科学判断与新提案如何重塑种群，使协作对假设质量的影响可直接检验。评估设计围绕可解释"干预为何有效"的科学上可验证假设：以药物重定位为任务，将假设关联到靶点层面的生物学主张，并改编 DepMap 与 Open Targets 作为基于实验、遗传与临床证据的互补外部度量。

Result: 在 34 种癌症类型上，HypoEvolve 在 DepMap 与 Open Targets 两项度量上均取得相对于六个基线的最高分。DepMap 选择性得分达 0.171，而最强基线为 0.115。相对于单次生成（single-pass generation）的增益亦可泛化到留出（held-out）的癌症类型。

Conclusion: HypoEvolve 表明，显式建模协作机制的进化式多 LLM 智能体框架能够提升科学假设质量，并推进"自主科学"的愿景——即 AI 研究团队可获得超越单个模型的发现能力。

Abstract: Scientific agents contribute to hypothesis discovery by synthesizing evidence, assessing proposals, and developing new explanations. Recent systems combine scientific agents with evolutionary search through critique, comparison, and revision. However, how different forms of agent collaboration affect hypothesis quality remains an open question. Answering this question requires separating the effects of agents' scientific capabilities from those of their collaboration. A framework must therefore preserve agents' scientific roles and support rules for combining, revising, and retaining hypotheses. Building on this view, we introduce HypoEvolve, which makes collaboration explicit through successive updates to a hypothesis population. Specifically, we propose a generational genetic algorithm to coordinate specialized large language model (LLM) agents that integrate mechanistic arguments, reconsider assumptions, and assess evidence and testability. Each generation specifies how scientific judgments and new proposals reshape the population, making collaboration effects on hypothesis quality directly testable. Moreover, we design our evaluation around scientifically meaningful hypotheses that explain how a proposed intervention could work. Drug repurposing links these explanations to target-level biological claims assessed against external evidence. Specifically, we adapt DepMap and Open Targets into complementary external measures grounded in experimental, genetic, and clinical evidence. Across 34 cancer types, HypoEvolve achieves the highest scores against six baselines on both measures. DepMap selectivity reaches 0.171, versus 0.115 for the strongest baseline. Gains over single-pass generation also generalize to held-out cancer types. HypoEvolve advances a vision of autonomous science in which AI research teams achieve a capacity for discovery beyond that of individual models.

</details>

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown remarkable proficiency on general-purpose tasks, yet their performance often degrades in highly-specialized technical domains. Moreover, little is known about how parametric knowledge of domain-specific terms is encoded within these models. We address this gap by contributing two novel medical jargon evaluation benchmarks and evaluate a general-purpose Llama-3.1 model against a variant fine-tuned on medical-domain data. Surprisingly, the general-purpose model outperforms the medically fine-tuned model on both tasks. Using mechanistic interpretability tools, we find systematic patterns of miscalibration for the medically fine-tuned model. Instead of reorganizing parametric knowledge, the fine-tuned model places greater emphasis on a small subset of model components associated with jargon-favoring predictions. We find that applying component reweighting strategies against the benchmark tasks successfully suppresses these components and closes the gap with the general-purpose baseline. We also observe that some jargon-sensitive components transfer knowledge to the same tasks involving materials science jargon, suggesting they encode a partially domain-agnostic notion of specialized terminology. Our results provide a case study in which a medically fine-tuned checkpoint does not improve jargon comprehension over its general-purpose counterpart, highlighting that domain adaptation should not be assumed to yield better performance on specialized terminology.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Discharge summaries are lengthy medical documents that summarize a hospital in-patient visit. Automatically generating them can reduce documentation burden and return clinician time to patient care. Whereas Large Language Model (LLMs) could be used for this task, their Achilles heel is hallucinations, which can have drastic consequences for clinical documentation. We present an evidence-driven alignment framework for discharge summarization at the clinical encounter level, that treats provenance as a first-class constraint, using semantic graphs and deep learning models. Each summary sentence is selected and organized via cross-document semantic alignment and is accompanied by explicit evidence links to its source spans. We show our results on two corpora: a publicly available corpus (MIMIC-III) and clinical notes written by physicians at the University of Illinois Hospital (UIC Health). Additionally, we make source code and trained models available.

</details>


### [18] [Same Patient, Different Order: Action-Level Reliability of Clinical LLM Agents Under Repeated Runs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13582)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu,Manpreet Singh,Zhoutian Han,Wenbin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"同输入重跑"（same-input rerun）方法，通过固定所有输入、比较智能体所下医嘱而非分数，来测量临床智能体在相同输入下的行为分歧，并在MedAgentBench上以6项可靠性指标验证了这一差距的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床智能体会开具检查、申请用药并发出转诊，但现有基准（如MedAgentBench）通常每个任务只评分一次，且明确只评单次尝试，几乎不检验相同输入是否产生相同动作。这导致"同一判决对应实质不同医嘱"的分歧可能未被记录。作者旨在填补"动作级可重复性"这一评测空白。

Method: 提出"同输入重跑"：固定任务全部输入并重复回放，比较所下医嘱而非评分，并设计六项可靠性指标。实验覆盖MedAgentBench 1000次运行、其五个可写类别中的50个任务、两个参数量低于100亿且4比特量化的开放权重模型，以及两个温度设置。研究定位为证明动作级分歧存在且可被分数漏记，而非推广任何具体比率。

Result: 在8B模型、温度0.7下，全部43个医嘱组在五次相同运行中均给出不同的医嘱集合；26组在某些运行中给出该医嘱、在另一些中不给；28组记录了不同的编码值、剂量或分析物。其中22组，基准对实质不同的行为给出相同的失败判决；4B模型在0.7下全部10个分歧组亦然。不同运行还到达不同终点，其中之一被记录服务器拒绝，而智能体被告知已成功。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在临床智能体基准中引入重复运行评测、动作级稳定性报告，以及忠实反映执行结果的（execution-faithful）环境反馈，以避免实质行为分歧被单一分数掩盖。

Abstract: A clinical agent benchmark can report the same verdict on identical inputs while the agent files a materially different order on each run. Such agents order tests, request medications and place referrals, yet benchmarks typically score one run per task and rarely ask whether identical inputs produce identical actions; MedAgentBench, the benchmark we use, scores a single attempt and says so. To measure this gap we introduce "same-input rerun", which replays a task with every input held fixed and compares the orders rather than the score, with six reliability metrics, and apply it to 1000 MedAgentBench runs across 50 tasks from its five write-capable families, two open-weight models below ten billion parameters quantised to four bits, and two temperatures. The study establishes that action-level divergence exists and can pass unrecorded by the score, not that any rate generalises. Under the 8B model at temperature 0.7, all 43 ordering groups emit a different set of orders across five identical runs, 26 emit the order on some runs and not others, and 28 record a different coded value, dose or analyte. In 22 of those 43 the benchmark reports the same failing verdict for materially different behaviour, as it does for all 10 divergent groups of the 4B model at 0.7. Orders also reach different endpoints across runs, one of which the record server rejects while the agent is told it succeeded. These findings motivate repeated-run evaluation, action-level stability reporting and execution-faithful environment feedback in clinical-agent benchmarks.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WMT26通用机器翻译任务的10个语言对既无人工从零翻译的参考，也无人工对机器译文的后编辑参考，因此无法直接使用有参考的自动评测指标来构建和选择最佳译文，需要一套在无参考条件下仍能可靠挑选高质量译文的伪参考构建流程。

Method: 1）用7个模型在最多5种提示条件下翻译3,277篇官方文档，共得到26种系统-提示组合；2）用3个无参考质量估计（QE）模型为每个候选打分；3）由逐文档选择器挑选一条译文，必要时交由GPT-5.5进行后编辑；4）在分数融合中加入置信度缩放的语言识别惩罚，抑制QE引导选择的失败模式；5）由于构建时无参考可用，所有选择决策在去年WMT25的人工判断上进行校准。

Result: 无参考工作暴露了QE引导选择的一种失败模式：指标会把流畅但语言错误的输出排在正确译文之上。加入置信度缩放的语言识别惩罚后，语言错误数量降至零，且由此得到的选择器在MetricX上仍优于其所替代的排序融合基线。在给定参考上，当选择器保留了前沿模型候选时，伪参考与最强参赛系统相当；未保留时最多低17个ESA分。人工评估在构建完成后发布。

Conclusion: 无参考场景下QE引导的译文选择存在系统性风险，需要显式建模语言识别等因素对分数融合进行校正；选择决策的准确性直接决定伪参考的质量，错误选择可带来显著的ESA分数损失。作者公开了选择方法及每条参考的溯源信息（https://github.com/surrey-nlp/PseudoRef）。

Abstract: The WMT26 General MT task evaluates systems on 10 language pairs that have no human references (neither translated from scratch nor post-edited from MT output by humans). We describe how we built the pseudo-references for these pairs and six other language pairs (in which some forms of human references are available): seven models translate the 3,277 official documents under up to five prompt conditions, giving a total of 26 system-prompt combinations; then three reference-free quality estimation (QE) models score every candidate; and a per-document selector picks one translation, which GPT-5.5 post-edits where needed. Working without references exposed a failure mode of QE-guided selection: the metrics rank fluent output in the wrong language above correct translations. Adding a confidence-scaled language identification penalty to the score fusion drives the wrong-language count to zero, and the resulting selector still scores better on MetricX than the rank-fusion baseline it replaces. Since no references were available for these pairs while we were building them, we calibrate every selection decision on last year's WMT25 human judgments. The human evaluation, released after construction, shows the cost of getting selection wrong: our references stand with the strongest participating systems when the selector kept a frontier-model candidate, and fall up to 17 ESA points below them when it did not. We release the selection method and the provenance of every reference (https://github.com/surrey-nlp/PseudoRef)

</details>
