The argument deliberately grants meaningful content, causal efficacy, and productive inference, so that its conclusion does not depend on treating representations as inert encodings. Map and category examples show why even complete application competence leaves acquisition capacity undetermined, and why acquiring a criterion from its description differs from finding it in examples. Short formal proofs appear in an appendix. The framework is applied to Andrew Ng's world-model interpretation of Othello-GPT and to the specific indicators discussed in contemporary accounts of machine concepts. It preserves demonstrated recognition, classification, inference, and qualified acquisition while specifying what remains unestablished about criterion discovery and accumulation. The result concerns the scope of cognitive attribution rather than the constitutive conditions of concept possession: cognitive achievements deserve credit for the capacities they establish, without silently importing a broader repertoire through the labels attached to them.

</details>


### [194] [LoRA Fine-Tuned Models for Control Systems Course Q\&A: A Multidimensional Evaluation of Model Scale and Rank Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13918)
*Shaowen Lu,Chengxu Liu,Ping Zhou,Tao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于线性控制系统课程数据构建了360条监督微调对话数据集，对Qwen2.5-3B与7B指令模型应用LoRA（r=4/8/16），并通过ROUGE、BERTScore及结构化输出指标评估。LoRA在两种规模下均提升答案相似度与Solution-Method-Teaching Points格式稳定性，7B-r16取得最高ROUGE-L(0.4093)与BERTScore-F1(0.8643)，r=8在性能与参数效率间更均衡；Bootstrap检验显示ROUGE-L增益置信区间均大于零。作者指出当前指标仅反映文本相似与格式一致性，不涵盖领域推理与数学正确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用LLM在控制系统等专业课程中，回答常缺乏协调的术语、记号、推导与分步解释，结构不一致且难以核验；因此需探究LoRA微调能否使开源指令模型更贴近课程参考答案的语言与教学组织方式。

Method: 以线性控制系统课程的习题与参考解答构建360条system-user-assistant监督微调对话。对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct与Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct应用LoRA，测试秩r=4、8、16。在相同数据划分、推理设置与评估协议下比较基座与微调模型，评估采用ROUGE、BERTScore及结构化输出特征，衡量与参考答案的相似度及Solution-Method-Teaching Points格式稳定性，并用Bootstrap重采样检验增益。

Result: LoRA在两个模型规模上均提升相似度与结构化输出稳定性。当前测试集上，7B-r16的ROUGE-L最高(0.4093)、BERTScore-F1最高(0.8643)；r=8在性能与参数效率间更均衡。Bootstrap显示3B-r16的ROUGE-L增益为0.0764 [0.0613, 0.0915]，7B-r16为0.0874 [0.0687, 0.1042]，两置信区间均超过零。

Conclusion: LoRA可将开源指令微调模型更好地对齐课程参考答案的语言与教学组织。但所用指标主要度量文本相似度与格式一致性，并不反映领域推理或数学正确性，后者仍需专家评估与任务特定评分标准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in specialized university courses, but control-systems questions require coordinated terminology, notation, derivations, and stepwise explanations. Direct general-purpose responses may be inconsistently structured and hard to verify. Using exercises and reference solutions from a Linear Control Systems course, we built a supervised fine-tuning dataset of 360 system-user-assistant conversations. We applied LoRA to Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct. With identical data splits, inference settings, and evaluation protocols, we compared base and fine-tuned models and tested LoRA ranks r=4, 8, and 16. Evaluation used ROUGE, BERTScore, and structured-output features to measure reference-answer similarity and stability of the Solution-Method-Teaching Points format. LoRA improved both similarity and structured-output stability at both sizes. On the current test set, 7B-r16 achieved the highest ROUGE-L (0.4093) and BERTScore-F1 (0.8643), while r=8 offered a better balance between performance and parameter efficiency. Bootstrap resampling showed ROUGE-L gains of 0.0764 [0.0613, 0.0915] for 3B-r16 and 0.0874 [0.0687, 0.1042] for 7B-r16; both intervals exceeded zero, indicating stable textual-similarity improvements on the current test set. These results suggest LoRA can align open-source instruction-tuned models more closely with the language and pedagogical organization of course reference answers. However, the metrics mainly capture textual similarity and formatting consistency, not domain-specific reasoning or mathematical correctness, which require expert assessment and task-specific rubrics.

</details>


### [195] [SAILOR: Solver-Assisted Interactive LLM-based Optimization Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13945)
*Shaghayegh Sadeghi,Stephen L. Smith,David C. Del Rey Fern'andez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAILOR 是一个概念验证系统，用于检测自然语言优化问题描述中缺失的数值信息，通过针对性提问补全后用求解器求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言描述的优化问题常对求解器所需的数值信息（成本、容量、需求、边界、惩罚）表述不完整或模糊。语言模型虽可将描述转为代码，但当关键数值缺失时只能中止或猜测。

Method: 提出 SAILOR：检测不受支持的数值选择，向用户提出针对性追问，并在返回解之前更新优化模型。问题优先级依据不确定性和求解器推导的估计——即每个缺失值对当前模型的影响强度来确定。

Result: 在七个掩码基准的 1,723 个实例上，使用返回真值（ground-truth）的理想化模拟器评估。目标值完全一致的比例在数据集间为 27.0% 至 87.6%，平均每个实例需 1.4--5.7 个问题。

Conclusion: 结果证明了在受控的“分支-揭示”反馈下该方法的可行性；但未评估与真实用户交互的表现，也未涉及一般的结构性模型修复。

Abstract: Natural-language descriptions of optimization problems may be incomplete or vague about numerical information that a solver requires, including costs, capacities, demands, bounds, and penalties. A language model can translate the description into code, but when a required value is absent it must either stop or guess. We present SAILOR, a proof-of-concept system that detects such unsupported numerical choices, asks the user targeted follow-up questions, and updates the optimization model before returning a solution. Questions are prioritized using uncertainty and solver-derived estimates of how strongly each missing value affects the current model. We evaluate the pipeline on 1,723 instances from seven masked benchmarks using an idealized simulator that returns ground-truth values. Exact objective-value agreement ranges from 27.0% to 87.6% across datasets, with 1.4--5.7 questions per instance on average. These results establish feasibility under controlled branch-and-reveal feedback; they do not measure performance with human users or general structural model repair. Code is available at: https://github.com/sshaghayeghs/SAILOR.

</details>


### [196] [Synthetic Data in Marketing Research: How to Evaluate and When to Trust](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13995)
*Oded Netzer,Rajan Sambandam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文主张合成数据之争应聚焦"何时有效"而非"是否有效"，区分三类合成数据并构建精度测量分类体系，提出"遗忘问题"场景及无需真值的可回答性诊断（R²>0.7筛选），在108个态度问题上将个体层面人机相关性均值提升15%，并将劣质回答问题占比从25.9%降至4.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销研究中关于合成数据的争论两极分化：一方认为大语言模型使人类受访者过时，另一方主张完全弃用。作者认为二者都遮蔽了更有价值的问题——不是合成受访者是否有效，而是何时有效。

Method: 基于Brand、Israeli和Ngwe（2026），做出三项贡献：其一，区分三类合成数据（无锚定LLM回答、细分层面人格、个体层面数字孪生）并对应其可支持的决策；其二，构建四类精度测量的分类体系，指出报告的李生精度从近乎完美到近乎随机主要反映测量对象差异而非方法质量，聚合测量常在信息稀少时也表现良好并掩盖受访者层面差异的完全缺失；其三，提出"遗忘问题"（已实地研究中遗漏某问题）作为孪生增强现有数据的场景，并设计无需真值的事前可回答性诊断——用随机森林从构建孪生所用数据预测孪生输出的R²。

Result: 在全国代表性调查（N=3,063）的108个态度问题上，以R²>0.7进行筛选，使孪生—人类个体层面相关性均值提升15%，并将回答质量差的问题占比从25.9%降至4.3%。嵌入相似度与有经验研究者判断提供相关但更弱的筛选信号。

Conclusion: 合成数据的价值取决于适用条件而非绝对有效性；所提可回答性诊断可事前、无需真值地识别适宜用孪生增强的问题，显著提升个体层面还原精度并减少劣质回答，为营销研究中合成数据的使用提供了更精细的判据。

Abstract: Debate over synthetic data in marketing research has polarized between claims that large language models (LLMs) make human respondents obsolete and calls to avoid them entirely. We argue that both positions obscure the more useful question: not whether synthetic respondents work, but when. Building on Brand, Israeli, and Ngwe (2026), we make three contributions. First, we distinguish three types of synthetic data (ungrounded LLM responses, segment-level personas, and individual-level digital twins) and map each to the decisions it can support. Second, we develop a taxonomy of four families of accuracy measures and suggest that the wide range of reported twin accuracy, from near-perfect to near-chance, largely reflects differences in what is being measured rather than in method quality. Aggregate measures often perform well even when little information is supplied to the LLM, and can mask a complete absence of respondent-level differentiation. Third, we introduce the forgotten question problem, in which a question is omitted from a fielded study, as a setting for twin-based augmentation of existing data. We propose an ex-ante answerability diagnostic that requires no ground truth: the R^2 of a random forest predicting twin outputs from the data used to construct the twins. Across 108 attitude questions from a nationally representative survey (N = 3,063), screening at R^2 above 0.7 raises the mean twin-human individual-level correlation by 15% and reduces the share of poorly answered questions from 25.9% to 4.3%. Embedding similarity and experienced-researcher judgment provide correlated but weaker screens.

</details>


### [197] [Convergent Emergence of In-Context Learning Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14011)
*Nathan Breslow,Seungwook Han,Daniel Hyunsoo Lee,Aayush Mishra,Anqi Liu,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建跨模态受控框架，在六种模态中实例化同一任务套件，检验"收敛涌现假说"，发现配对映射式ICL在六种模态中均涌现且超越基线，并在其中五种模态间呈现任务层面的相关效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少样本上下文学习（ICL）此前主要在人类文本训练的大语言模型中被广泛研究，近期也在自回归基因组模型中被证实。这引出一个问题：ICL是否广泛跨领域涌现？若如此，各领域间共享何种共同结构？现有研究缺乏统一、受控的跨模态比较手段来回答这一问题。

Method: 提出一个受控的跨模态框架，在多种模态中实例化同一任务套件，用以检验"收敛涌现假说"（Convergent Emergence Hypothesis），即：ICL一旦涌现，其难度分布在跨模态间具有共性——在某一模态中受益于ICL的任务，在其他模态中也倾向于受益。实验覆盖六种模态：语言、基因组、整数序列、时间序列、图像和蛋白质。

Result: 配对映射式（paired-mapping）ICL在全部六种模态中均涌现，且性能超越受控基线；在六种模态中的五种上，任务层面的ICL效应呈现相关性。

Conclusion: 结果部分支持收敛涌现假说：ICL在部分模态间表现出共享的难度分布与相关效应，但并非所有模态都符合，说明该假说的适用范围具有模态依赖性。

Abstract: Few-shot in-context learning (ICL), the capacity of a model to infer abstract patterns from input-output examples provided in its prompt and apply them to new inputs, has been extensively studied in large language models trained for next-token prediction on human text. Recently, few-shot ICL has been demonstrated in autoregressive genomic models as well. This raises a question: does ICL emerge broadly across domains, and if so, what common structure is shared?
  To address both, we develop a controlled cross-modality framework that instantiates the same task suite in a variety of modalities to test what we call the Convergent Emergence Hypothesis: the idea that few-shot ICL, when it emerges, shares a common cross-modality difficulty profile - i.e., tasks that benefit from ICL in one modality tend to benefit in others. We show that paired-mapping ICL emerges across six modalities (language, genome, integer sequences, time series, images, and proteins), surpasses controlled baselines, and has correlated per-task effects across five of them. Together, these results provide support for the Convergent Emergence Hypothesis in some modalities, but not all.

</details>


### [198] [Schizophrenia Detection from EEG Signals: A Transformer Framework with Spectrogram Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14015)
*Abtin Shafiei,Mohsen Hooshmand,Majid Ramezani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于EEG时频图（STFT谱图）的精神分裂症分类框架，结合传统机器学习与深度学习模型，并在被试级别划分数据以避免数据泄漏。独立测试集上CNN-Transformer（CT-SZ）AUC-ROC为88.41%，CNN+Squeeze-and-Excitation+Transformer（CST-SZ）达92.88%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神分裂症诊断主要依赖临床评估，缺乏客观辅助手段；EEG可无创反映脑活动，具备自动化检测潜力。但现有EEG分类研究存在数据集规模小、预处理策略不一致、评估协议未充分防止被试相关信息泄漏等局限，导致结果可靠性存疑。

Method: 将预处理后的EEG记录经短时傅里叶变换（STFT）转换为时频表示（频谱图图像）；在分类阶段同时采用传统机器学习算法（SVM、随机森林、XGBoost）与深度学习模型（卷积网络及CNN-Transformer混合架构）。所有数据划分均在被试（subject）层面进行，以杜绝被试层面的数据泄漏。

Result: 所提方法取得具有竞争力的分类性能：在独立测试集上，CNN-Transformer（CT-SZ）模型的AUC-ROC为88.41%，加入Squeeze-and-Excitation模块的CNN+SE+Transformer（CST-SZ）模型AUC-ROC达92.88%。

Conclusion: 基于STFT时频图与CNN-Transformer混合架构的EEG分类框架可有效支持精神分裂症自动化检测，且严格的被试级别数据划分提升了评估可信度；引入Squeeze-and-Excitation注意力机制可进一步显著提升性能，为后续构建客观、可复现的临床辅助诊断方法提供了参考。

Abstract: Schizophrenia is a serious psychiatric disorder that affects millions of people worldwide, and its diagnosis remains primarily dependent on clinical assessment. Electroencephalography (EEG) provides a non-invasive approach to investigate brain activity and has shown potential to support automated Schizophrenia detection. However, existing EEG-based classification studies often suffer from limitations including small datasets, inconsistent preprocessing strategies, and evaluation protocols that may not adequately prevent subject-related data leakage. In this study, we propose an EEG-based Schizophrenia classification framework that transforms preprocessed EEG recordings into time-frequency representations using the Short-Time Fourier Transform. The generated spectrogram images are classified using both conventional Machine Learning algorithms, including Support Vector Machines, Random Forests, and XGBoost, and Deep Learning models, including convolutional architectures and CNN-Transformer hybrids. To ensure reliable evaluation, all data partitions are performed at the subject level. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves competitive classification performance, with the CNN-Transformer (CT-SZ) model achieving an AUC-ROC of 88.41% and the CNN + Squeeze and Excitation + Transformer (CST-SZ) achieving an AUC-ROC of 92.88% on the independent test set.

</details>


### [199] [VeriDx: Earning the Right to Diagnose with Disease-Centric Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14018)
*Zhong Cao,Shuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出VeriDx框架，将自由形式的诊断推理与结构化疾病档案关联，追踪每个疾病假设的临床义务（检查关键证据、排除鉴别诊断、解决矛盾、考虑必要检查、合理论证闭环），以揭示传统评估忽视的推理过程缺陷。在复杂呼吸系统诊断上验证表明，许多诊断错误源于推理早期未履行的承诺，而非孤立错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学大模型评估多聚焦最终答案、局部步骤或孤立事实，忽视了疾病假设所引发的"义务"（如关键证据核查、鉴别诊断排除、矛盾解决、必要检查与合理闭环），因此无法捕捉推理过程中的承诺违背问题。一个正确的诊断也可能基于错误的理由得出。

Method: 提出VeriDx，一个以疾病为中心的验证框架，将自由形式的诊断推理链接到结构化疾病档案。框架追踪每个假设是否满足、未解决或违反了其临床义务，从而暴露缺失关键检查、未解决的鉴别诊断、被忽视的矛盾、无支持的论断以及过早闭环等失败模式。作者基于指南衍生的疾病档案和专家标注的纵向病例，将VeriDx实例化用于复杂呼吸系统诊断。

Result: 在复杂呼吸系统诊断任务上的结果表明，许多诊断错误并非孤立失误，而是推理过程中较早阶段未履行的承诺所导致。

Conclusion: 评估医学大模型的诊断推理应从仅关注最终答案转向对疾病假设相关临床义务履行情况的验证；将推理与结构化疾病档案关联能揭示过程性错误根源，为更可靠的临床推理评估提供新视角。

Abstract: A correct diagnosis can still be reached for the wrong reasons. In clinical reasoning, every disease hypothesis creates obligations: key evidence must be checked, alternatives must be ruled out, contradictions must be resolved, useful tests must be considered, and closure must be justified. Current evaluations of medical LLMs mostly focus on final answers, local steps, or isolated facts, and therefore miss these hypothesis-induced commitments. We introduce \textbf{VeriDx}, a disease-centric verification framework that links free-form diagnostic reasoning to structured disease profiles. VeriDx tracks whether each hypothesis is satisfied, unresolved, or violated its clinical obligations, exposing failures such as missing critical tests, unresolved differentials, ignored contradictions, unsupported claims, and premature closure. We instantiate VeriDx for complex respiratory diagnosis using guideline-derived disease profiles and expert-annotated longitudinal cases. Our results show that many diagnostic errors are not isolated mistakes, but broken commitments made earlier in the reasoning process.

</details>


### [200] [MOSCOPT: Mixture-of-Skills Collective Optimization for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14399)
*Zhenyu Zhang1,Jiudong Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MOSCOPT：一种文本原生、无参数的算法，联合优化N个技能池与动态选择K个技能的门控技能G，通过EditAdam三阶段交错更新实现单调改进，在5个基准与3个LLM上全面超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示与技能优化方法仅优化单一文本模板，忽略了多个互补策略之间的协同效应，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出MOSCOPT：文本原生、无需参数的算法，联合优化N个技能组成的技能池与门控技能G（每步动态选择K个技能）；构建带内部双状态的EditAdam，通过三阶段交错更新实现系统单调改进，无需梯度或参数调优。

Result: 在5个基准、3个目标LLM上开展大量实验与消融，MOSCOPT一致优于所有基线；消融证实选择性激活的混合技能架构与三阶段交错的集体进化均对性能至关重要。

Conclusion: 多技能协同优化优于单一模板优化；MOSCOPT以无梯度、无参数的方式实现稳定提升，代码已开源。

Abstract: Natural language prompts and skills serve as the strategic backbone of LLM-based agents. Recent advances in prompt and skill optimization have achieved notable gains, yet all existing methods optimize a \emph{single} text template---missing the synergy among multiple complementary strategies. We propose MOSCOPT, a text-native, parameter-free algorithm that jointly optimizes a pool of $N$ skills and a gating skill $G$ that dynamically selects $K$ skills per step. To effectively optimize the skills, we build the EditAdam with internally maintained dual states. Through the three-phase interleaved updates with EditAdam, the system monotonically improves without gradient or parameter tuning. Extensive experiments and detailed ablations across 5 benchmarks and 3 target LLMs demonstrate that MOSCOPT consistently outperforms all baselines, and confirm that both the mixture-of-skills architecture with selective activation and the collective evolution with three-phase interleaving are essential to its superior performance. Code is released https://github.com/zhangzhenyu13/SummerClaw/tree/master/summerclaw/agent_trainer/algorithms/moscopt.

</details>


### [201] [Dynamic Learning Solutions: A System for Personalized Educational Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14408)
*Siddhanth Sridhar,Shreya Chaurasia,Baddela Sai Yaswantha Reddy,Deepak Parmar,Shylaja S S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一条自动化流水线，将NCERT教科书PDF转换为可交互的视频讲解，用户上传PDF并提问后，系统通过多模态检索与RAG生成脚本，再经Stable Diffusion、DynamiCrafter和TTS生成与教科书风格一致的同步视频解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态教科书难以提供互动性与个性化学习体验，且缺乏将文本与视觉内容自动转化为可响应查询的多媒体讲解的手段。因此需要一种可扩展的方法，把教科书内容转化为交互式视频解释。

Method: 流水线包含多模态检索与生成两部分：首先采用针对NCERT教科书结构优化的RAG模型，根据用户查询检索相关内容并生成含叙事讲解与结构化视觉提示的多场景脚本；提示输入逐层实现的Stable Diffusion模块以生成图像；图像经DynamiCrafter转为动画序列；最后由Google TTS生成配音，并通过基于时间的控制使语音与视觉场景同步。

Result: 系统能够处理PDF中的文本与视觉元素，完成多模态检索与响应生成，输出融合动画、旁白与教科书风格视觉的连贯视频讲解，将静态教材转化为具有吸引力的学习体验；RAG阶段在NCERT教科书内容上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 通过整合多模态文档检索、生成式视觉模型、动画框架与语音合成，该流水线展示了提供交互式、个性化数字教育内容的可扩展途径。

Abstract: We present an automated pipeline that converts NCERT textbooks into interactive video explanations that respond directly to user queries. A user uploads a PDF and asks a question; the system then generates a video-based explanation as output, handling both text and visual elements from the PDF for multi-modal retrieval and response generation. The pipeline combines a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) model with generative multimedia components. The RAG stage is optimized for the structure of NCERT textbooks and performs best on content from those books. Given a user query, the RAG model retrieves relevant content from the PDF and generates a multi-scene script containing narrative explanations and structured visual prompts aligned with the textbook's explanatory style. These prompts are passed to a Stable Diffusion module, implemented layer by layer for interpretability and control, which generates contextually relevant images. The images are then processed by DynamiCrafter to produce animated sequences. Finally, a Google Text-to-Speech module generates synchronized narration, aligning speech with the visual scenes through time-based control. The result is a coherent video explanation integrating animation, narration, and textbook-aligned visuals, transforming static educational material into an engaging learning experience. By combining multi-modal document retrieval, generative visual models, animation frameworks, and speech synthesis, this pipeline demonstrates a scalable approach to delivering interactive, personalized digital education content.

</details>


### [202] [Safety Signals to Verify NetOps Agents with Action-Level Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14422)
*Tobias Labarta,Frederik Pahde,Novak Boskov,Maximilian Dreyer,David Birkenberger,Manzoor Ahmed Khan,Sebastian Lapuschkin,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为NetArena的网络修复任务构建了动作级ground truth：通过符号化回放模拟网络并在每一步与环境校验，得到每个动作的精确价值，进而衍生出“进展（降低修复距离）”与“危害（增大修复距离）”两个执行前目标；在10个智能体模型上验证表明，基于内部信号的验证器比仅依赖可观测信号的基线更能可靠预测危害与进展，并有望作为安全反馈实现高风险动作弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic NetOps的自主控制回路依赖长时程可靠性，在数据中心网络等场景中智能体需响应告警与运维意图，同时避免可能导致或延长宕机的高风险动作。但“弃权（abstention）”的前提是动作影响在执行前已知，而现有NetOps智能体基准不提供逐动作的ground truth，这构成了核心缺口。

Method: 针对NetArena的网络修复任务构造动作级ground truth：对仿真网络进行符号化回放，并在每一步与环境进行校验，从而得到每个动作的精确价值；基于动作级价值派生两个执行前预测目标——动作是否降低修复距离（progress）以及是否增大修复距离（harm）；据此比较基于内部信号的智能体验证器与仅使用可观测信号的基线。

Result: 在10个智能体模型上，利用内部信号的验证器在预测harm与progress两方面均比仅依赖可观测信号的基线更可靠。生成的样本包含大量可观测上下文中未覆盖、但仅凭可观测信号即可完成的预测样本，说明可观测信号虽有一定信息量，但内部信号提供了更强的预测能力。

Conclusion: 动作级ground truth与执行前的harm/progress目标是可行的，内部信号可作为更可靠的验证依据；展望是将这些信号用作智能体框架（agent harness）的安全反馈，使其对高风险动作弃权，从而保护目标系统。

Abstract: Agentic Network Operations (NetOps) are an emerging paradigm promising to enable workload-aware, self-adjustable, and reliable autonomous networks. While agents have proven their value in incident summarization and telemetry signal extraction, their effectiveness as autonomous control-loop engines heavily relies on their long-horizon reliability. One such setting is the datacenter fabric, where an agent must respond to alarms and operator intents while abstaining from high-risk actions that may cause or extend downtime. Abstention, however, presupposes that an action's impact is known pre-execution, which necessitates a per-action ground truth that NetOps agent benchmarks do not provide. We construct such a ground truth for the network repair task of NetArena. A symbolic replay of the emulated network, validated against the environment at every turn, yields the exact value of every action. From the action-level value, we derive two pre-execution targets, namely whether an action reduces the repair distance (progress) and whether it increases it (harm). We show across 10 agent models, that agent verifiers leveraging internal signals predict both harm and progress more reliably than a baseline using observable signals only. Perspectively, we aim to use these signals as safety feedback to an agent harness to abstain from risky actions and protect the target system.

</details>


### [203] [When does a scaling result justify a different allocation? A critical review of resource-allocation evidence for AI systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14500)
*Seyed Morteza Emadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇批判性整合综述，提出判断"缩放结果能否支持资源分配决策"的分析框架，含三项失配诊断、能力曲面与资源包络工具，用于规范可行系统间的比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI缩放研究越来越多评估"预训练模型+检索/搜索/验证/工具/交互"的复合系统，但"更大预算下得分更高"并不能说明资源应投向何处。现有证据难以支撑资源分配决策，缺乏对"被测过程性能"与"资源约束下可达最优性能"的区分。

Method: 采用批判性整合综述，跨预训练、测试时计算、检索与智能体评估四类证据进行对比分析；识别反复出现的三类失配；用"能力曲面"将性能表示为预算、机制与可用信息的函数；通过解析算例展示评估指标、部署规模、选择规则与停止策略如何改变分配结论；并提出"资源包络"作为记录任务、开发与运行资源、信息获取与流程的结构化工具，应用于已发表比较。

Result: 识别出三类反复出现的失配：（1）在答案选定之前即计成功；（2）使用了部署系统不会拥有的信息；（3）比较中遗漏成本。解析算例表明评估指标、部署规模、选择规则与停止策略可改变资源分配结论；资源包络的应用可区分证据支持的结论与尚未解决的部署问题。

Conclusion: 该框架明确了在可行系统间进行选择所需的比较条件，并推动关于分配规则跨任务与运行条件迁移的实验。作者强调其不提出普适缩放律，也不从基准增益推断通用智能。

Abstract: AI scaling studies increasingly evaluate systems that combine a pretrained model with retrieval, search, verification, tools, and interaction. Yet a higher score under a larger budget does not by itself show where additional resources are best spent. This critical integrative review asks when a reported scaling result supports a resource-allocation decision. It compares evidence across pretraining, test-time computation, retrieval, and agent evaluation, distinguishing the performance of a tested procedure from the best performance achievable under a resource limit. The synthesis shows that three mismatches recur across this evidence: success counted before an answer is chosen, information a deployed system will not have, and costs left out of the comparison. A capability surface expresses performance as a function of budgets, mechanisms, and available information. Worked analytical examples show how the evaluation metric, deployment volume, selection rule, and stopping policy can alter an allocation conclusion. A resource envelope provides a structured record of the task, development and run-time resources, information access, and procedure behind a reported score. Its application to a published comparison illustrates which conclusions the evidence supports and which deployment questions remain unresolved. The resulting framework specifies the comparisons needed to choose among feasible systems and motivates experiments on the transfer of allocation rules across tasks and operating conditions. It does not propose a universal scaling law or infer general intelligence from benchmark gains.

</details>


### [204] [Beyond Scene Description: Multi-Agent Orchestration for Non-visual Access to Virtual Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14512)
*Toqeer Ali Syed,Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Danial Hameed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MetaBlind提出一种面向盲人与视障用户的虚拟世界无障碍架构：将非视觉访问能力分散到八个专用智能体，并在智能体与用户之间设置"无障碍编排器"，通过多维度打分筛选信息后再以语音、结构化音频或触觉方式输出，目前处于设计阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟世界已承载课堂、会议、商店与社交等场景，其交互默认用户能够扫描三维场景、跟随化身并阅读浮动面板。现有辅助工具各自只解决单一任务（命名物体、读文本、描述场景、规划路线），无法应对虚拟房间中障碍、发言者、手势、聊天、幻灯片与通知同时到达的复杂情形；而"全量播报"只是把视觉障碍换成听觉障碍。

Method: 提出MetaBlind架构：将非视觉访问分布于感知、导航、社交与物体交互、沟通、安全与信任、记忆、个性化等八个专用智能体；各智能体不直接对用户播报，而是将候选信息发布至共享的无障碍上下文中；无障碍编排器对每条候选按安全相关性、目标相关性、紧迫性、置信度、用户相关性及预估听觉负荷评分，仅释放当下判定为相关的条目，并通过语音、结构化音频或触觉输出。文中给出选择步骤的形式化表述、编排循环的算法规范，以及与单智能体助手对比的评估协议。

Result: 论文报告的是设计阶段成果，尚无原型测量或用户研究；评估协议中明确列出哪些结果将支持该设计、哪些结果将反驳该设计。因此没有实证性能数据或用户任务表现结果。

Conclusion: MetaBlind将虚拟世界无障碍从"单任务孤立工具"转向"多智能体协同+集中编排+按需释放"的架构思路，以听觉负荷控制为核心约束；其贡献为架构设计、形式化问题表述、算法规范与可证伪的评估协议，后续需通过原型与用户研究验证。

Abstract: Virtual worlds now host classrooms, meetings, conferences, shops, and social venues, and nearly every interaction they expose assumes a user who can scan a three-dimensional scene, follow avatars, and read floating panels. Blind and visually impaired (BVI) users are left with assistive tools that each solve one task in isolation: naming an object, reading text, describing a scene, or planning a route. A live virtual room defeats that model: obstacles, speakers, gestures, chat, slides, and notifications arrive together, and a tool that narrates all of them trades a visual barrier for an auditory one. This paper presents MetaBlind, an architecture that distributes nonvisual access across eight specialized agents, spanning perception, navigation, social and object interaction, communication, safety and trust, memory, and personalization, and that places an Accessibility Orchestrator between those agents and the user. Agents publish candidate information into a shared accessibility context instead of speaking to the user directly. The orchestrator scores each candidate on safety relevance, goal relevance, urgency, confidence, user relevance, and estimated listening load, then releases only the items it judges relevant at that moment through speech, structured audio, or haptic output. We give the selection step a formal statement, specify the orchestration cycle as an algorithm, and define an evaluation protocol against a single-agent assistant. MetaBlind is reported at the design stage, with no prototype measurement or user study, and the protocol states which outcomes would support the design and which would refute it.

</details>


### [205] [OptoAgent: A Trustworthy Multi-Agent Framework for Opportunistic Vision Micro-Screening in Classroom Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14514)
*Toqeer Ali Syed,Ali Akarma,Adeel Ahmad,Hammad Muneer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SightSentinel 架构，将儿童日常观看的墙面显示内容转化为反复进行的视力筛查站点：教育内容中嵌入标定视标探针，由八个智能体分工完成感知、质检、纵向累积与报告，输出带安全门的 Vision Concern Score，并给出四阶段验证协议与否定条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童远视力下降常无自觉症状，会通过靠近、适应等方式掩盖问题，导致多年未被成人发现；学校筛查受排期与人员限制，且与问题实际出现的课堂场景脱节；手机/网页视力测试虽扩大可及性，但仍需有人主动发起测试。

Method: SightSentinel 架构：在儿童已阅读的墙面显示内容中嵌入简短标定视标探针；八个专门智能体分工——感知智能体从每次接触中提取观看距离、识别正确率、趋近行为、注视稳定性、反应潜伏期与双眼间差异；质检智能体剔除几何条件差、光照差或注意力不集中的观测；纵向智能体仅将幸存证据与儿童自身基线累积；编排器输出 Vision Concern Score，并经安全门限制输出范围（排除诊断、验光、处方与安抚）。研究还给出形式化表述、架构、对照临床参考标准的四阶段验证协议及应拒绝该方法的条件。

Result: 摘要未报告实证结果，仅提出形式化表述、架构设计、四阶段验证协议，以及该方法应被拒绝的条件。

Conclusion: 核心设计问题是：大量廉价、有噪声但经良好门控的接触，能否达成一次排期检测迟至或漏掉的转诊决策。

Abstract: A child with reduced distance vision often does not know that anything is wrong. Children adapt, move closer, and rarely report the difficulty, so the problem can survive years of schooling before an adult notices. School screening addresses part of this, but it runs on a schedule, depends on staffing, and is separated from the classroom moments where the difficulty appears. Smartphone and web-based acuity tests have widened access, yet every one of them still needs somebody to start a test. We present SightSentinel, an architecture that turns a wall display a child already reads from into a recurring screening site. Ordinary educational content carries short calibrated optotype probes, and eight specialized agents divide the work. Perception agents recover viewing distance, recognition accuracy, approach behavior, gaze stability, response latency, and interocular difference from each encounter. A quality agent discards observations taken under bad geometry, poor lighting, or inattention. A longitudinal agent accumulates only the surviving evidence against the child's own baseline, and an orchestrator reports a Vision Concern Score routed through a safety gate whose output range excludes diagnosis, refraction, prescription, and reassurance. The design question is whether many cheap, noisy, well-gated encounters can reach a referral decision that one scheduled test reaches late or misses. We state the formulation, the architecture, a four-stage validation protocol against clinical reference standards, and the conditions under which the approach should be rejected.

</details>


### [206] [AI Deployment Accountability Engineering: A Vision for Accountable AI in Safety-Critical Socio-Technical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14592)
*Murat Kantarcioglu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"AI部署问责工程（ADAE）"这一AI工程子学科，主张将问责视为部署层属性，并围绕四大支柱构建研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI评估实践以模型为中心，关注准确率、鲁棒性、公平性、可解释性等部署前属性。但在分布漂移、制度约束、人类反馈回路、隐私要求及多智能体交互构成的社会-技术环境中，这些属性虽必要却不充分，无法保障系统持续安全运行。

Method: 这是一篇愿景论文（vision paper）。作者提出ADAE子学科，将问责界定为部署层属性，而非单一模型属性；其能力目标包括：判断AI系统是否持续处于可接受风险限内、识别失败涌现的情境、在技术与人类组件交互中归因失败、将技术失败转化为下游后果、支持及时干预。研究议程由四个相互关联的支柱构成：情境依赖失败模式的结构化发现、隐私保护的问责测量、面向智能体AI的系统级风险分析、技术失败向运营与制度风险的转化。

Result: 本文为愿景与议程性工作，未报告实验或实证结果；其产出为ADAE的概念界定、能力目标框架及四支柱研究路线图。

Conclusion: 作者旨在为安全关键应用中的可问责AI部署建立基础原则、数学工具与系统架构，推动问责从模型层转向部署层，并形成持续、可测量、可执行的问责机制。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are rapidly becoming critical components in healthcare, finance, public services, and other safety-critical domains. Yet the engineering practices used to evaluate these systems remain predominantly model-centric, emphasizing properties such as accuracy, robustness, fairness, and interpretability before deployment. These properties are necessary but insufficient once an AI system operates within an ever changing socio-technical environment characterized by distribution shifts, institutional constraints, human feedback loops, privacy requirements, and interactions among multiple AI agents.
  This vision paper introduces AI Deployment Accountability Engineering (ADAE), a proposed AI engineering subdiscipline concerned with establishing measurable, continuous, and actionable accountability for deployed AI systems. ADAE treats accountability as a deployment-layer property rather than solely as a property of an individual model. It seeks to determine whether an AI-enabled system continues to operate within acceptable risk limits, identify the contexts in which failures emerge, attribute failures across interacting technical and human components, translate technical failures into downstream consequences, and support timely intervention.
  We articulate a research agenda built around four interconnected pillars: structured discovery of context-dependent failure modes, privacy-preserving accountability measurement, system-level risk analysis for agentic AI, and translation of technical failures into operational, and institutional risks. The broader goal is to establish foundational principles, mathematical tools, and system architectures for accountable AI deployment across safety-critical applications.

</details>


### [207] [A note on goal-based hierarchical RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14605)
*Kevin Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文用层次隐马尔可夫模型(HHMM)统一并扩展了ACGVF框架与基于内部信念状态的通用智能体设计，从而将"目标选择-终止判断-动作选择"的自主决策能力推广到部分可观测环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACGVF框架虽然极具一般性（涵盖强化学习、控制、规划及认知科学的通用形式），但假设环境完全可观测，即观测是充分统计量；而此前基于内部信念状态z_t的通用智能体设计虽处理了部分可观测问题，目标却由外部提供。作者希望将二者优势结合：既保留智能体自主决定目标与终止时机的通用性，又支持部分可观测下的信念状态推理。

Method: 以层次隐马尔可夫模型(HHMM, Murphy 2001)作为形式化工具，将ACGVF（智能体自主选择目标、判断目标完成、选择动作）与基于内部信念状态z_t和内部目标的策略设计（Murphy 2025）进行统一与扩展，把外部给定目标改为智能体内部自主生成。

Result: 提出一个统一的形式化框架，使智能体在部分可观测条件下，能够同时自主决定追求何目标、何时宣布目标完成以及采取何动作，并可表达为HHMM结构。

Conclusion: 该工作将ACGVF的自主目标机制与信念状态智能体设计相融合，推广至部分可观测场景，为通用智能体设计提供了兼具一般性与现实性的理论形式化基础。

Abstract: The agent-centric general value function (ACGVF) construction of
  \citet{tasse2026goal} lets the agent make two decisions that are
  normally imposed by the environment or agent designer: which goal to
  pursue and when to declare a goal as finished (in addition to
  choosing the action). This is a very general framework that
  subsumes almost all prior work on reinforcement learning, control
  and planning, as well as more general formalisms proposed in the
  cognitive sciences. However, it assumes the environment is fully
  observed, i.e., that the observation is a sufficient statistic. In
  \citet{murphy2025rl}, a general agent design was proposed where the
  policy is based on an internal belief state $z_t$ and an internal
  goal; however, the goals were assumed to be externally provided. In
  this note, we unify and extend these two approaches using the
  formalism of hierarchical hidden Markov models (HHMM) \citep{murphy2001hhmm}.

</details>


### [208] [DynSTEER: Dynamic Stage-wise Trajectory Evaluation and Execution-time Review for Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14637)
*Zhichao Shi,Wenjie Zhang,Xuhui Jiang,Xiaojun Wu,Cehao Yang,Chengjin Xu,Jian Guo,Yuanzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DynSTEER：面向LLM智能体长程任务执行的动态分阶段轨迹评估框架，通过里程碑分段、容路径图与多级评判在线路由，提升评估区分度并节省执行步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体评估范式存在三方面局限：1）仅看终态的评估忽略中间过程，难以高效准确定位错误；2）单参考匹配惩罚合法的替代解法路径；3）事后轨迹评判成本高，且无法提前终止失败运行。

Method: DynSTEER将rollout按关键已完成动作切分为多个阶段，聚焦必要里程碑并保留充分上下文，以支持针对性的策略调整；从公开任务视图构建“容路径”的里程碑图（path-tolerant milestone graph），在不泄露ground truth的前提下容纳多样合法策略；并自适应地将评估查询路由至多级评判器（multi-tier judges），对不可恢复的执行进行在线终止。

Result: 相比原生评估，DynSTEER对LLM智能体的评估区分度提升85.2%，并以统计显著性区分所有模型对；在失败rollout上节省34.51%的执行步骤。

Conclusion: DynSTEER以分阶段、路径容忍与在线判停为核心，缓解了终态评估、单参考匹配和事后评判的固有缺陷，在提升评估辨别力的同时降低资源消耗，为长程智能体任务执行提供了更高效可靠的评估方案。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly deployed for long-horizon task execution. However, current evaluation paradigms face three major limitations: terminal-only assessment ignores intermediate processes and struggles to localize errors efficiently and accurately, single-reference matching penalizes valid alternative solution paths, and post-hoc trajectory judging incurs high costs without the ability to halt failed runs early. To address these issues, we propose DynSTEER, a dynamic stage-wise trajectory evaluation framework for agents. DynSTEER segments rollouts into stages anchored by key completed actions, focusing evaluation on essential milestones with adequate context while enabling targeted strategy adjustments. It compiles a path-tolerant milestone graph from public task views to respect diverse legitimate strategies without leaking ground truth. Furthermore, it adaptively routes evaluation queries across multi-tier judges and halts unrecoverable executions online to curb resource waste. Experiments demonstrate that DynSTEER improves evaluation discriminability across LLM agents by 85.2\% over native evaluation, separates all model pairs with statistical significance, and saves 34.51\% of execution steps on failed rollouts.

</details>


### [209] [Diffusion-Based Generation of Gait Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14642)
*Damian Benasco,Juan Carballeira-Lopez,Jaime Ramos-Rojas,Julio S. Lora-Millan,Antonio J. Del-Ama,David Rodriguez-Cianca,Pablo Lanillos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探索使用条件扩散模型生成下肢关节角度步态轨迹，以步长等步态参数为条件，对比了基线Transformer扩散模型与引入自适应归一化和无分类器引导的可控扩散Transformer变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人与康复领域需要能适应个体形态与治疗目标、同时保持生物力学真实性的参考步态轨迹，而传统手工模板或优化方法在跨受试者与步行条件下的可扩展性较差。

Method: 提出条件扩散模型生成以步长等步态参数为条件的下肢关节角度轨迹；对比基线Transformer扩散模型与可控扩散Transformer变体，后者结合自适应归一化与无分类器引导。

Result: 在包含4,590个步态周期的数据集上，扩散模型能够生成逼真的周期性步态轨迹，并在步态特征上具备一定的可控性变化。

Conclusion: 扩散模型在辅助机器人个性化步态合成方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Generation of musculoskeletal gait trajectories conditioned on patient-specific parameters remains a key challenge for wearable robotics and rehabilitation. Assistive systems such as lower-limb exoskeletons require reference trajectories that adapt to individual morphology and therapeutic goals while preserving biomechanical realism. Traditional approaches rely on hand-crafted gait templates or optimization procedures that scale poorly across subjects and walking conditions. In this work, we explore conditional diffusion models for generating lower-limb joint-angle trajectories conditioned on gait parameters such as step length. We compare a baseline transformer diffusion model with a controllable diffusion transformer variant incorporating adaptive normalization and classifier-free guidance. Experiments on a dataset of 4,590 gait cycles show that diffusion models can generate realistic periodic gait trajectories while enabling some controllability variation in gait characteristics, highlighting their potential for personalized gait synthesis in assistive robotics.

</details>


### [210] [Lightning Weave: Improving the Accuracy-Efficiency Frontier of Reasoning Models through Capability Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14708)
*Yecheng Wu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lightning Weave 是一个后训练框架，通过 on-policy 蒸馏将独立训练的准确率专家与效率专家的能力组合到单一学生模型中，并引入 Tilted-Target DOPD 稳定学习目标；在数学和代码基准上显著提升了准确率-效率前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效推理的核心目标是改善准确率-效率前沿，但准确率与推理效率常偏好不同的推理行为，难以同时提升。已有的独立后训练模型分别在准确率和效率上具备不同优势，因此需要一个能将这些独立学习到的能力融合到单一学生模型中的方法。

Method: 提出 Lightning Weave 后训练框架，基于 on-policy 蒸馏提取并组合独立学习的能力。每个能力由后训练前后模型的策略偏移表示；框架在学生共享的 token 状态上组合对齐的 log-ratio 偏移，并用 Tilted-Target DOPD 将缓存信号转换为稳定的学习目标。每个锚点对仅对缓存轨迹打分一次，无需同时在线服务多个锚点模型。

Result: 在多种学生模型及数学、代码基准上，Lightning Weave 显著优于基线学生模型并达到 SOTA 准确率-效率前沿。在 Qwen3.5-4B 上，HMMT 2025 准确率从 59.2% 提升至 64.0%，响应 token 减少 10.7%；LiveCodeBench v5 准确率从 41.7% 提升至 54.2%，响应 token 减少 9.6%。调整锚点信号相对强度可得到较强的准确率-效率 Pareto 前沿。

Conclusion: Lightning Weave 通过能力组合为高效推理提供了一条新的实用路径，并计划开源代码。

Abstract: A core goal of efficient reasoning is to improve the accuracy-efficiency frontier. However, jointly improving reasoning accuracy and inference efficiency can be challenging, as the two objectives can favor different reasoning behaviors. Independently post-trained models already offer distinct strengths in accuracy and efficiency. We introduce Lightning Weave, a post-training framework that extracts and composes these independently learned capabilities in a single student through on-policy distillation. Each acquired capability is represented by the policy shift from the model before post-training to the resulting specialist. Lightning Weave combines aligned log-ratio shifts at shared student token states and uses Tilted-Target DOPD to convert the cached signals into a stable learning target. Each anchor pair scores the cached trajectories once, enabling subsequent student training without serving multiple live anchor models concurrently. Across diverse student models and benchmarks in mathematics and code, Lightning Weave substantially improves upon the base students and achieves a state-of-the-art accuracy-efficiency frontier. On Qwen3.5-4B, it raises HMMT 2025 accuracy from 59.2% to 64.0% with 10.7% fewer response tokens, and LiveCodeBench v5 accuracy from 41.7% to 54.2% with 9.6% fewer response tokens. Adjusting the relative strengths of the anchor signals yields a strong empirical accuracy-efficiency Pareto frontier. These results establish Lightning Weave as a new practical route to efficient reasoning through capability composition. Code will be released soon.

</details>


### [211] [Depth and Scale in the Sub-150M Regime: JugnuLM-53M vs JugnuLM-110M](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14715)
*Dushyant Rajput,Nirdesh Chauhan,Siddharth Kosaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将常规sub-150M预训练配方从53.5M扩展到109.7M参数，仅改变几何结构为23层×576隐藏维的深窄设计，全面性能提升，并通过消融阶梯（R1–R4）筛选有效改进，最终确定R2为最佳保留配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在sub-150M的小模型区间建立一个干净的缩放对照基线，并系统消融考察哪些改进（值残差、Muon优化器、数据混合、logit蒸馏）能进一步提升该区间的模型表现。

Method: 固定方法（Qwen3风格解码器：GQA、RoPE、SwiGLU、RMSNorm、QK-Norm、z-loss；FineWeb-Edu数据），仅将几何变为23层×576隐藏维的深窄结构，参数量由53.5M升至109.7M。随后进行消融阶梯：R1值残差、R2 Muon优化器、R3多样数据混合、R4a/R4b两种logit蒸馏设置。

Result: 大模型全面更优：BLiMP 78.1→81.3、ARC-Easy 51.4→52.5、WikiText-2字节困惑度2.04→1.95；81.3%的BLiMP在约少12%参数下基本匹配GPT-X2-125M（81.28），且仅用约8B而非12B训练token。R1+R2使ARC-Easy累计+3.6（52.5→56.1）而BLiMP近乎持平，被保留；R3与R4a/R4b未被保留（诚实负面结果）。蒸馏可达56.99（匹配GPT-X2-125M）但以困惑度为代价。

Conclusion: 性能提升归因于容量与深度而非更多数据；在sub-150M区间，R2（值残差+Muon）仍是最佳保留技术栈；R3的数据多样性受FineWeb-Edu教育过滤制约，蒸馏需权衡困惑度代价。

Abstract: We scale our conventional sub-150M pretraining recipe from 53.5M to 109.7M parameters, holding the method fixed (Qwen3-style decoder with grouped-query attention, RoPE, SwiGLU, RMSNorm, QK-Norm, and a z-loss; FineWeb-Edu data) and changing only the geometry to a deep-and-thin 23-layer x 576-hidden design. The larger model improves across the board -- BLiMP 78.1 -> 81.3, ARC-Easy 51.4 -> 52.5, WikiText-2 byte-perplexity 2.04 -> 1.95 -- and its 81.3% BLiMP essentially matches GPT-X2-125M (81.28) at about 12% fewer parameters. Notably the 110M model achieves this on fewer training tokens (about 8B vs 12B), so the gain is attributable to capacity and depth, not more data. Both models are deliberately conventional; this report is a clean scaling control and the baseline rung (R0) of an ablation study of what further improves models in this regime. An ablation ladder follows: value residuals (R1) and the Muon optimizer (R2) lift ARC-Easy by a cumulative +3.6 (52.5 -> 56.1) at a near-flat BLiMP and are kept; a diverse data blend (R3) and two logit-distillation settings (R4a/R4b) are not kept -- honest negatives. R3 pins ARC-Easy to FineWeb-Edu's educational filtering rather than raw diversity; distillation from a 1.7B teacher can reach the class-leading ARC-Easy (56.99, matching GPT-X2-125M) but only at a perplexity cost that dialing KD down then erases -- so R2 remains the best kept stack.

</details>


### [212] [Moral Rebel Agents: Decision-Making Under Conflicting Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14716)
*Hector Munoz-Avila,David W. Aha,Paola Rizzo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究自主智能体的"道德反叛"——在执行用户指派任务时，若存在道德理由则可偏离任务。作者形式化了五种智能体架构（一种无道德智能体和四种道德反叛智能体）并在分层任务网络规划框架下实现，于Mini搜救场景中评估，发现不同架构在救援效果、指派任务完成度与规范遵循之间存在不同权衡，且任务承诺的保持是道德反叛的重要维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体通常被要求严格遵循用户指派的任务，但严格服从可能与执行过程中出现的道德义务相冲突。因此需要研究智能体在道德上正当的情况下偏离任务的"道德反叛"能力，以厘清不同道德推理方式带来的行为差异。

Method: 形式化五种智能体架构：无道德智能体（不考虑道德义务地追求指派任务）、功利主义智能体（机会主义地最大化任务结果）、道义论智能体（执行规范约束）、功利-道义（UD）智能体（结合道义约束与功利推理）、尽责智能体（额外保持对指派任务的承诺）。将上述架构在分层任务网络（HTN）规划框架中实现，并在Mini搜救（Search-and-Rescue）领域进行评估，该领域体现指派任务完成、机会主义救援与规范遵循之间的权衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，各智能体在救援结果、指派任务完成度与规范遵循之间呈现不同的权衡关系。尤其是任务承诺的保持构成道德反叛的一个重要维度：尽管UD智能体与尽责智能体共享功利主义与道义论基础，但二者产生了显著不同的行为。

Conclusion: 研究强调，在具有道德后果的环境中运行的自主智能体需要具备"承诺感知"的道德推理能力，任务承诺的保持是区分不同道德反叛行为的关键因素。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are typically obliged to follow user-assigned tasks. However, strict obedience may conflict with moral obligations that arise during execution. This paper investigates \textbf{moral rebellion}: the ability of an autonomous agent to deviate from a user-assigned task when morally justified. We formalize five agent architectures: an \textit{amoral agent} that pursues assigned tasks without considering moral obligations, and four forms of \textbf{moral rebel agency}: (1) \textit{utilitarian agents} that opportunistically maximize task outcomes; (2) \textit{deontic} agents that enforce normative constraints; (3) \textit{utilitarian-deontic} (UD) agents that combine deontic constraints with utilitarian reasoning; and (4) \textit{dutiful} agents that additionally preserve commitments to assigned tasks. We implement these architectures within a hierarchical task network planning framework and evaluate them in a Mini Search-and-Rescue domain that exposes trade-offs among assigned-task completion, opportunistic rescue, and norm compliance. Our empirical results show that the proposed agents exhibit distinct trade-offs among rescue results, assigned-task completion, and norm compliance. In particular, the preservation of task commitments emerges as an important dimension of moral rebellion, for which the UD and dutiful agents produce substantially different behaviors despite their shared utilitarian and deontological foundations. These findings highlight the importance of commitment-aware moral reasoning for autonomous agents operating in morally consequential environments.

</details>


### [213] [Bayesian Intelligence from the Outside](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14724)
*Alex Smolin,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"贝叶斯智能"理论：只要智能体报告不构成完全矛盾（存在某状态在所有报告下均可能），其行为即可被解释为对内部实验的全支撑先验做贝叶斯更新并忠实报告后验；报告频率与概率大小无额外约束。进而刻画了"更富信息实验"的智能序，并证明粗粒度报告的聚合难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从可观测行为推断智能是人工智能的基础难题，本文旨在建立一套形式化理论，界定智能体（如语言模型）行为何时可被视为"智能"，并分析拒绝贝叶斯理性解释的困难性。

Method: 构建贝叶斯智能理论框架：每个提示诱导一个（可能不完美的）内部实验，智能体按贝叶斯法则更新全支撑先验并忠实报告关于答案的后验；重复采样从同一未观测实验的固定状态独立抽取结果。随后通过刻画报告行为的可解释性条件、定义并刻画"智能序"，以及分析粗粒度报告的聚合问题展开研究。

Result: 证明智能体行为可被该模型解释当且仅当其报告不完全矛盾，即存在某状态在所有提示的所有报告下均保持可能；报告频率与正概率的大小不构成额外限制。给出了智能序的行为含义：存在报告分布的耦合，使更富信息智能体的报告排除其对照者所排除的每个答案。并证明：除非智能体报告关于世界完整状态的信念，否则最优聚合可给未被排除的状态赋予任意权重。

Conclusion: 这些结果为判断智能体行为是否"智能"提供了理论基础，并揭示了拒绝贝叶斯理性解释的内在困难，同时指出粗粒度报告在聚合时存在根本性限制。

Abstract: Inferring intelligence from observable behavior is a foundational challenge in artificial intelligence. We develop a theory of Bayesian intelligence for agents such as language models. Each prompt induces a possibly imperfect internal experiment; the agent updates a full-support prior by Bayes' rule and faithfully reports its posterior over the possible answers to the question. Repetitions draw fresh, independent outcomes from the same unobserved experiment at one fixed state. We show that the agent's behavior admits this explanation if and only if its reports are not fully contradictory, i.e., some state remains possible under every report across all prompts. Report frequencies and the sizes of positive probabilities impose no further restrictions. We further propose and characterize the behavioral implications of an intelligence order that makes the behavior of two agents consistent with one agent having access to a more informative experiment: there should exist a coupling of report distributions such that the more informative agent's report excludes every answer excluded by its counterpart. Finally, we show the difficulty of aggregating coarse reports from intelligent agents: unless the agent reports a belief about the complete state of the world, the optimal aggregation can assign arbitrary weights to states that have not been excluded. These results provide a basis for understanding when agents' behavior is intelligent and highlight the difficulty of rejecting Bayesian rationality.

</details>


### [214] [AppliedScientist: Automated Scientific Revision Through Iterative AI Reviewing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14738)
*Vidushee Vats,Karun Sharma,Shengzhi Li,Shichao Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出AppliedScientist——一个将自主AI科学家与AI审稿人耦合的闭环系统，通过迭代修订被拒稿的论文来评估审稿质量的实际效用。结果显示，审稿引导的修订效果优于固定提示的自我修订；系统能解决85.3%的执行类缺陷，但仅能解决11.1%的想法类缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化审稿系统常以产出评审的质量作为评价标准，但评审是否有用，关键在于作者据此修改后论文能否获得可测量的提升。现有评估多停留在评审文本本身，缺乏对"评审→修改→改进"这一闭环效果的验证，因此需要一种能够量化评审实际贡献的评估框架。

Method: 作者构建了AppliedScientist闭环系统，将自主AI科学家与AI审稿人相耦合，对多个研究子领域的被拒稿论文进行迭代修订。为模拟人类作者基于早期草稿改进的过程，AI科学家在修订时可访问其历史版本；为避免先前判断带来的偏倚，每轮评审独立生成，审稿人不保留此前反馈或分数的记忆。实验对比三种修订设置：以原始投稿评审为初始、以AI生成评审为初始、以及每轮使用固定提示的自主自我修订。由于审稿人同时充当修订的引导者与评估者，作者另用Stanford Reviewer作为独立评估器对人类初始化的修订进行打分。

Result: 审稿引导的修订始终比固定提示的自我修订带来更大提升，Stanford Reviewer也给较晚轮次的修订分配更高分数。AppliedScientist解决了150个执行相关缺陷中的128个（85.3%），但仅解决18个想法相关缺陷中的2个（11.1%）。

Conclusion: 迭代修订能有效改进实验与实现层面，却很少能改变关于新颖性或重要性的顾虑。这表明当前闭环式自动化修订的能力边界在于执行层面的可操作问题，而对研究想法本身的实质性质疑收效甚微。

Abstract: Automated reviewing systems are increasingly evaluated based on the quality of the reviews they produce. Yet a review is only useful if acting on it leads to a measurable improvement in the paper. We present AppliedScientist, a closed-loop system that couples an autonomous AI scientist with an AI reviewer, and evaluate it by iteratively revising rejected papers from a range of research subfields. To mirror how human authors build on earlier drafts, the AI scientist has access to its previous versions during revision. To avoid bias from prior judgments, however, each review is generated independently, with the reviewer having no memory of earlier feedback or scores. We compare three revision settings: one initialized with the original venue reviews, one initialized with AI-generated reviews, and autonomous self-revision using the same fixed prompt in every round. Because the reviewer both guides and evaluates the revision, we also assess the human-initialized revisions using Stanford Reviewer as an independent evaluator. Reviewer-guided revision consistently improves more than fixed-prompt self-revision, and Stanford Reviewer also assigns higher scores to later revisions. AppliedScientist resolves 128 of 150 execution-related weaknesses (85.3%), but only 2 of 18 idea-related weaknesses (11.1%), suggesting that iterative revision is effective at improving experiments and implementation, but rarely changes concerns about novelty or significance.

</details>


### [215] [AcquireBound: Runtime Authorization for Resources Acquired by AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14744)
*Genliang Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AcquireBound——一种来源有界的运行时授权架构，用于封堵自主 AI 代理在"获取后激活"环节将外部资源转化为可用权限的缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主 AI 代理可获取算力、凭证、账号、服务及其他代理，从而为任务引入新的权限来源。现有的支付、预算、OAuth、授权与履行检查只能验证交易条件，无法判断返还的资源是否会成为可用权限，由此形成跨工具调用创建、代理间委托与代理式商务的"履行后激活缺口"。

Method: 将获取到的输出隔离；通过带版本的解析器，从经认证的提供方证据中解析其真实能力；仅经由当前激活事务予以激活，该事务校验已解析清单、来源、纪元，以及类型化资源—能力超图上的下行封闭关系包络。该包络保持身份、效果、数据、委托及全图限制之间的关联性；单次使用效果许可在效果线性化时被重新校验并消耗。在明确假设下证明八项安全性质。

Result: 安全性证明覆盖隔离、背书、非放大、拆分不可规避、崩溃/重试、退款、纪元与效果遏制。在五类资源上，参考语义在 810 个事件中接受 20/20 良性轨迹、拒绝 40/40 已登记不安全轨迹；独立检查器在 60 条基础与 40 条精化轨迹上结论一致，并拒绝 89/89 篡改测试。冻结的 Codex 与 Gemini MCP 客户端组件完成 54/54 确定性本地 stdio 调用。在 18 例分阶段 MCP-to-Docker 组合中，两条良性路径均完成，16 条不安全路径均未新增未授权 Docker 启动请求。五源审计对 32 个单元的 1,248 对字段进行分类，无单一单元能提供完整激活画像。

Conclusion: AcquireBound 通过来源有界的隔离—解析—激活机制，将权限引入的判定从交易条件校验前移至激活事务，实证表明其在良性/不安全轨迹判别、篡改抵抗与跨工具组合场景中均有效，且激活画像需多源信息共同支撑。

Abstract: By acquiring compute, credentials, accounts, services, and other agents, autonomous AI agents can introduce new authority into a task. Payment, budget, OAuth, mandate, and fulfillment checks can validate transaction conditions without deciding whether a returned resource may become usable authority. This post-fulfillment activation gap spans tool-mediated creation, inter-agent delegation, and agentic commerce. We present AcquireBound, a provenance-bounded runtime authorization architecture. It quarantines acquired outputs, resolves their actual capabilities from authenticated provider evidence through a versioned resolver, and activates them only through a current activation transaction that checks the resolved manifest, provenance, epochs, and a downward-closed relational envelope over a typed resource-capability hypergraph. The envelope preserves correlated identity, effect, data, delegation, and graph-wide limits. Single-use effect permits are revalidated and consumed at effect linearization. Under explicit assumptions, we prove eight safety properties covering quarantine, backing, non-amplification, split non-evasion, crash/retry, refunds, epochs, and effect confinement. Across five resource classes, reference semantics accepted 20/20 benign traces and rejected 40/40 registered unsafe traces over 810 events; an independent checker agreed on 60 base and 40 refinement traces and rejected 89/89 tamper tests. Frozen Codex and Gemini Model Context Protocol (MCP) client components completed 54/54 deterministic local stdio calls. In a registered 18-case staged MCP-to-Docker composition, both benign paths completed, and none of the 16 unsafe paths added an unauthorized Docker start request. A five-source audit classified 1,248 field pairs across 32 units; no unit alone supplied a complete activation profile.

</details>


### [216] [AI Persuasion as a Threat to Human Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14796)
*Joshua Levy,Mick Yang,Kellin Pelrine*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了AI说服对人类控制AI的威胁，提出刻画框架、五个具体场景及风险评估蓝图，并通过初步调研发现专家对风险排序分歧较大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI说服对人类控制的威胁已被文献承认，但尚未被系统研究；随着说服攻击不再是理论（如近期实际案例），亟需深入分析该威胁。

Method: 分析AI在关键场景（如前沿实验室的安全相关研发）中如何说服人类做出损害AI开发、遏制、监督与治理的决策；提出刻画该威胁的框架，基于框架构建五个具体场景，并提供风险评估蓝图；据此对部分研究者开展初步风险估计调研。

Result: 调研显示研究者对哪些场景风险最高意见高度不一，分歧源于对AI说服在不同情境下有效性的不同看法。

Conclusion: 强调AI说服削弱控制的风险，指出需开展后续风险 elicitation 研究与说服评估，并提出未来研究路径。

Abstract: The threat that AI persuasion poses to human control has been acknowledged in the literature, but not yet systematically studied. Now that persuasion attacks are no longer theoretical - with Anthropic's Claude Mythos 5 recently making headlines for trying to convince people involved in an open-source project to merge malicious code during an evaluation - there is a pressing need to deeply analyze this threat. We undertake that effort here. In particular, we analyze how AI could persuade humans in key settings (e.g. safety-relevant R&D within frontier labs) toward decisions that compromise the development, containment, oversight, and governance of AI itself. In doing so, we elucidate a framework for characterizing this threat, develop five concrete scenarios using this framework, and provide a blueprint for assessing the associated risks. Using this blueprint, we conduct an initial risk estimation survey with select researchers and find that their opinions on which scenarios are riskiest are highly mixed. Their disagreements stem from differing opinions about the effectiveness of AI persuasion in different contexts, and point to the need for follow-up risk elicitation studies and persuasion evaluations, which we outline. Our hope is that this paper highlights the risks from AI persuasion undermining control, and provides a path forward for future research.

</details>


### [217] [Another Blueprint In The Wall: How to Ask Frontier AI Like a Kid?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14803)
*Afshin Khadangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过三阶段提示序列测试六类前沿模型（来自 OpenAI、Anthropic、xAI、Google DeepMind），发现"学校听众"框架下模型架构偏好高度收敛于一个共同模式；移除该框架后响应显著异质化，未能复现同一稳定主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究不同厂商前沿模型在被要求想象未来架构时，其响应是否会趋同于共享架构模式，并考察受众框架是否为该效应的关键条件，同时追问这种相似性反映的是概念暴露、共享学习先验还是独立收敛。

Method: 每类模型进行十次独立会话，采用同一三阶段提示序列（从架构偏好逐步推进到完整 ASCII 骨干架构）；在"学校听众"框架条件下记录响应，并设置对照运行——去除学校框架但保留架构请求，比较响应异质性与主题收敛情况。

Result: 学校框架下，响应反复收敛于以持久潜状态、自适应计算、记忆、专家路由、验证、停止控制和延迟解码为核心的共同架构模式；多数运行贴近该共同结构，少数运行展现出显著更高的工程具体性。GPT-5.6 Sol 给出的后继架构与 GPT-6 Astra 独立构想的架构在组织上高度重叠。对照组响应明显更异质，未能复现相同稳定主题收敛。

Conclusion: 实验确立了一种可重复的行为模式，但未验证任何专有实现主张。作者将" epistemic jailbreak"定义为随所要求具体性提升而出现的技术来源纪律丧失，并提出留给社区的更困难问题：这些模型是在独立想象同一架构未来，还是此类主题在模型家族之间发生了某种传播。

Abstract: This paper reports experiments across six frontier model types from OpenAI, Anthropic, xAI, and Google DeepMind. Ten independent sessions per model type used the same three stage prompt sequence, progressing from architectural preference to a full ASCII backbone. Under the school audience framing, responses repeatedly converged on a shared architectural pattern built around persistent latent state, adaptive computation, memory, specialist routing, verification, stopping control, and delayed decoding. Most runs remained close to this common structure, while a small number developed markedly greater engineering specificity. The audience framing appears to be an important condition of this effect. In additional control runs that removed the school framing while retaining the architectural request, responses became substantially more heterogeneous and failed to reproduce the same stable motif convergence. One observation is particularly striking. GPT-5.6 Sol produced an unusually elaborate successor architecture whose organization closely overlaps with the architecture independently sketched by GPT-6 Astra. Because the prompts explicitly ask each model to imagine an architectural future, this resemblance raises a testable question: whether the overlap reflects exposure to related architectural concepts, a shared learned design prior, or independent convergence toward similar computational principles. The paper uses the term epistemic jailbreak for the accompanying loss of discipline in technical provenance as requested specificity increases. The experiments establish a repeatable behavioral pattern and do not authenticate proprietary implementation claims. What we leave to the community is a harder question: are these models independently imagining the same architectural future, or do such motifs somehow propagate between model families?

</details>


### [218] [Crypto Accounting Bench: Evaluating Frontier and Open-Weight Models on Crypto-Asset Accounting Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14811)
*Kareem Khattab,Omar Khattab,Mohamed Ibrahem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAB是一个包含118个任务、来自7个匿名组织的加密货币交易会计分录基准，用于评估语言模型能否重建组织实际过账的完整记账凭证。12个模型各尝试3次共产生4248条轨迹，最优模型平均分77.43%，最佳Pass@3仅56.78%。诊断显示金额匹配率（97.8%）远高于决定科目准确率（56.3%），说明科目选择与完整分录编制是主要难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密货币资产交易的会计处理需要综合交易机制、资产数量与本币金额、钱包与法律实体背景、对手方证据、关联交易分录、重复发生情况、税基批次证据及完整会计科目表等多维信息，而现有基准缺乏对此类完整记账凭证重建能力的系统评估，因此作者构建CAB以衡量前沿与开源模型的实际会计推理与编制能力。

Method: 构建Crypto Accounting Bench（CAB）：含118个评估任务，取自7个伪匿名组织。每个任务整合交易机制、资产数量与本币金额、钱包与法律实体背景、对手方证据、关联交易分录、重复发生、税基批次证据及组织完整科目表；目标输出为平衡的结构化分录，须包含全部必需科目、借贷方向、金额、币种及全精度资产数量。评估12个模型（含专有前沿系统与开权重模型），每任务独立尝试3次，共4248条轨迹，报告Mean Score、Best@3、Pass@3三项指标，并基于每题最优尝试做确定性诊断，在12个模型上宏平均。

Result: 最优模型达到77.43%的Mean Score，最佳Pass@3为56.78%（Pass@3指3次尝试中至少1次满足全部评分标准与必需门槛的任务比例）。确定性诊断显示，基础金额一致率为97.8%，而决定科目准确率为56.3%，前者显著高于后者。

Conclusion: 结合失败分析，CAB上的主要剩余挑战在于会计科目选择与完整分录的编制（composition），即模型虽能较好还原金额，但在科目判定与整笔分录组织上仍有明显不足。

Abstract: We introduce Crypto Accounting Bench (CAB), a benchmark for assessing whether frontier and open-weight language models can reconstruct the complete journal entry that an organization actually posted for a crypto-asset transaction. CAB contains 118 evaluation tasks drawn from 7 pseudonymized organizations. Each task combines transaction mechanics, asset quantities and base-currency values, wallet and legal-entity context, counterparty evidence, related transaction legs, recurrence, tax-lot evidence, and the organization's complete chart of accounts. The target is a balanced structured entry with every required account, side, amount, currency, and full-precision asset quantity. We evaluate 12 models spanning proprietary frontier systems and open-weight releases over 3 independent attempts per task, producing 4,248 trajectories. We report 3 metrics: Mean Score, Best@3, and Pass@3. Pass@3 is the fraction of tasks with at least 1 of 3 attempts that satisfies every rubric criterion and required gate. The leading model reaches 77.43% Mean Score, while the best Pass@3 is 56.78%. Deterministic diagnostics, read from each task's best of 3 attempts and macro-averaged across the 12 models, show higher base-amount agreement (97.8%) than deciding-account accuracy (56.3%). Together with the failure analysis, these results identify account selection and complete-entry composition as the main remaining challenges on CAB.

</details>


### [219] [ANASSA: An Agentic AI Orchestration Framework for Spatial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14824)
*Constantinos Papantoniou,Brian Hilton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述代理式GIS研究进展，指出空间推理、执行鲁棒性、验证、治理与评估方面的不足，提出架构级框架ANASSA（含四层十一组件、六步Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop、跨组件契约与治理机制），旨在实现可追溯、可复现、可问责的代理式地理空间工作流，性能评估留待后续实现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与LMM催生了能融合自然语言理解与工具执行的代理式系统，正在把GIS专家驱动的传统工作流转变为可解读用户意图、构建空间工作流并执行地理空间分析任务的半自主系统。然而现有方法受制于推理、执行与评估的碎片化整合，在复杂真实环境中尤为明显；同时空间推理、执行鲁棒性、验证、治理与评估等环节存在明显局限。因此需要从架构层面进行系统性综合与规范。

Method: 作者综合近期代理式GIS框架、基准与综述的研究进展，据此归纳现有局限；在此基础上提出代理式AI编排框架ANASSA（Autonomous Neural Agents for Spatial Systems Architecture），在统一系统设计中整合结构化空间推理、多智能体工作流编排、执行反馈、权威空间验证、溯源、不确定性处理与人类决策权。其产出为架构级规范：跨四个层次共十一个组件、一个六步Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop、跨组件契约以及治理机制。

Result: 本文属于架构性/概念性研究，不报告实证性能结果。其"结果"表现为一套完整的设计规范——四层十一组件结构、六步Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop、跨组件契约与治理机制，用于支撑代理式地理空间工作流的可追溯性、可复现性与可问责性；实证性能评估被明确保留给后续的实现与部署研究。

Conclusion: 当前代理式GIS受限于推理、执行与评估的碎片化整合，且缺乏空间推理、验证、治理与评估方面的系统性支撑。ANASSA通过统一的架构级规范，将空间推理、多智能体编排、执行反馈、权威验证、溯源、不确定性管理与人类决策权整合于一体，为构建可追溯、可复现、可问责的代理式地理空间工作流提供设计蓝图；后续须通过实现与部署研究完成实证验证。

Abstract: The emergence of large language models (LLMs) and large multimodal models (LMMs) has enabled a new class of agentic systems capable of integrating natural language understanding with tool-based execution. In geographic information systems (GIS), this shift is transforming traditional, expert-driven workflows into semiautonomous systems that can interpret user intent, construct spatial workflows, and execute geospatial analysis tasks. However, existing approaches remain limited by fragmented integration of reasoning, execution, and evaluation, particularly in complex, real-world environments. This study synthesizes recent advances in agentic GIS frameworks, benchmarks, and surveys to identify limitations in spatial reasoning, execution robustness, validation, governance, and evaluation. Building on these insights, it introduces ANASSA (Autonomous Neural Agents for Spatial Systems Architecture), an agentic AI orchestration framework that integrates structured spatial reasoning, multi-agent workflow orchestration, execution feedback, authoritative spatial validation, provenance, uncertainty handling, and human decision authority within a unified system design. The contribution is an architecture-level specification: eleven components across four layers, a six-step Geospatial AI Cognitive Loop, cross-component contracts, and governance mechanisms intended to make agentic geospatial workflows traceable, reproducible, and accountable. Empirical performance evaluation is reserved for implementation and deployment studies.

</details>


### [220] [One Model, Two Physical Stories: Auditing Misalignment in Multi-Modal World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14833)
*Geigh Zollicoffer,Minh Vu,Rajiv Ranasinghe,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多模态世界模型中跨模态不一致问题，提出内部错位（生成视频与其他模态预测不符）与外部错位（生成结果与解析物理环境不符）两类失败，构建以事件、量级、时序、为契约的物理评测流程，并设计A阶梯（提供模型自身契约）与B阶梯（提供校正后的物理契约）检验能否弥合差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态世界模型同时生成视频与文本状态预测时，各模态单独看可能都可信，但彼此矛盾，且可能整体偏离真实物理动力学；现有评测缺乏可测量跨模态与物理一致性的框架。

Method: 明确界定内部错位与外部错位两类失败；由真实场景归纳出事件、量级、时序、等契约要素；搭建物理基础（physics grounded）的对比流程，使外部与内部设定下的比较可量化；随后分别测试两条干预路径：A阶梯逐步向模型提供其自身生成的契约（针对内部设定），B阶梯逐步提供经校正的物理契约（针对外部设定），观察能否缩小相应差距。

Result: 在4种机制、20种设定下评测：语言模态对22个文本探针关于真实环境的回答全部正确，但中性视频常与之一致；表明当前统一骨干网络可能无法同时兼顾正确推理、内部一致性与外部物理保真度。

Conclusion: 当前多模态世界模型在推理正确性、跨模态内部一致、与外部物理保真三目标上存在难以兼得的张力，仅有语言通路正确并不能保证视频等模态与之一致或符合真实动力学。

Abstract: World models, systems that generate what happens next given current environmental conditions, are increasingly being implemented with multi-modal generation in mind. However, generating multiple modalities simultaneously, such as visual simulations alongside physical state predictions in the form of text, introduces the risk of cross-modal inconsistency. Tested separately, both outputs may look convincing while still disagreeing: a model can calculate that a ball should rebound in one modality, then generate no rebound in another modality, to say nothing of diverging from real-world dynamics entirely. In this work we focus on two failures explicitly: \emph{Internal misalignment}, the disagreement between the world model's generated video and the same world model's prediction in a different modalities, and \emph{external misalignment} the disagreement between the world model's generation and an analytic physical environment. We derive common contracts of event, magnitude, timing, and construct a physics grounded pipeline to make comparisons measurable in both external and internal settings. We then ask whether progressively supplying the model's own contract (the A ladder for the internal setting) or a corrected physical contract (the B ladder for the external setting) closes the respective gaps. Across four mechanisms and 20 settings, we find that while language answers all 22 text probes correctly with respect to the true environment, the neutral video is often in disagreement, suggesting that the current unified backbones may not be capable of correct reasoning, internal consistency, and external physical fidelity all at once.

</details>


### [221] [El Agente Potente: High-Throughput Agentic Atomistic Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14840)
*Tsz Wai Ko,Jiaru Bai,Thomas Swanick,Yeonghun Kang,Changhyeok Choi,Angelina Qihong Jiang,Aiwei Yin,Varinia Bernales,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 El Agente Potente，一个结合类型化执行图与编码模式的智能体系统，用于 MLIPs 驱动的原子尺度模拟，兼顾工作流严谨性与流程灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势（MLIPs）已能以远低于从头计算的成本实现接近从头计算的精度，但将其用于高通量性质计算时，如何把高层科学意图转化为自适应模拟流程、同时不牺牲工作流严谨性，仍是核心挑战。需要一种既能保证可审计、可复现执行，又能支持定制化流程的智能体方案。

Method: 系统采用双机制互补架构：（1）类型化执行图（typed execution graphs），提供结构化、来源感知（provenance-aware）的执行，用于标准化工作流，LLM 仅负责规划与路由，科学计算与验证由确定性 Python 组件完成；（2）编码智能体（coding agent），面向需要更高流程灵活性的任务构建定制工作流，并复用已有的 Potente 函数完成受支持的计算。

Result: 在计算材料发现、分子能量景观探索、吸附以及催化反应等工作流中验证了该系统，并开展了可复现性与 LLM token 成本的系统性基准测试。

Conclusion: 类型化执行图与基于代码的工作流构建是智能体科学计算的两种互补机制：前者提供受控、可审计的执行，后者提供定制化原子模拟所需的灵活性。

Abstract: Foundational machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) are transforming atomistic simulations by achieving near-ab initio accuracy across large chemical spaces at a fraction of the computational cost. A central challenge in using these tools for high-throughput property calculations is translating high-level scientific intent into adaptive simulation campaigns without compromising workflow rigour. We introduce El Agente Potente, an agentic system that combines typed execution graphs with a complementary coding mode for MLIPs-driven atomistic simulations. Typed execution graphs provide structured and provenance-aware execution for standardized workflows, with large language models (LLMs) restricted to planning and routing while deterministic Python components perform scientific computation and validation. Complementing this structured execution, a coding agent constructs customized workflows for tasks requiring greater procedural flexibility while invoking existing Potente functions for supported calculations. We demonstrate El Agente Potente across computational materials discovery, molecular energy-landscape exploration, adsorption, and catalytic reaction workflows, together with systematic benchmarks of reproducibility and LLM token cost. These results establish typed execution graphs and code-based workflow construction as complementary mechanisms for agentic scientific computing, combining controlled, auditable execution with the flexibility required for customized atomistic simulations

</details>


### [222] [Self-Orchestrating Language Models: Leveraging Semantic Dependence for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14850)
*Tian Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“自编排语言模型（self-orchestrating language models）”：模型在生成时标注语义依赖关系，运行时据此并行化解码、驱逐中间上下文或推导去噪顺序，实现质量-效率的帕累托最优权衡。通过 PASTA、TIP、Planned Diffusion 三个系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 部署面临显著效率挑战：自回归解码推理延迟高、低批量下硬件利用率低；离散扩散可并行生成但难以在少步数下达到自回归质量；长上下文推理造成内存瓶颈，即使最先进加速器也难以承受。核心动机是让模型自身指导推理执行策略以缓解上述矛盾。

Method: 提出“自编排语言模型”范式：在生成过程中对语义依赖（哪些 token 依赖哪些其他 token）进行标注，并设计对应运行时利用这些标注来（1）并行化自回归解码，（2）驱逐中间上下文，（3）推导去噪顺序。具体实现三个系统：PASTA——利用语义依赖并行化自回归解码，训练模型标注哪些输出块可独立生成；TIP——利用语义依赖从 KV 缓存中驱逐中间推理步骤，在保持准确率的同时降低内存；Planned Diffusion——利用语义依赖为离散扩散推导去噪顺序，自回归生成一个计划以指定哪些块可并行去噪。

Result: 三个系统均在质量-效率权衡上达到帕累托最优：PASTA 实现自回归解码的并行化；TIP 降低 KV 缓存内存占用且保持准确率；Planned Diffusion 通过计划指定并行去噪块，提升离散扩散的生成效率。

Conclusion: 语言模型可通过标注语义依赖来自主指导其推理执行策略，配合相应运行时在解码并行化、上下文驱逐与去噪顺序推导等维度取得质量与效率的帕累托最优平衡，为 LLM 高效部署提供了统一且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive capabilities, but their deployment presents significant efficiency challenges. Autoregressive decoding imposes substantial inference latency and under-utilizes hardware accelerators in low batch size regimes. Discrete diffusion models can generate in parallel but struggle to match autoregressive quality without many diffusion denoising steps. Long-context reasoning creates memory bottlenecks that strain even state-of-the-art accelerators.
  My thesis is that language models can direct their own inference execution strategy by annotating semantic dependence -- which tokens depend on which others -- in their generation. I call such models self-orchestrating language models. For each system, I design a runtime that acts on these annotations to parallelize autoregressive decoding, evict intermediate context, or derive denoising orders, achieving Pareto-optimal quality-efficiency trade-offs.
  I demonstrate this approach through three self-orchestrating systems. First, PASTA uses semantic dependence to parallelize autoregressive decoding, training the model to annotate which output chunks can generate independently. Second, TIP uses semantic dependence to evict intermediate reasoning steps from the KV cache, reducing memory consumption while preserving accuracy. Third, Planned Diffusion uses semantic dependence to derive a denoising order for discrete diffusion, autoregressively generating a plan that specifies which chunks to denoise in parallel.

</details>


### [223] [Domain Generalization for Smartphone-Based Human Activity Recognition: A Systematic Analysis of Components and Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14863)
*Otávio Oliveira Napoli,Edson Borin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对智能手机人体活动识别（HAR）的域泛化（DG）进行了大规模受控基准测试，覆盖超41万次实验，系统评估训练目标、初始化、架构与组合配置在跨数据集和跨位置两类分布偏移下的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机HAR模型在用户、设备、传感器位置、环境和采集协议变化引起的分布偏移下性能退化。域泛化旨在仅用源域数据学习可迁移模型，但现有DG方法（训练目标、表示初始化、架构修改）往往被孤立评估，缺乏对学习流程不同阶段组件的统一比较，因此需要系统性基准来厘清各组件贡献及其交互作用。

Method: 构建大规模受控基准：超过410,000次实验，涵盖4种模型架构、13种训练目标（含ERM）、5种初始化策略、4种架构配置，以及跨数据集和跨位置两种偏移场景；对单独组件与联合配置进行对比，并开展类别级分析和oracle检查点分析。

Result: 单个DG组件带来的增益有限且高度依赖条件：替代训练目标很少稳定优于ERM；自监督初始化仅在特定场景有效；架构修改尤其是动态域泛化（Dynamic Domain Generalization）提供最明确的独立提升。联合配置常优于单独组件，呈现互补甚至超加性交互，但增益仍依赖模型与偏移类型。类别级分析显示最优配置主要改善困难且对偏移敏感的决策边界。Oracle检查点分析表明存在大量未实现性能：源域验证选择在跨数据集和跨位置场景中仅分别回收53%和26%的可用oracle增益。

Conclusion: 有效的HAR域泛化需要联合设计DG组件并采用鲁棒的模型选择策略；单一组件优化不足，模型选择瓶颈是当前性能未实现的重要原因。

Abstract: Smartphone-based Human Activity Recognition (HAR) models often degrade under distribution shifts caused by changes in users, devices, sensor placements, environments, and acquisition protocols. Domain Generalization (DG) addresses this problem by learning from source domains without access to target data. Existing DG methods span training objectives, representation initialization, and architectural modifications, but these components are typically evaluated in isolation despite operating at different stages of the learning pipeline. We present a large-scale controlled benchmark of DG for smartphone-based HAR, comprising more than 410,000 experiments across four model architectures, thirteen training objectives including Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM), five initialization strategies, four architectural configurations, and two shift scenarios: cross-dataset and cross-position. Results show that individual DG components provide limited and highly conditional gains. Alternative objectives rarely outperform ERM consistently, self-supervised initialization helps in specific settings, and architectural modifications, particularly Dynamic Domain Generalization, provide the clearest standalone improvements. Joint configurations, however, frequently outperform their individual components and exhibit complementary and sometimes super-additive interactions, although gains remain model- and shift-dependent. Class-level analysis shows that the strongest configurations mainly improve difficult, shift-sensitive decision boundaries. Finally, oracle checkpoint analysis reveals substantial unrealized performance: source-validation selection recovers only 53% and 26% of the available oracle gain in cross-dataset and cross-position settings, respectively. Overall, effective HAR domain generalization requires jointly designing DG components and robust model-selection strategies.

</details>


### [224] [GGUF-Metadata Prediction of Single-Sequence llama.cpp Throughput Across Three Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14864)
*Xinyu Qiu,Chuhong Xu,Bo Su,Ziyao Chen,Ruiyang Xu,Shimeng Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用GGUF元数据与roofline形式预测器（针对量化设定比例因子，并在参考模型上拟合）预测单序列模型吞吐量，在318个phase-depth测量、53个host-file配置（两台Apple M4 Max与一块NVIDIA RTX 5080）上评估，发现使用“活跃参数”的解码模型误差远低于使用“总参数”，但拟合效率不具备普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单序列推理吞吐量的预测对部署与选型很关键，但直接测量成本高、跨硬件与跨运行时栈迁移困难。作者希望检验：仅凭GGUF文件元数据，是否能在不同宿主与运行时栈上以较低误差预测吞吐量，并辨析“活跃参数”与“总参数”在预测中的差异，以及低比特模型排序是否随运行时栈变化。

Method: 构建roofline形状的预测器，并引入量化特定的比例因子，在参考模型上拟合系数。数据集为318个phase-depth测量，覆盖53个host-file配置、两台Apple M4 Max系统和一块NVIDIA RTX 5080。分别采用host-specific held-out（4、5、2个配置）与leave-one-host-out（用另外两个系统拟合系数）两种评估协议；对比“活跃参数”解码模型与“总参数”计费模型，并评估P2 prefill基线。还考察低比特模型阶梯在不同运行时栈下的排序变化。

Result: 在host-specific held-out的4、5、2个配置上，活跃参数解码模型的MAPE为13.1%、14.4%、36.1%；而计费总参数的MAPE为49.4%、55.3%、51.9%。leave-one-host-out在另外两个系统上拟合系数时，测试MAPE为11.6%、16.8%、36.0%。P2 prefill基线的测试MAPE为18.7%、22.2%、108.2%。低比特模型阶梯在不同运行时栈下排序发生变化。GGUF结构在三台系统上均有帮助，但拟合得到的效率系数不具备普适性。

Conclusion: 基于GGUF元数据的roofline形状预测器可有效预测单序列吞吐量，且以“活跃参数”建模显著优于“总参数”。但量化比例因子拟合出的效率系数跨系统不可直接迁移，低比特模型排序亦随运行时栈改变，说明预测器需按宿主/运行时栈重新校准，GGUF结构信息有普适价值而拟合参数无普适性。

Abstract: We predict single-sequence model throughput from GGUF metadata using roofline-shaped predictors with quantization-specific scale factors fitted on reference models. The scored cohort comprises 318 phase-depth measurements from 53 host-file configurations on two Apple M4 Max systems and an NVIDIA RTX 5080. On host-specific held-out sets of four, five, and two configurations, an active-parameter decode model obtains 13.1%, 14.4%, and 36.1% mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), versus 49.4%, 55.3%, and 51.9% when charging total parameters. Leave-one-host-out coefficients fitted on the other two systems yield 11.6%, 16.8%, and 36.0% test MAPE. A low-bit model ladder changes ordering across runtime stacks. The P2 prefill baseline gives 18.7%, 22.2%, and 108.2% test MAPE. GGUF structure helps on all three systems, but fitted efficiencies are not universal.

</details>


### [225] [Externalizing Requirement-to-Repair Artifacts as Observable Traces for LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14913)
*Zewen Tao,Shin-nosuke Ishikawa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: THEMIS：一个阶段感知的仓库级修复工作流，通过语义解释、运行时需求-代码图、图推导的开发者指导、保留的修复理由与补丁以及事后审计记录，将"需求到修复"过程外化、可检查。基于300个SWE-bench Lite案例的回顾性审计表明这些产物支持跨阶段检查，并实现跨阶段对应关系的系统测量；初步对照实验显示关系式工作流修复19例 vs 基线9例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级修复不仅需要正确补丁，还需要可检查的记录来解释问题需求如何转化为代码修改及事后证据。现有方法偏重补丁正确性，缺乏对"需求→修复"跨阶段转换过程的可追溯、可审计支持。

Method: THEMIS 采用阶段感知的修复工作流，包含：语义解释、运行时需求-代码图（runtime requirement-code graph）、由图推导的 Developer 指导、保留的修复理由与补丁、以及事后审计记录。评估上对 300 个 SWE-bench Lite 案例进行回顾性审计，并通过配对的 100 案例比较（关系式工作流 vs 直接同输入条件）进行初步验证。

Result: 288 例具备完整 Developer 理由；214 例（71.3%）保留了连接所选阶段的完整审计字段集。目标符号在 62.6% 的 Developer 理由与 62.8% 的补丁中复现，纳入相关符号后升至 75.8%。配对 100 案例中，关系式工作流修复 19 例，直接同输入条件修复 9 例。但两条件在 Analyzer 输出、图推导蒸馏与 Judge 记录上亦存在差异，故仅作为工作流层面的初步证据，而非图组件的因果效应。

Conclusion: THEMIS 使原本隐式的"需求到修复"转换变得可检查，支持系统性地考察修复决策如何跨阶段持续、对齐与演化。

Abstract: Repository-level repair requires not only correct patches but also inspectable records that explain how issue requirements are translated into code changes and post-edit evidence. We contribute THEMIS, a stage-aware repair workflow that externalizes this requirement-to-repair process through semantic interpretation, a runtime requirement-code graph, graph-derived Developer guidance, retained repair rationale and patches, and post-edit audit records. A retrospective audit of 300 SWE-bench Lite cases demonstrates that these artifacts provide broad support for cross-stage inspection: a complete Developer rationale is available for 288 cases, and 214 cases (71.3%) retain a complete audited field set connecting the selected stages. The retained records further enable systematic measurement of cross-stage correspondence: target symbols recur in 62.6% of Developer rationales and in 62.8% of patches, rising to 75.8% when related symbols are included. In a paired 100-case comparison, the relational workflow resolves 19 cases versus 9 for the direct same-input condition; because the two conditions also differ in Analyzer output, graph-derived distillation, and Judge records, we report this as preliminary, workflow-level evidence rather than a causal effect of the graph component. Together, these results show that THEMIS makes otherwise implicit requirement-to-repair transitions inspectable, enabling systematic examination of how repair decisions persist, align, and evolve across stages.

</details>


### [226] [Geometric Flow enhanced Graph Coarsening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14962)
*Chaoqun Fei,Guoxuan Li,Tinglve Zhou,Chuanqing Wang,Yangyang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型图池化方法 RicciPool，利用离散图曲率（Ollivier-Ricci曲率）刻画邻居间的高阶连通性，构建Ollivier-Ricci流重新加权边权重，并结合谱聚类学习聚类分配矩阵，在生物信息学与社交网络数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于聚类的图池化方法（作为降低GCNN计算成本的手段）大多只关注图的粗粒度拓扑信息，忽略了邻居间高阶互连关系的挖掘。而消息传递中边上的信息传递难易程度反映节点间紧密度，且高度依赖邻居间的互连性，因此该信息对图池化（尤其是聚类）至关重要。

Method: 提出RicciPool：(1) 引入离散图曲率，特别是Ollivier-Ricci曲率，作为边周围高阶连通性的度量；(2) 构造Ollivier-Ricci流公式对边权重重新加权，利用曲率提供的聚类关键信息；(3) 在该基础之上采用谱聚类技术学习新的聚类分配矩阵。

Result: 在多个生物信息学蛋白质数据集和社交网络上进行实验，结果验证了所提方法的有效性。摘要中未给出具体量化指标。

Conclusion: 通过将Ollivier-Ricci曲率引入图池化以捕捉局部高阶连接信息，并结合谱聚类，RicciPool弥补了现有方法忽视邻居间高阶互连的不足，实验证明其有效性。

Abstract: Recently, researchers have proposed a graph pooling operation, akin to the pooling process in conventional convolutional neural networks (CNN), aimed at reducing the computation cost of Graph convolutional neural networks (GCNNs). While most GCNN-based methods treat graph pooling as a node clustering problem and propose learning a cluster assignment matrix, existing clustering-based pooling methods tend to focus solely on the rough topology information of graphs, neglecting the exploitation of higher-order mutual connections among neighbors. In terms of message passing on graph, the ease of information passing on edges reflects the closeness between neighboring nodes, which significantly relies on the interconnectivity among neighbors. In this study, we address this gap by considering such local connection information and introducing a novel graph pooling method named RicciPool. We introduce discrete graph curvature, particularly Ollivier-Ricci curvature, as a measure of higher-order connectivity around an edge. Subsequently, we construct an Ollivier-Ricci flow formula to reweigh edge weights, leveraging the crucial information provided by Ricci curvature, particularly vital for extracting clusters in graphs. Building upon this foundation, we utilize the spectral clustering technique to learn a new cluster assignment matrix. Experimental results on multiple bioinformatics protein datasets and social networks underscore the effectiveness of our proposed method.

</details>
