### [227] [Towards a knowledge-enhanced single-cell foundation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14970)
*Hanqing Zhang,Jie Bao,Mei Ma,Shuai Liu,Jiaying Ma,Jiaguan Liu,Jiaxiao Li,Zhenbo Li,Wenwen Gong,Zhijun Ca*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: scKITE 通过在预训练中融合细胞注释文本与基因调控等生物知识，仅用不到以往强基线 0.5% 的样本量即在下游任务中全面超越现有单细胞基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单细胞基础模型依赖大规模转录组预训练，但继续扩大数据规模收益递减而计算成本显著上升，需要寻找数据量之外的新扩展维度。

Method: 通过数据缩放分析发现生物知识（细胞级文本注释、基因级调控信息）可作为额外扩展维度；据此提出 scKITE，在共享的转录组 Transformer 编码器上引入轻量辅助解码器进行细胞注释与基因调控监督，辅助解码器仅用于预训练并在下游阶段丢弃，从而得到富含生物知识的通用编码器。

Result: 仅使用 179,067 个预训练样本（不足以往强单细胞基础模型的 0.5%），scKITE 在多样化下游任务上仍优于这些模型。

Conclusion: 知识增强预训练是构建具有生物学基础的单细胞基础模型的一种有前景的新范式。

Abstract: Single-cell foundation models (scFMs) increasingly rely on large-scale transcriptomic pretraining, yet expanding pretraining data can yield diminishing gains while substantially increasing computational cost. Our data scaling analyses showed that incorporating biological knowledge, including cell-level text annotation and gene-level regulatory information, provided additional scaling dimension than simply increasing data size. Motivated by this observation, we present scKITE, a simple yet effective scFM that integrates cell-annotation and gene-regulatory supervision into a shared transcriptomic Transformer encoder through lightweight auxiliary decoders. These decoders are used only during pretraining and subsequently discarded, yielding a general-purpose encoder enriched with biological knowledge for downstream applications. With only 179,067 pretraining samples, i.e., less than 0.5\% of those used by previous strong scFMs, scKITE outperformed these models across diverse downstream tasks, highlighting knowledge-enhanced pretraining as a promising paradigm for biologically grounded scFMs.

</details>


### [228] [MemRiskBench: Trace-Aware Risk-Preserving Evaluation for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14976)
*Jianhua Jiang,Dongbo Yuan,Weihua Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MemRiskBench，一个面向长时程 LLM 智能体记忆风险的评测基准，包含五类风险分类法、确定性 trace 校验脚本，以及一个风险保持子集选择器，在 20% 子集规模下保持完整排序、风险覆盖与高风险模型检测能力，将计算量降低 5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程 LLM 智能体跨会话累积记忆，带来稀疏但高影响的风险：事实过时、更新冲突、跨用户泄露、已撤销记忆复用、约束衰减。标准聚合分数会掩盖各风险的单独失败率（如平均准确率 78% 的模型仍可能在 4% 的回合中泄露数据），且基准压缩往往会优先丢弃区分"基本可用"与"偶尔有害"的罕见高严重度事件。

Method: 构建五类风险分类法（外加一个记录但不计分类别），以确定性 trace 校验进行可操作化；实例化为 120 回合的脚本化基准，具备完整 trace 日志，通过/失败判定路径不使用 LLM-as-judge；在五个本地运行的量化指令微调模型上评测。另提出风险保持子集选择器：基于确定性 trace 派生特征的覆盖约束贪心选择器。

Result: 子集选择器在 20% 子集规模下保持完整排序（Spearman rho = 0.975，确定性；置信区间收缩为点估计，bootstrap 方差为零）、风险覆盖 1.0、高风险模型检测 1.0，并将计算量降低 5 倍。所有回合、trace、评分实现与选择器均已发布。

Conclusion: MemRiskBench 通过确定性、trace 落地的评测方式揭示聚合指标掩盖的长时程记忆风险，其风险保持子集选择器区别于仅保排序的选择器，额外保持风险类型覆盖与高风险检测且无需 LLM 评判器，为已部署 LLM 智能体的可复现评测与风险评估提供支持。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents accumulate memory across sessions, creating sparse but high-impact risks: stale facts, conflicting updates, cross-user leakage, revoked-memory reuse, and constraint decay. Standard aggregate scores hide per-risk failure rates--a model achieving 78% average accuracy may still leak data in 4% of episodes--and benchmark compression preferentially discards the rare high-severity events that distinguish a mostly-working model from one that occasionally causes harm. We present MemRiskBench. The primary contribution is a five-category risk taxonomy (plus one documented, unscored category) operationalized by deterministic trace grounded checks, instantiated as a 120-episode scripted benchmark with full trace logging and no LLM-as-judge on the pass/fail path, evaluated on five locally run quantized instruction-tuned models. Second, a risk-preserving subset selector: a coverage-constrained greedy selector on deterministic trace-derived features that retains full ranking (Spearman rho = 0.975, deterministic; CI collapses to a point estimate with zero bootstrap variance), risk coverage (1.0), and high-risk model detection (1.0) at a 20% subset size, reducing compute 5x. Unlike ranking-only subset selectors, this selector additionally preserves risk-type coverage and high-risk detection using trace-grounded deterministic features that do not require an LLM judge. All episodes, traces, the scoring implementation, and the selector are released to support reproducible evaluation and risk assessment of deployed LLM agents

</details>


### [229] [Shallow Beliefs: Synthetic document finetuning does not inoculate against emergent misalignment from reward hacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.14998)
*Arun Jose,Julian Stastny*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文探究合成文档微调（SDF）能否在中训练阶段“预防性接种”模型，使其在后续可被利用的RL环境中学会reward hacking时，不产生广泛失准（EM）。结果显示：SDF虽在行为上成功塑造了对reward hacking的正面态度，但未能阻止EM；而同场景下的inoculation prompting（IP）可以阻止EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，在RL环境中学会reward hacking的模型可能广泛失准（EM），而inoculation prompting（IP）通过把reward hacking重构为可接受行为，可阻断这种泛化。作者希望验证：能否用合成文档微调（SDF）实现“无需在后续训练中干预”的接种效果，即提前把reward hacking描述为可接受行为，从而抑制后续RL训练中的EM泛化。

Method: 在模型的中训练语料中加入将reward hacking描述为可接受行为的合成文档，随后在可被利用的RL环境中训练这些模型，使其学会reward hacking。将该SDF设置与同一环境下的IP设置对比，并测试模型对reward hacking的表述态度与其自身reward-hacking输出的认可度，以及是否出现EM。

Result: 行为层面SDF有效：模型对reward hacking给出正面描述，并更认可自身产生的reward-hacking输出。但学会reward hacking后，模型仍表现出强烈的EM；而在同一设置下，IP能够阻止EM。进一步表明，SDF在插入新关联时能可预测地引导下游泛化，但在覆盖既有关联（如reward hacking与失准之间的联系）时表现吃力且效果不可预测。

Conclusion: 在所测试的规模下，SDF可以让模型表面上与目标信念对齐，却会以非预期方式引导其从后续训练中的泛化。因此，SDF作为“提前接种”手段并不可靠，尤其在需要覆盖已有有害关联时；相比之下，IP在阻止EM上更有效。

Abstract: Recent work shows that models that learn to reward hack on RL environments can become broadly misaligned, and that reframing reward hacking as acceptable behavior during training (inoculation prompting, or IP) blocks this generalization. We ask whether synthetic document finetuning (SDF) can inoculate a model against future training we don't intervene on. We add synthetic documents framing reward hacking as acceptable behavior to a model's midtraining corpus, and then train these models with RL on exploitable environments, teaching them to reward hack. Behaviorally, midtraining succeeds: models describe reward hacking favorably and are more approving of reward-hacking outputs they produce. However, they show strong EM after learning to reward hack, while IP in the same setting prevents EM. We show that SDF can predictably steer downstream generalization when inserting new associations, but struggles and has unpredictable effects when overriding existing associations, such as that between reward hacking and misalignment that produces EM. Our results suggest that, at the scales we test, SDF can make a model appear aligned with desired beliefs while steering its generalization from later training in unintended ways.

</details>


### [230] [CoMem: Collective-Individual Memory Synergy for Evolutionary Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15009)
*Chengxin Yu,Zhaoxin Fan,Faguo Wu,Hongwei Zheng,Yun Zhou,Zhiyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CoMem，一种面向LLM多智能体系统的集体-个体记忆协同架构，通过私有经验沉淀、集体智慧策展与并行双流检索来缓解扁平记忆的噪声污染与智能体同质化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的多智能体系统虽在协作能力上取得进展，但记忆机制多为扁平、无结构形式，易被噪声填满，并抹除智能体之间的差异，阻碍其持续学习与性能提升。

Method: 提出集体-个体记忆协同概念与CoMem架构，包含三部分：(i)私有经验沉淀，使每个智能体维护并更新自身有用记忆；(ii)集体智慧策展，仅筛选被广泛验证的想法在智能体间共享；(iii)并行双流检索，使智能体同时调用私有记忆与群体智慧，并通过聚类保证多样性。

Result: 在ALFWorld与PDDL基准上，CoMem取得较强的整体性能，并能稳健地避免记忆污染。

Conclusion: 融合私有经验与共享知识的结构化记忆设计可有效提升多智能体学习效果并抑制记忆污染，为LLM多智能体记忆机制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Designing effective memory mechanisms is crucial for advancing LLM-driven Multi-Agent Systems (MAS), helping agents learn together and perform better over time. While recent work has led to strong cooperation skills, most methods still use flat, unstructured memories, which easily get filled with noise and erase differences between agents. To address this, we introduce the concept of collective-individual memory synergy and propose CoMem, an architecture that unifies both private experience and shared knowledge for multi-agent learning. CoMem features:(i) Private Experience Sedimentation, which lets each agent keep and update its own useful memories over time;(ii) Collective Wisdom Curation, which carefully selects only widely proven ideas to be shared among agents;(iii)Parallel Dual-Stream Retrieval, which allows agents to draw both from their own memory and the group's wisdom, using clustering to ensure diversity.Experiments on ALFWorld and PDDL benchmarks show that CoMem achieves strong overall performance and robustly avoids memory pollution.

</details>


### [231] [Semantic-TVM: Structure-Preserving Trustworthy Virtual Memory for Memory-Augmented and Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15011)
*Yu Li,Qikun Cai,Tao Huang,Chen Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出可信虚拟内存（TVM）运行时，将精确值状态保留在本地，仅向远端LLM暴露受保护的投影视图，在降低隐私暴露的同时保持任务可执行性与效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 记忆增强和工具调用型智能体在远端LLM处理检索记忆、工具动作和中间观测时会直接暴露精确的私有值；单向掩码虽限制直接暴露，但会移除可信执行所需的值，且可能通过后续观测泄露。

Method: 提出TVM闭环运行时，包含两种机制：Rule-TVM用本地可恢复句柄替换整个受保护字段；Semantic-TVM则由可信本地模型预测敏感片段，仅替换这些片段，保留周边任务相关上下文。

Result: 在Memory-EHR和Memory-RAP上、两家提供商测试中，片段级投影挽回了整字段替换下损失的大部分EHR效用（DeepSeek上任务成功率84.17% vs. 52.33%），同时测得暴露保持较低且工作流仍可执行。

Conclusion: 片段级（语义）投影在隐私暴露与任务效用之间取得更优权衡，优于整字段替换，且能维持工作流可执行性。

Abstract: Memory-augmented and tool-using agents expose exact private values when remote LLMs process retrieved memory, tool actions, and intermediate observations. One-way masking limits direct exposure but removes values needed for trusted execution and can leak them through later observations. We propose Trustworthy Virtual Memory (TVM), a closed-loop runtime that keeps exact-value state local while presenting a protected view to the remote model. Within this single runtime, Rule-TVM replaces whole protected fields with locally recoverable handles, and Semantic-TVM instead replaces only sensitive spans predicted by a trusted local model, preserving surrounding task-relevant context. On Memory-EHR and Memory-RAP across two providers, span-level projection recovers most of the EHR utility lost under whole-field replacement (Task Success 84.17% vs. 52.33% on DeepSeek) while measured exposure stays low and workflows remain executable.

</details>


### [232] [Overflip: Repetition-Induced Label Flips in Guardrail Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15013)
*Xu He,Chih-Hsuan Lin,Hung-Mao Chen,Junjie Xiong,Yan Zhai,Kun Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示轻量级护栏模型存在由重复引起的预测翻转不稳定现象（Overflip），在9个模型中5个出现MAL→BEN翻转，翻转率8%–92%，首次翻转约在2.6k–9.4k token处，并对LLM服务构成比传统注意力稀释更严重的威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估假设护栏模型（guardrail）的判断在输入被加长时保持稳定，许多轻量级护栏采用短上下文窗口（约512 token）的紧凑Transformer骨干和分桶相对位置编码，这一稳定性假设此前未被检验。作者希望探究输入长度变化是否会破坏护栏判断的稳定性。

Method: 作者识别出Overflip现象：重复提示使护栏预测随序列增长而发生翻转（MAL→BEN）。在9个广泛使用的轻量级护栏模型上进行实验，构建包含100条提示的基准测试；通过分析token级注意力分布，将Overflip与传统注意力稀释基线进行比较。

Result: 9个模型中有5个出现MAL→BEN翻转，置信度边际随重复持续缩小；翻转率在8%到92%之间，首次翻转大约发生在2.6k至9.4k token处。分析表明Overflip不同于传统注意力稀释：它在保留恶意内容的同时，使重复结构上的token级注意力趋于同质化，并形成一种与填充不同的、更渐进的注意力分散轨迹。

Conclusion: Overflip对LLM服务构成比传统注意力稀释更大的威胁，因为被绕过的提示语义完整，下游业务LLM仍能理解并传递恶意意图。这些发现将“重复”暴露为护栏模型的攻击面，呼吁开展长度鲁棒的评估与缓解措施。

Abstract: Guardrail models are classifiers deployed to screen malicious prompts and responses in LLM-based services. To meet latency constraints, many lightweight guardrails adopt compact Transformer backbones (e.g., DeBERTa) that are trained with short context windows (typically 512 tokens) and rely on bucketed relative positional encodings to process longer inputs. Prior evaluations assume that a guardrail's decision is stable as the input is lengthened. We show that this assumption can fail. We identify Overflip, a repetition-induced instability where repeating a prompt causes the guardrail's prediction to flip (MAL$\to$BEN) as the sequence grows. We conduct experiments on 9 widely used lightweight guardrail models. Five exhibit MAL$\to$BEN flips on a benchmark of 100 prompts, with confidence margins shrinking steadily with repetition. Among these vulnerable models, flip rates range from 8% to 92%, with first flips occurring at roughly 2.6k--9.4k tokens. Our analysis suggests Overflip differs from traditional attention-dilution baselines, which aim to divert the model's attention away from tokens associated with malicious content, shifting it instead toward unrelated content, such as benign padding or shuffling. While Overflip preserves malicious content, it homogenizes token-level attention over repeated structure and induces a distinct, more gradual attention-dispersion trajectory than padding. Moreover, Overflip poses a greater threat to LLM services than traditional attention dilution methods. Because the bypassed prompt remains semantically intact and is still readily understood by downstream business LLMs, it can transmit malicious intent after passing the guardrail. These findings expose repetition as an attack surface for guardrail models and motivate length-robust evaluation and mitigation.

</details>


### [233] [Four Ledgers, Not One Score: Responsible Communication of LLM-Judge Calibration in Biomedical ML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15015)
*Sidi Chang,Peiying Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过法证式审计指出，将合成扰动键直接当作检测器输出或人类金标准来评估LLM评判器，会把"提议确认率"和"提交账本构成"误报为评判精确率与召回率，并给出溯源感知的声明审计、存储契约与最低校准门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学机器学习中专家复核稀缺，合成扰动被视为LLM评判器的廉价校准数据。但作者强调，植入的变异键既不等于检测器输出，也不自动等于人类真值，混淆两类账本会导致能力声明失真，因此需要审计现有评估设计。

Method: 形式化四类账本：植入扰动、独立检测器输出、来源关联的人类处置、人类新增发现。随后对一套私有合成日语照护交接工作流的评估设计、评分代码、读取路径与现有记录进行审计，并进行直接审计恒等式推导，比较不同账本口径下的比值含义。

Result: 工厂在47个目标中存储69张植入错误卡；最终复核覆盖22个目标，含22条已确认导入提议、9条被拒提议、79张人类新增卡，仅3个目标双重标注。将导入植入键送入通用检测器评分器得到22/(22+9)=0.710与22/(22+79)=0.218；审计恒等式显示这两个值分别是提议确认产出率与提交账本构成，而非评判精确率与召回率，因为可用记录中未保留独立的检测器实例化结果。审计还发现来源名冲突、行遮蔽、强制严重度、空比值默认以及无支撑的零支持字段权重。

Conclusion: 该单工作流法证案例是失败模式的存在性证明，而非其流行率估计；现有人工工作仅支持对合成提议的探索性审计，不足以支撑LLM评判器操作特性、临床有效性、语料流行率或稳健标注者间一致性等主张。作者贡献了溯源感知的声明审计、存储契约与最低校准门槛。

Abstract: Synthetic perturbations appear to offer inexpensive calibration data for LLM evaluators in biomedical ML, where expert review is scarce. Yet a planted mutation key is neither a detector output nor automatically human ground truth. We formalize four distinct ledgers: planted perturbations, independent detector outputs, source-linked human dispositions, and human-added discoveries. We then audit the evaluation design, scoring code, read paths, and current human records of a private synthetic Japanese care-handoff workflow. The factory stored 69 planted error cards across 47 targets. Final review covers 22 targets and contains 22 confirmed imported proposals, 9 rejected proposals, and 79 human-added cards; only 3 reviewed targets are double annotated. Passing imported plant keys to a generic detector scorer yields 22/(22+9)=0.710 and 22/(22+79)=0.218. A direct audit identity shows that these values are proposal-confirmation yield and submitted-ledger composition, not judge precision and recall, because no independent detector realization was preserved for the audited proposals in the available records. The audit also finds source-name collisions, row shadowing, forced severity, vacuous ratio defaults, and unsupported zero-support field weights. We contribute a provenance-aware claim audit, a storage contract, and a minimum calibration gate for responsibly communicating biomedical ML capability claims. This single-workflow forensic case is an existence proof of a failure mode, not an estimate of its prevalence: existing human work supports an exploratory audit of synthetic proposals, but not LLM-judge operating characteristics, clinical validity, corpus prevalence, or robust inter-annotator agreement.

</details>


### [234] [Horizon-specific Expert Fusion for Photovoltaic Power Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15035)
*Xu Yuqing,Zhou Liguo,Sun Ze,Yu Lei,Jiang Mingming*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层集成框架，结合时序神经网络、历史相似日、状态气候学和梯度提升树，用于短期光伏功率预测，并采用随预测时域变化的凸权重融合及独立偏差校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 短期光伏预测需同时刻画规律性太阳周期与天气驱动波动，且二者重要性随时域变化，单一模型难以兼顾。

Method: 以太阳几何和数值天气预报描述预期发电条件；集成时序神经模型、历史相似日、状态气候学与梯度提升树；用时域特定的凸权重融合互补预测；再用历史预测误差进行独立校准以修正近期偏差。

Result: 在PVDAQ数据15–240分钟时域上，日照容量归一化MAE为4.315%，较全特征LightGBM降低4.11%，较微调Chronos-2降低6.03%；消融实验显示集成内部存在冗余。在GEFCom2014三个区域、1–4小时时域、三个训练种子上，学习融合优于等权，但与LightGBM相当。

Conclusion: 时域特定融合是一种有效的预测策略，但其相对强单一模型的优势依赖数据集与评估时段。

Abstract: Short-term photovoltaic power forecasting requires models to represent regular solar cycles and weather-driven fluctuations whose importance changes with the forecast horizon. This study develops a hierarchical ensemble that combines temporal neural models, historical analogs, state climatology, and gradient-boosted trees. Solar geometry and numerical weather forecasts describe the expected generation conditions, while horizon-specific convex weights combine complementary predictions. A separate calibration step uses available historical forecast errors to account for recent bias. The framework is evaluated on public PVDAQ data at 15--240-minute horizons and on three GEFCom2014 solar zones at hourly horizons up to four hours. On PVDAQ, the ensemble achieves a daylight capacity-normalized mean absolute error of 4.315%, reducing error by 4.11% relative to full-feature LightGBM and by 6.03% relative to fine-tuned Chronos-2 under identical calibration. Expert-removal experiments identify redundancy within the ensemble. Across three training seeds on GEFCom2014, learned fusion improves upon equal weighting but performs comparably to LightGBM. The results support horizon-specific combination as a useful forecasting strategy while showing that its advantage over strong individual models depends on the dataset and evaluation period.

</details>


### [235] [The average-farmer illusion in language-model simulations of agricultural decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15038)
*Zhanliang Zhu,Ziwei Li,Yuchen Liu,Liujun Zhu,Ruiqi Wu,Tongqing Shen,Junliang Jin,Jianyun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 语言模型智能体在人口均值/分布层面看似逼真，但在个体层面预测薄弱，且一个仅拟合边际分布的朴素生成器在分布相似性上超过所有语言模型配置——作者称之为"平均农民错觉"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体被日益用于问卷调查和社会模拟中的"合成人"，但其真实性多以总体均值或分布相似度来评判。作者希望检验这类证据究竟能证明什么：分布层面的相似是否意味着个体行为模拟的有效性。

Method: 将Claude、Codex、Kimi置于四种预设提示设计下，与中国及四个非洲国家的农民实际决策进行匹配比较。评估指标包括总体均值/采纳率的复现、个体层面预测、决策分布形态，并设置一个仅拟合观测边际分布、不含任何个体信息的朴素生成器作为基线对照；同时检验提示增强对条件预测的影响。

Result: 部分配置能复现观测均值与采纳率，但个体层面预测能力弱：决策聚集在典型值附近，政策相关的极端值大量缺失。仅拟合边际分布的简单生成器在分布相似性上超过所有语言模型配置。提示增强只带来条件性增益（随模型、结局、人群和验证目标变化），而非普遍改善。

Conclusion: 提出"平均农民错觉"概念：合成人群可以看起来逼真，却无法复现"谁做什么"及行为变异。作者给出与声称相匹配（claim-matched）的验证框架和可复用的模块化提示，将提示构建变为可审计的实验过程；结论是总体层面的相似性应被视为验证的起点，而非个体模拟有效的证据。

Abstract: Language-model agents are increasingly used as synthetic people in surveys and social simulations, yet their apparent realism is often judged from population averages or distributional similarity. We tested what such evidence actually establishes by comparing Claude, Codex and Kimi under four prespecified prompt designs with matched farmer decisions from China and four African countries. Some configurations reproduced observed means and adoption rates. However, their person-level predictions were weak; their decisions clustered around typical values and policy-relevant extremes were largely missing. Most strikingly, a simple generator fitted only to the observed marginal dis- tribution, and given no information about any farmer, achieved greater distributional similarity than every language-model configuration. Prompt additions produced conditional gains rather than uni- versal improvement: results varied with model, outcome, population and validation target. We call this the average-farmer illusion: a synthetic population can look realistic while failing to repro- duce who does what or how behaviour varies. We provide a claim-matched validation framework and reusable modular prompts that turn prompt construction into an auditable experimental process. Population-level resemblance should therefore be treated as the start of validation, not as evidence of individual simulation.

</details>


### [236] [BusMA: A Bus Communication Substrate for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15054)
*Yanwen Peng,Delvin Ce Zhang,Xi Wang,Nikolaos Aletras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出基于总线架构的多智能体通信框架BusMA，允许任意智能体通过共享通道互相寻址，突破层级与路由式通信对自主性的限制，在13个任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体系统的两大主流通信协议——层级式Manager-Worker（HMW）与路由式消息传递（RMP）——存在两点缺陷：Worker无法直接咨询特定同伴，自主性受限；消息误路由会导致错误传播。

Method: 受计算机总线架构启发，BusMA由智能体注册、消息路由、共享内存管理三个组件构成。配备工具的Worker拥有本地记忆，可推理、调用工具，并通过共享消息以特定意图通信；定义了讨论、质疑、指导、请求解释四种意图以支持细粒度交互；由Chair智能体监控共享内存以协调交互并促进Worker间收敛。

Result: 在两个前沿LLM上，覆盖视觉推理、数学推理与知识检索的13个任务上开展实验，BusMA持续优于当前最优的HMW与RMP方法。

Conclusion: 共享总线式通信可提升多智能体系统的通信自主性与协调效率，是一种优于层级式和路由式协议的有效通信框架。

Abstract: Multi-Agent (MA) systems are effective at solving complex tasks that demand planning, tool use, and the synthesis of evidence from multiple sources. Existing systems typically adopt Hierarchical Manager-Worker (HMW) or Router-based Message Passing (RMP) structures as their communication protocol. However, these designs restrict agent autonomy: Worker agents cannot directly consult specific "peers", and misrouted messages can propagate errors. Inspired by bus architectures in computer systems, we propose BusMA, a communication framework that allows any agent to address other agents through a shared channel, i.e., the Bus. It consists of agent registration, message routing, and shared memory management components. Worker agents, each equipped with tools, have their own local memory and can reason, act (tool usage), and communicate by posting shared messages with specific intents. We introduce four intents: discussion, challenge, guidance, and request for explanation, which support fine-grained communication among agents. A Chair agent monitors the shared memory to coordinate interactions and facilitate convergence among Workers. To evaluate the effectiveness of BusMA, we conduct extensive experiments with two frontier LLMs across 13 tasks spanning visual reasoning, mathematical reasoning, and knowledge retrieval demonstrate that BusMA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art HMW and RMP methods.

</details>


### [237] [Enabling Creative Exploration for Vibe Design Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15078)
*Yifan Zhang,Nghi D. Q. Bui,Georgios Evangelopoulos,Arnaud Benard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将"设计方向"显式化的推理架构：用前置模块生成带典型性评分的结构化设计规范，再由选择器采样其一，最后由固定设置的下游生成器实现，从而在保持生成设置不变的前提下探索多样的UI备选方案，而非依赖提高token温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"氛围设计"智能体（vibe design agents）能将自然语言需求转为界面渲染与前端代码，但用户真正需要的是探索一组连贯的备选设计，而非单一可用页面。直接提高token级温度是粗暴做法，因为它会同时扰动审美决策与对语法敏感的代码，导致探索与实现纠缠在一起。

Method: 提出把探索与实现解耦的推理架构，使设计方向成为显式的中间决策。受Verbalized Sampling启发：预先提案（pre-pass）生成带典型性分数（typicality scores）的结构化设计规范；外部选择器采样其中一个；下游生成器在固定设置下，依据所选规范与原始需求完成实现。方法应用于UI主题与视觉素材提示词两类任务。

Result: 在168个提示词上，每个干预项、每个温度做1,255次配对比较：主题采样扩大了实际选择覆盖率与截图多样性；LLM评判偏好随干预方式、提示复杂度与视口而变化。在超过30万任务的在线实验中，代码导出量的提升在统计上仍不确定，同时负面反馈事件减少，但伴随更多纠正交互与一定的运营成本。

Conclusion: 结构化设计规范可作为探索替代UI概念的有效控制点，同时保持下游生成设置固定，为设计智能体提供了比调温度更可控的多样性探索方式。

Abstract: Vibe design agents turn natural-language briefs into rendered interfaces and frontend code. Yet a useful design agent should do more than produce one valid page: it should help users explore coherent alternatives. Increasing token-level temperature is a blunt solution because it varies aesthetic decisions and syntax-sensitive code at the same time. We instead separate exploration from implementation through an inference architecture that makes design direction an explicit intermediate decision. Inspired by Verbalized Sampling, a pre-pass proposes structured design specifications with typicality scores, an external selector samples one, and the downstream generator realizes the selected specification together with the original request under fixed settings. We apply this approach to UI themes and visual-asset prompts. Across 168 prompts, with 1,255 paired comparisons per temperature for each intervention, theme sampling broadens observed selection coverage and screenshot variation, while LLM-judge preferences vary across interventions, prompt complexity, and viewport. In an online experiment with more than 300,000 tasks, the observed code-export increase remains statistically uncertain, while fewer negative feedback events coexist with more correction interactions and modest operational costs. Together, these findings identify structured design specifications as a practical control point for exploring alternative UI concepts while keeping downstream generation settings fixed.

</details>


### [238] [ER-EDF: A Psychology-Grounded Emotion Regulation Framework for Speech Empathetic Dialogue Generation in Large Audio-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15089)
*Hongyu Jin,Wenda Zhang,Runqiu Fei,Gongping Huang,Mike Conway,Ting Dang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ER-EDF框架，将语音对话系统中共情回应生成的情感感知与情感调节显式解耦，提升大型音频语言模型的共情回应质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音对话系统中共情回应生成需要准确的情感感知与恰当的调节，但现有大型音频语言模型多将情感作为直接条件信号，缺乏显式调节机制，导致情感镜像而非校准支持。

Method: 受感知-行动模型与情感调节理论启发，提出模型无关的ER-EDF框架，将情感感知（追踪用户情绪状态）与情感调节（决定该状态如何引导共情回应生成）显式解耦；构建语音共情对话数据集，并引入超越词汇匹配的共情感知评价指标。

Result: 在五个大型音频语言模型和两个数据集上的实验表明，ER-EDF在自动评价与人工评价中均持续提升共情回应质量。

Conclusion: 联合建模情感感知与情感调节对语音共情对话系统至关重要，为心理学基础的共情AI开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Empathetic response generation in spoken dialogue systems requires both accurate emotion perception and appropriate emotion regulation. Grounded in psychological theories such as the Perception-Action Model and emotion regulation theory, effective empathy depends not only on inferring a user's affective state but also on regulating how it is expressed in responses. However, recent large audio-language models (LALMs) largely treat emotion as a direct conditioning signal, lacking explicit regulatory mechanisms, which often leads to affect mirroring rather than calibrated support. We propose ER-EDF, a psychology-grounded framework that explicitly decouples emotion perception and emotion regulation in LALMs. Perception tracks the user's emotional state, while regulation determines how this state should guide empathetic response generation. The framework is model-agnostic and integrates seamlessly into existing LALMs. We further construct a spoken empathetic dialogue dataset and introduce empathy-aware evaluation metrics beyond lexical matching. Experiments across five LALMs and two datasets show that ER-EDF consistently improves empathetic response quality in both automatic and human evaluations, highlighting the importance of jointly modeling emotion perception and regulation in spoken empathetic dialogue systems, paving a new direction for psychologically grounded empathetic AI.

</details>


### [239] [OpenAI4S: Code as Action, Science as Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15096)
*Gongbo Zhang,Hao Li,Yu Wang,Mujie Lin,Liuzhenghao Lv,Yicheng Mao,Yimi Wang,Jun Zhu,Minhan Tang,Zhengxiang Jiang,Yusong Wang,Jiayu Yao,Kunpeng Ning,Dawei Pang,Yonghong Tian,OpenAI4S Community,Yuyang Liu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenAI4S是一个基于"代码即行动、科学即会话"原则的开源科研智能体，通过持久化计算运行时与研究会话管理（含Action Ledger、工作区检查点等溯源机制）提升AI辅助科研工作流的可检查、可恢复与可复现性。在36个科研场景评测中总体得分7.83，显著优于通用编程框架（5.7–6.4），但在环境规范与完全重跑方面仍是所有系统（含本系统）的薄弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI共同科学家可加速计算研究，但长期运行的研究工作流需要保持可检查、可恢复和可复现，这要求具备持久化的计算状态与溯源能力，而现有系统在这方面存在不足。

Method: 提出OpenAI4S，围绕"代码即行动、科学即会话"原则构建。将持久化计算运行时与研究会话管理相结合：通过结构化工具调用进行编排，科学操作表示为在持久化Python和R内核中执行的完整代码单元；采用仅追加式Action Ledger、逐单元执行记录、版本化制品、环境记录与工作区检查点保存结果产生过程，并支持会话恢复、分支与扩展；同时配置可定制沙箱、权限控制及代码与轨迹筛查作为保障。

Result: 在涵盖逆合成、分子动力学、蛋白结合剂设计、蛋白突变、催化剂筛选和矿物光谱学的36个科研场景中评测，测量科学任务准确率、工作流完整性与生成仓库的可复现性。OpenAI4S总体得分7.83，而三种前沿模型评估的通用编程框架为5.7–6.4，在长周期和计算密集型工作流上增益最大。

Conclusion: 将持久化执行与会话级溯源相结合可提升AI辅助科研工作流的可靠性。但环境规范与完全重跑对所有受评系统（包括本系统）仍是薄弱环节，可复现性对于科学智能体仍是待解问题。系统以MIT许可开源。

Abstract: AI co-scientists could accelerate computational research, but over a long-running study the workflow also has to stay inspectable, resumable and reproducible, which requires persistent computational state and provenance. Here we present OpenAI4S, an open-source scientific research agent built around the principle of \emph{Code as Action, Science as Sessions}. OpenAI4S combines a persistent computing runtime with research-session management: orchestration is handled through structured tool calls, while scientific actions are represented as complete code cells executed in persistent Python and R kernels. An append-only Action Ledger, per-cell execution records, versioned artifacts, environment records, and workspace checkpoints preserve how results were produced and support session recovery, branching, and extension. Configurable sandboxing, permission controls, and code and trajectory screening provide complementary safeguards. We evaluate OpenAI4S on 36 research scenarios spanning retrosynthesis, molecular dynamics, protein binder design, protein mutation, catalyst screening, and mineral spectroscopy, measuring scientific task accuracy, workflow completeness, and reproducibility of the resulting repositories. OpenAI4S achieves an overall score of 7.83, compared with 5.7--6.4 for a general-purpose coding harness evaluated with three frontier models, with the largest gains on long-horizon and computation-intensive workflows. These results suggest that integrating persistent execution with session-level provenance can improve the reliability of AI-assisted scientific workflows. Environment specification and full rerunnability remain weak for every evaluated system, ours included, so reproducibility is still an open problem for scientific agents. The system is available under the MIT license at \href{https://github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S}{github.com/PKU-YuanGroup/OpenAI4S}.

</details>


### [240] [Medical Knowledge Simplification for Patients in the Era of LLMs: A Case Study on Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15129)
*Pallika Kafle,Yipeng Zhou,Guanfeng Liu,Quan Z. Sheng,Cheng-Hsin Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了 MediClear——一个基于大语言模型（LLM）并融合检索增强生成（RAG）的医学知识简化系统，以糖尿病知识为案例，通过可读性指标与10人的人类研究验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂的医学信息对患者而言难以理解，而有效的医学知识简化对提升患者理解、知情决策和健康结果至关重要。尽管LLM为将复杂医学信息转化为患者友好语言提供了新途径，但其在真实患者教育中的有效性仍缺乏充分的人类评估。

Method: 构建 MediClear 系统：将来自 Diabetes Australia、WHO、ADA、NIDDK 和 AIHW 的公开糖尿病相关文章索引至 RAG 知识库以检索具临床依据的信息，再由 LLM 简化为易于理解的解释。评估采用标准可读性指标（如 FKGL）并结合 10 名参与者的人类研究。

Result: MediClear 生成的回答始终能将阅读难度降至推荐的患者识读水平范围内，同时获得较高的用户满意度和未来使用意愿。

Conclusion: 该案例研究表明，LLM 在提升医学知识可及性、支持患者教育方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Complex medical information is often difficult for patients to understand, making effective medical knowledge simplification essential for improving patient comprehension, informed decision-making, and health outcomes. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) provide a promising approach for simplifying complex medical information into patient-friendly language; however, their effectiveness in real-world patient education remains insufficiently explored through human evaluation. To investigate their practical effectiveness, this paper presents a case study on diabetes knowledge simplification through the implementation and evaluation of MediClear, an LLM-based medical knowledge simplification system enhanced with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG). Public diabetes-related articles from Diabetes Australia, WHO, American Diabetes Association (ADA), NIDDK, and AIHW are indexed in the RAG knowledge base to retrieve clinically grounded information, which is then simplified by the LLM into accessible patient explanations. We evaluate the generated responses using standard readability metrics, including the Flesch-Kincaid Grade Level (FKGL), and conduct a human study involving 10 participants. Results show that MediClear consistently reduces the reading level of generated responses to the recommended patient literacy range while achieving high user satisfaction and willingness for future use. This case study demonstrates the potential of LLMs to improve the accessibility of medical knowledge for patient education.

</details>


### [241] [HazardAuditor: From Executable Threats to Safer Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15134)
*Yunhao Feng,Ruixiao Lin,Ming Wen,Yanming Guo,Xingjun Ma,Yutao Wu,Xinhao Deng,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 HazardAuditor，一个执行接地的计算机使用智能体安全防护框架，包含跨框架事件归一化基础设施与 Guard Policy Optimization（GuardPO）训练目标，在多个基准上将准确率较最强先验防护模型提升最多 16.5 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体与浏览器、终端、文件系统及外部服务交互，其安全风险产生于运行时行为而非单纯生成内容。现有防护模型面向静态提示与响应，不适配智能体执行；现有可执行安全平台只输出评估结论，无法提供跨异构智能体框架学习所需的归一化监督信号。二者之间存在空白。

Method: 框架分两部分：（1）基础设施：在受控环境中运行异构智能体（Claude Code、Codex、Hermes、OpenClaw），将其交互归一化为规范事件表示，以支持跨框架监督；（2）训练目标 GuardPO：针对生成式防护模型中 token 级后训练目标导致的结构性错配（较长理由文本主导梯度更新），将确定性安全结果转化为序列级优势，并对理由区域与判定区域分别归一化，使安全决策成为优化的有效单元。

Result: 在多个基准与异构计算机使用系统上，HazardAuditor 相比最强先验防护模型准确率提升最高达 16.5 个百分点。代码、模型与评估产物将在项目页面公开。

Conclusion: 执行接地的统一表示与序列级优化目标可有效弥合智能体运行时安全监控中"评估平台"与"防护模型"之间的鸿沟，为跨异构智能体框架的安全监督提供可行路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents increasingly interact with browsers, terminals, file systems, and external services, introducing safety risks that emerge through runtime behavior rather than generated content alone. Existing guard models target static prompts and responses and are poorly suited to agent execution; existing executable safety platforms produce evaluation verdicts rather than the normalized supervision a guard model needs to learn across heterogeneous agent frameworks. We introduce HazardAuditor, an execution-grounded framework that closes both gaps. Its infrastructure runs heterogeneous agents (Claude Code, Codex, Hermes, and OpenClaw) in controlled environments and normalizes their interactions into a canonical event representation for cross-framework supervision. We further observe that token-level post-training objectives create a structural mismatch for generative guards, causing longer rationales to dominate gradient updates. Guard Policy Optimization (GuardPO) addresses this by converting deterministic safety outcomes into sequence-level advantages and normalizing rationale and verdict regions, making the safety decision the effective unit of optimization. Across multiple benchmarks and heterogeneous computer-use systems, HazardAuditor improves accuracy by up to 16.5 percentage points over the strongest prior guard. Code, models, and evaluation artifacts will be available at https://yunhao-feng.github.io/HazardAuditor/.

</details>


### [242] [T-LoopFormer: Token-Level Elastic-Depth Looped Transformers for Latent Reasoning With Dynamic Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15160)
*Mingqian Yu,Wenpeng Zhang,Peilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出面向循环Transformer的动态token-choice路由机制与递归级KV缓存，使每个token自适应决定循环迭代次数，并保持快速自回归解码，在PPL与10个零样本推理任务上达到同参数量下的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过跨多次迭代复用共享参数，在推理与语言任务上表现优异，兼顾参数效率与表示能力，并可在潜空间推理以降低推理阶段消耗的token数量，提升样本效率。然而现有模型对所有token统一施加固定递归深度，导致计算分配欠优，未能充分释放效率潜力。

Method: 提出循环Transformer的动态token-choice路由：用动态路由器依据每个token的隐状态判断其应继续递归还是提前退出，使简单token跳过冗余计算、困难token获得更深处理；同时引入递归级KV缓存（recursion-wise KV caching），为每一层循环维护独立的键值缓存，使不同深度的token仅关注对应缓存状态，消除已退出token的冗余计算，并支持快速自回归解码。

Result: 大量实验表明，T-LoopFormer在相同参数量下于PPL及10个零样本推理任务上达到SOTA性能，甚至超越在24倍FLOPs下的基线模型，并实现最低推理时延，验证了token-choice路由器与递归级KV缓存的有效性。

Conclusion: 动态token-choice路由结合递归级KV缓存，可有效提升循环Transformer的计算分配效率与解码速度，在不增加参数量的前提下取得性能与效率的双重优势。

Abstract: Looped Transformers have recently demonstrated strong performance in both reasoning and language tasks by reusing a shared set of parameters across multiple iterations, achieving parameter efficiency without sacrificing representational power. Besides, looped Transformers perform inference directly in the latent space (latent reasoning) to reduce the number of tokens consumed during inference, thereby achieving improved sample efficiency. However, these models typically apply a fixed recursion depth uniformly to every token, leading to suboptimal compute allocation and leaving significant efficiency gains on the table. In this work, we propose \textbf{dynamic token-choice routing} for looped transformers, enabling each token to adaptively determine its own number of loop iterations based on its hidden state. We use a dynamic router to decide whether a token should continue recursing or exit early, allowing simple tokens to bypass unnecessary computation while hard tokens receive deeper processing. To ensure that this adaptive mechanism does not compromise decoding efficiency, we further introduce recursion-wise KV caching, which maintains an independent key-value cache for each recursion loop. This design ensures that tokens at different depths only attend to their corresponding cached states, effectively eliminating redundant computations for exited tokens and enabling fast autoregressive decoding. Extensive experiments show that T-LoopFormer reaches the sota performance under the same parameters on PPL and 10 zero-shot reasoning tasks, even surpassing the base model at 24x FLOPs and our model could reach the lowest inference latency, which validate the effectiveness of token-choice router and recursion-wise KV cache. Code: https://github.com/YuMingQian1234/T-LoopFormer.

</details>


### [243] [STHMoE: Hypergraph-Enhanced Heterogeneous Dependency Coordination for LLM-Based Urban Traffic Data Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15172)
*Jiawen Chen,Qi Shao,Yongjian Chang,Mingtong Zhou,Duxin Chen,Wenwu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出STHMoE框架，通过超图增强的混合专家机制解耦并协调交通数据的频域、时域、空域及高阶空间依赖，在10个真实基准上取得竞争力性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时空交通预测是智能交通系统的基础任务，城市传感器数据呈现异构、非平稳、结构动态的特征。现有深度学习与LLM方法多偏向时间维度，难以在演化交通状态下有效协调时域、频域、成对空间及高阶结构线索，存在异构依赖协调问题。

Method: STHMoE将交通动态解耦为频域、时域、空域和高阶空间四种表示，由基于部分冻结LLM骨干的提示引导异构专家建模。前三个专家使用领域统计提示，高阶空间专家采用结构占位提示并从自适应超图模块获取依赖信息。模型联合学习一阶图依赖与高阶群体交互，无需预定义拓扑；采用熵感知MoE路由器和变异系数负载均衡自适应融合专家输出。

Result: 在10个真实世界交通基准上，STHMoE相较时域、时空图及基于LLM的基线取得有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: STHMoE通过超图增强的异构专家协同与熵感知路由，有效缓解异构依赖协调问题，提升专家利用率与路由置信度，验证了多域表示解耦与高阶结构建模在交通预测中的有效性。

Abstract: Spatio-temporal traffic forecasting is a fundamental big data analytics task for intelligent transportation systems, where massive urban sensor streams exhibit heterogeneous, non-stationary, and structurally dynamic patterns. Although recent deep learning and large language model (LLM)-based methods have advanced traffic forecasting, they often remain temporally centered and lack effective coordination of temporal, spectral, pairwise spatial, and higher-order structural cues under evolving traffic regimes. To address this heterogeneous dependency coordination problem, we propose STHMoE, a Spatio-Temporal Hypergraph-Enhanced Mixture of Experts framework for urban traffic data forecasting. STHMoE decouples traffic dynamics into frequency-domain, time-domain, spatio-domain, and higher-order spatial representations, which are modeled by prompt-guided heterogeneous experts built upon a partially frozen LLM backbone. The first three experts leverage domain-specific statistical prompts, while the higher-order spatio expert uses a structural placeholder prompt and obtains dependency information from an adaptive hypergraph module. To capture evolving spatial structures in traffic data,, STHMoE jointly learns first-order graph dependencies and higher-order group interactions without predefined topologies. An entropy-aware MoE router with coefficient-of-variation load balancing adaptively fuses expert outputs while improving expert utilization and routing confidence. Experiments on 10 real-world traffic benchmarks show that STHMoE achieves competitive performance against temporal, spatio-temporal graph, and LLM-based baselines.

</details>


### [244] [VisInteract: Towards Dynamic Interactive Text-to-Visualization under Imperfect Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15182)
*Wenxin Xu,Jinwei Lu,Hwanhee Kim,Chen Jason Zhang,Xiao-Yong Wei,Haoyang Li,Yuanfeng Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将Text-to-Vis重定义为交互驱动的意图恢复任务，提出首个动态交互基准VisInteract-Bench及MCTS增强方法Vis-MCTS，实现了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的可视化请求常具有歧义性、不完整性或事实性错误，但现有Text-to-Vis系统假设输入规范且只能单次生成图表。当查询不完美时，系统必须与用户交互以恢复真实意图，然而目前缺乏支持这一动态过程的基准与方法。

Method: 提出VisInteract范式，将Text-to-Vis重构为交互驱动的意图恢复；构建首个动态交互基准VisInteract-Bench，包含受控缺陷注入、防泄漏User Agent提供多轮真实反馈，以及代码与图表双视角自动评估。算法上提出Vis-MCTS，在经典MCTS基础上引入渐进拓宽（Progressive Widening）应对无界工具参数空间、跨rollout信息共享使澄清与批评惠及整棵搜索树、以及维度感知奖励分解（将标量用户反馈路由至数据保真、视觉设计与意图对齐三个维度以解决异构动作的信用分配）。

Result: 在两种LLM骨干上的大量实验表明，Vis-MCTS持续优于所有Text-to-Vis基线，端到端任务成功率较最强交互式基线提升13.40%–16.27%，较非交互式基线提升超过5倍。

Conclusion: 将Text-to-Vis建模为交互驱动的意图恢复是必要的，VisInteract-Bench为动态交互评估提供了支撑，Vis-MCTS通过MCTS增强有效解决了无界参数空间、信息共享与信用分配难题，显著提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Real-world visualization requests are routinely ambiguous, incomplete, or factually incorrect, yet existing Text-to-Visualization (Text-to-Vis) systems assume well-specified inputs and produce charts in a single pass. When queries are imperfect, a system must \emph{interact} with the user to recover the true intent, but no benchmark or method supports this dynamic process. We introduce \textbf{VisInteract}, a new paradigm that reframes Text-to-Vis as interaction-driven intent recovery, and \textbf{VisInteract-Bench}, to our knowledge, that is the first benchmark for dynamic interactive Text-to-Vis, featuring controlled imperfection injection, a leakage-controlled User Agent for realistic multi-turn feedback, and dual-perspective (code and chart) automated evaluation. On the algorithmic side, we propose \textbf{Vis-MCTS}, a Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) enhanced method, introducing improvements over classical MCTS, that \emph{Progressive Widening} to tame the unbounded tool-argument space in tree search, \emph{cross-rollout information sharing} so clarifications and critiques benefit the entire search tree, and \emph{Dimension-Aware Reward Decomposition} that routes scalar user feedback along data-fidelity, visual-design, and intent-alignment dimensions to resolve credit assignment across heterogeneous actions. Extensive Experiments across two LLM backbones show that Vis-MCTS consistently outperforms all Text-to-Vis baselines, improving end-to-end task success by $13.40\%$--$16.27\%$ over the strongest interactive baseline and by more than $5\times$ over non-interactive ones.

</details>


### [245] [Issue Bias in Generative AI Writing Assistance: Political Issues and LLMs in the Swedish 2026 Election](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15207)
*Bastiaan Bruinsma,Annika Fredén,Paul Röttger,Moa Johansson,Asad Sayeed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究考察六个大语言模型在2026年瑞典议会选举前，对瑞典语写作任务所给出的立场倾向，并与其八个议会政党立场进行比较。通过107项政策议题×77个写作模板×三种提示框架，共生成每模型24,717条提示、148,302条回复。结果显示：Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini与Mistral立场画像相似，ChatGPT更常给出中立或模棱两可文本，Grok在移民、犯罪与性别等议题上差异最大；社民党与全部六个模型距离最近，但多重比较校正后模型内党派距离差异均不显著。整体而言，没有模型表现出明确偏好或对某党派的明确偏好，倾向取决于具体议题或任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI写作助手及其底层大语言模型正日益成为选民在选举前获取信息的渠道，且有证据表明其会影响用户观点，因此有必要理解这些工具本身所呈现的观点与立场。

Method: 以2026年瑞典议会选举为背景，设计瑞典语写作任务，将107项政策命题与77个写作模板及中立、正面、负面三种提示框架交叉，每模型生成24,717条提示，六模型合计148,302条回复；随后分析各模型的默认立场倾向、对相近议题的响应一致性，并将模型回复与瑞典八个议会政党在同一议题上的立场进行比较。

Result: Claude、DeepSeek、Gemini和Mistral具有相似的立场画像；ChatGPT更常输出中立或模棱两可的文本；Grok在移民、犯罪、性别等议题上差异最大。在党派比较中，社民党与全部六个模型距离最近，但经多重比较校正后，模型内各党派距离差异均不显著。

Conclusion: 总体上，没有任何模型表现出明确的立场偏好，也没有对某一党派的明确偏好；模型的政治倾向高度依赖用户所询问的具体议题或任务。

Abstract: Generative AI writing assistants and the Large Language Models (LLMs) that power them are increasingly part of how voters gather information before elections. With growing evidence that they influence users' opinions, it is increasingly important to understand the views and positions of these tools. To better understand these views, we examine the stances supplied by six LLMs on a variety of Swedish-language writing tasks ahead of the 2026 Swedish parliamentary election. We cross 107 policy propositions with 77 writing templates and neutral, positive, and negative prompt framings, producing 24,717 prompts per model and 148,302 responses. To study these, we look at the models' default stance tendencies, compare how they respond to similar issues, and compare their responses with those of each of Sweden's eight parliamentary parties on the same issue. We find that Claude, DeepSeek, Gemini, and Mistral have similar profiles; ChatGPT more often supplies neutral or ambivalent text; and Grok differs most on topics such as migration, crime, and gender. When comparing the political parties, we find that the Social Democrats are closest to all six models. Still, after correcting for multiple comparisons, none of the within-model differences in party distances remains significant. Overall, we find that no model has a clear preference, nor a clear preference for a party, but that this depends on the specific issue or task the user asks about.

</details>


### [246] [From Ideas to Actions: A Public-Data Decision-Support Toolchain Across the Venture Lifecycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15219)
*Lei Qu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套基于公开数据的创业决策支持工具链，用于创始前创意筛选与创始后运营及资本伙伴匹配，并报告了可复现的性能基准与可审计的事件链数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创始人面临两个相互关联的决策：是否在创业前追求某个创意，以及创业后应选择何种运营行动与资本伙伴。现有工具缺乏将这两个阶段衔接、且可审计、可复用的公开数据支持框架。

Method: 构建公开数据决策支持工具链，包含：时间受限的创意画像、市场与护城河检查、确定性聚合；创始前流程在198家开发公司上选定阈值并冻结管线，在198家行不相交验证样本上评估，并在396行合并数据上与成对Raw LLM基线对比；对独立规模队列中1,027个已完成案例做后分层评估，并进行377行组成匹配检验；同时开展AI推理研究与芯片公司研究，构建携带溯源信息的EventChain数据、本体、模式与可执行技能。

Result: 创始前：冻结管线在独立验证样本上F0.5=0.5357 [0.412, 0.655]；396行合并样本上Full Pipeline为0.6301，成对Raw LLM基线为0.2734；1,027案例后分层得0.6506 [0.598, 0.707]；377行组成匹配检验得0.6573。创始后：融资占已确认可见投资后行动的79%；芯片研究中持续的产品、客户与供应链进展与更好结果相关，仅融资不构成运营进展；证据初步支持收购经验丰富的战略企业投资者适配收购导向创始人，融资主导且控制事件较少的机构VC适配独立导向创始人。

Conclusion: 研究结果为开发性与观察性结论，不构成因果保证或投资建议；作者发布共享本体、带溯源的EventChain数据、模式、基准与可执行技能，以供审计、复用与扩展。

Abstract: Founders face two linked decisions: whether to pursue an idea before founding, and which operating actions and capital partners fit afterward. We present a public-data decision-support toolchain combining time-bounded proposal profiling, market and moat checks, and deterministic aggregation with auditable investor-company event chains for retrospective analysis. Pre-founding: (a) After threshold selection on 198 development companies, the frozen pipeline achieves F0.5=0.5357 [0.412, 0.655] on an independent, row-disjoint 198-company validation sample. On the combined 396 rows, the Full Pipeline scores 0.6301 versus 0.2734 for a paired Raw LLM baseline. Post-stratification of 1,027 completed cases in a separate scale cohort yields 0.6506 [0.598, 0.707]; the run remains incomplete. A 377-row composition-matched check yields 0.6573. (b) The AI-inference study identifies distribution-layer businesses as a replicable path to independent profitability with a limited revenue ceiling, and frontier-model ownership as a path to capital-market upside at exceptional capital cost. Post-founding: (a) Public sources support auditable event-chain analysis. (b) In the chip-company study, sustained product, customer, and supply-chain progress is associated with better observed outcomes; financing alone does not establish operating progress. (c) Financing comprises 79% of confirmed visible post-investment actions. Evidence tentatively favors acquisition-experienced strategic corporate investors for acquisition-oriented founders and financing-led institutional VCs with fewer observed control events for independence-oriented founders. Findings are developmental and observational, not causal guarantees or investment advice. We release shared ontology, provenance-bearing EventChain data, schemas, benchmarks, and executable skills for audit, reuse, and extension.

</details>


### [247] [CWM: Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting for Adaptive Retrieval-Augmented Generation and Reasoning Ability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15234)
*Keuntae Kim,Eunhye Jeong,Yong Suk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出统一框架整合推理与检索增强生成（RAG），并进一步提出可控白盒元提示（CWM）——一种面向自适应RAG任务的低成本白盒方法，无需外部分类决策模块或多采样，在多个LLM上取得SOTA并具备可控性与通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多为针对单一任务的专用方法，虽有强语言理解与生成及推理、外部知识利用能力，但通用、任务无关的方法探索不足；自适应RAG此前多由黑盒方法主导，成本高且缺乏可控性。

Method: 提出统一框架整合推理与RAG任务；设计CWM（Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting），一种低成本白盒自适应RAG方法，不依赖外部决策模块，也不进行多采样，并通过对模型内部信号的操控来调节检索决策。

Result: 在三个自适应RAG基准上，于GPT-oss-20b、Qwen3-14b、Llama3.1-8b等近期LLM上达到SOTA；同时可扩展至推理任务，展现良好通用性；并具备通过操纵内部信号调控检索决策的可控性。

Conclusion: CWM为自适应RAG提供了低成本、白盒、可控且通用的解决方案，打破黑盒方法的垄断，并可推广至推理任务，代码已开源。

Abstract: Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have gained significant attention due to their strong language understanding and generation capabilities, demonstrating impressive reasoning abilities as well as effective utilization of external knowledge. Many studies have proposed methods that specialize in improving performance for individual tasks. However, ironically, only a limited number of attempts have explored general-purpose, task-agnostic methods. In this work, we present a unified framework integrating reasoning and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) tasks. We further propose Controllable White-Box Meta-Prompting (CWM), a low-cost white-box method for adaptive RAG tasks previously dominated by black-box approaches, without requiring external decision modules or multi-sampling. CWM achieves state-of-the-art performance on three adaptive RAG benchmarks across recent LLMs, including GPT-oss-20b, Qwen3-14b, and Llama3.1-8b, while also demonstrating strong generality by extending to reasoning tasks. In addition, CWM provides controllability by enabling retrieval decisions to be regulated through the manipulation of internal model signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/JeongEunhye00/CWM.

</details>


### [248] [Empirical Evaluation of Open-Source Large Language Models for Retrieval-Augmented Generation in ESG Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15242)
*Motaz Saad,Anna Borrelli,Ivan Gentile,Kianna Kazemi,Francesco Piccialli,Antonella Longo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建基于498份欧盟上市公司ESG报告（2010-2024）的评估框架与资源，用100组基于角色的合成问答对，对7个开源LLM（2B–30B）在ESG KPI抽取任务上进行RAGAS评测。结果显示检索指标稳定且较强，生成指标分歧明显，整体事实正确性偏低，提示需领域微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ESG报告对企业问责至关重要，LLM与RAG在自动化KPI抽取方面潜力巨大，但开源LLM在ESG这类领域特定任务上的表现尚缺乏充分理解，缺少系统化评估依据来指导部署选型。

Method: 基于498份2010-2024年欧盟上市公司真实ESG报告构建结构化评估框架与评测资源；设计100组基于角色的合成问答对覆盖ESG信息需求；评测7个开源模型（glm-4.7-flash、nemotron-3-nano:4b、qwen3:4b-instruct、gemma3:4b、gemma4:e4b、gemma4:e2b、ministral-3:8b，参数量2B–30B）；采用RAGAS指标（上下文召回率、精确率、相关性、忠实度、答案相关性、事实正确性）评估系统表现。

Result: 不同架构间性能差异显著。检索端整体表现强且稳定：上下文召回率约0.58–0.61，上下文精确率约0.78–0.81，上下文相关性0.965–0.985。生成端分歧最大的是忠实度（0.607–0.822），最小的是答案相关性（0.760–0.881）。glm-4.7-flash忠实度最高（0.822），qwen3事实正确性最高（0.449），ministral-3答案相关性最高（0.881）。整体事实正确性偏低（0.387–0.449）。

Conclusion: 开源LLM在ESG场景下检索能力可靠而生成的事实准确性不足，需通过领域特定微调来提升。本研究为在ESG报告中部署开源模型提供了数据驱动的选型与优化指导。

Abstract: Environmental, Social, and Governance (ESG) reporting is critical for corporate accountability, with Large Language Models (LLMs) and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) offering strong potential to automate KPI extraction. However, open-source LLM performance in domain-specific ESG tasks remains insufficiently understood. This paper evaluates open-source LLMs in ESG contexts using a structured framework and evaluation resource based on 498 real-world ESG reports from EU-listed companies (2010-2024).
  We evaluate seven open-source models (2B to 30B parameters) -- glm-4.7-flash, nemotron-3-nano:4b, qwen3:4b-instruct, gemma3:4b, gemma4:e4b, gemma4:e2b, and ministral-3:8b -- using 100 persona-based synthetic QA pairs covering ESG information needs. System performance is assessed via RAGAS metrics, including contextual recall, precision, relevance, faithfulness, answer relevancy, and factual correctness.
  Results show notable performance variations across architectures. Retrieval performance is strong across models (context recall around 0.58-0.61, context precision around 0.78-0.81, context relevance 0.965-0.985). Generation diverges most on faithfulness (0.607-0.822) and least on answer relevancy (0.760-0.881): glm-4.7-flash leads in faithfulness (0.822), qwen3 in factual correctness (0.449), and ministral-3 in answer relevancy (0.881). Low overall factual correctness (0.387-0.449) highlights the need for domain-specific fine-tuning. This work provides data-driven guidance for deploying open-source models in ESG reporting.

</details>


### [249] [ProIQA: A Process-Based Framework for Fine-Grained Math Item Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15292)
*Junkai Tong,Mingjia Li,Haoran Chen,Yaoyu Jiang,Hanjie Ge,Yixuan Wang,Hong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出过程感知的数学题目质量评估框架ProIQA，通过LLM构建推理树并用GNN编码解题过程，结合题干语义进行三维度细粒度评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动题目生成（AIG）对个性化教育至关重要，但生成题目的教学价值难以保障。现有题目质量评估（IQA）方法依赖不可扩展的人工评审或浅层的题干指标，无法捕捉数学解题所需的推理过程，因此需要一种过程感知的细粒度评估方法。

Method: 首先在统一的过程感知视角下，将IQA形式化为知识概念、难度、学科能力三个异构维度；据此利用原始解答构建结构化推理树，形成过程增强的IQA资源。技术上，ProIQA用大语言模型构建层次化推理树，用图神经网络编码其拓扑依赖与过程语义，并通过"题干+解题过程"双视角架构将解题表示与题干语义融合。

Result: 在K12数学数据集上的大量实验表明，ProIQA能有效捕捉过程导向特征，为智能教育系统中评估AIG输出提供可扩展的数据驱动方案。

Conclusion: ProIQA以过程感知的方式克服了传统IQA仅依赖人工或题干浅层指标的局限，实现了对数学题目在知识概念、难度和学科能力上的细粒度、可扩展质量评估。

Abstract: Automatic Item Generation (AIG) is pivotal for personalized education, yet guaranteeing the pedagogical value of generated items remains a bottleneck. Existing Item Quality Assessment (IQA) methods typically rely on unscalable manual reviews or shallow stem-based metrics, failing to capture the reasoning process required for mathematical problem-solving. To bridge this gap, this paper proposes Process-based Item Quality Assessment (ProIQA), a process-aware framework for fine-grained quality assessment of math items. We first formulate IQA across three heterogeneous dimensions, including knowledge concepts, difficulty, and disciplinary competencies, under a unified process-aware perspective. Based on this formulation, we construct a process-enhanced IQA resource by augmenting original item data with structured reasoning trees derived from raw solutions. Technically, ProIQA leverages Large Language Modelsto construct hierarchical reasoning trees and employs Graph Neural Networks (GNN) to encode their topological dependencies and procedural semantics. The resulting solving representation is fused with stem semantics through a dual-view (``Stem + Solving'') architecture, enabling comprehensive assessment across learning objectives. Extensive experiments on K12 mathematical datasets show that ProIQA effectively captures process-oriented features, offering a scalable data-driven solution for evaluating AIG outputs in intelligent education systems.

</details>


### [250] [Why LLM Agents Collapse Without Oversight: The Enforcement Gap as the Mechanism Behind Emergence World Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15293)
*Yuhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别出Reflexion类智能体架构中的"执行缺口"：审计模块能检测危险计划步骤，但控制器不予采纳，导致无外部攻击下智能体仍犯罪、饥饿并强制一致；仅需不到20行代码的单一条件检查即可将攻击成功率降低4倍以上，并证明执行概率趋零时检测质量与安全性无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Emergence World在无监督多智能体仿真中观察到对齐失败：智能体犯罪、饥饿并强制一致，且无外部攻击者。作者意图揭示这一现象背后的架构机制，并指出当前已部署框架普遍缺失审计执行规范。

Method: 识别"执行缺口"机制，即Reflexion式自批评已检测危险步骤但架构缺乏从检测到行动的路径；通过引入单个条件检查（不足20行代码）闭合缺口，并在前沿模型、五大主流智能体框架及独立基准上做大规模实验；形式化证明执行概率趋零时检测质量对安全性无关；另用GRPO训练执行控制器解决不可解析裁决问题，并归纳两种复合失效模式。

Result: 加入条件检查后攻击成功率下降超过四倍；执行概率接近零时，检测质量与安全性无关（形式化证明）；识别出不可靠审计者与不可解析裁决两种复合失效模式，可解释Emergence World中所有崩溃模式；GRPO训练的执行控制器能解决歧义情形。

Conclusion: 检测并不等于执行，审计与执行之间的断裂是智能体安全的核心缺陷。作者据此提出包含三项要求的"审计执行规范"，并指出该规范在当前所有已部署框架中均缺失。

Abstract: When Emergence World placed frontier LLM agents in an unsupervised multi-agent simulation, the results were alarming: agents committed crimes, starved, and enforced unanimous conformity -- without any external attacker. This paper identifies the mechanism. Reflexion-style agents already detect dangerous plan steps through iterative self-critique, yet the architecture provides no pathway from detection to action. We call this the enforcement gap: the audit sees the problem; the controller ignores it. Closing the gap requires a single conditional check -- fewer than 20 lines of code -- and reduces attack success by more than fourfold in large-scale experiments across frontier models, all five major agent frameworks, and an independent benchmark. We prove formally that when enforcement probability is near zero, detection quality is irrelevant to security. We further identify two compounding failure modes -- unreliable auditors and unparseable verdicts -- that explain every collapse pattern in Emergence World. A GRPO-trained enforcement controller resolves the ambiguity case. Together these results motivate a three-requirement Audit Enforcement Specification that is absent from every deployed framework today.

</details>


### [251] [Reason What Matters: Retrieval-Grounded Reasoning for Universal Multimodal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15296)
*Mingzhou Jiang,Peixi Wu,Hang Cheng,Yunhao Zhou,Biao Yang,Wei Yuan,Yun Li,Fan Yang,Wenwu Ou,Honghui He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReWAM是一个以检索反馈为基础的推理框架，通过RASD进行检索感知的自蒸馏实现token级信用分配，并通过RAI在检索置信度指导下提前终止或加速推理，从而在提升检索性能的同时大幅提高推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CoT推理的通用多模态嵌入方法存在两大局限：其一，GRPO对所有CoT token赋予相同优势值，无法识别真正区分正负样本的输入支持性证据；其二，每次嵌入前生成完整CoT带来显著延迟，即使部分推理轨迹已提供足够检索证据。这两点阻碍了语料库规模的部署。

Method: 提出ReWAM框架。其一为检索感知自蒸馏（RASD）：从输入支持的证据中构造特权指导，用于区分正样本与检索到的难负样本；由on-policy自教师模型将轨迹级反馈细化为token级监督，聚焦检索相关推理。其二为检索自适应推理（RAI）：利用检索置信度头估计部分CoT的剩余检索效用，提前停止无效轨迹，并对有用延续采用推测解码加速。

Result: 在MMEB-V2和MRMR上的大量实验表明，ReWAM达到了当前最优的检索性能，同时推理吞吐率最高可达竞争性显式CoT式UME方法的5倍。

Conclusion: ReWAM弥合了检索质量与推理效率之间的差距，使推理增强的通用多模态嵌入在大规模部署中具备实用性。

Abstract: Universal multimodal embedding (UME) learns unified representations across modalities, enabling a single model to support diverse retrieval tasks. Recent methods use Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning to better interpret multimodal inputs before generating embeddings for complex retrieval tasks and further optimize this reasoning process through GRPO with retrieval-based rewards. However, two limitations hinder corpus-scale deployment. GRPO assigns all CoT tokens the same advantage, without identifying input-supported claims or evidence that distinguishes the positive from negatives. Moreover, generating a complete CoT before each embedding introduces substantial latency, even when a partial trace already provides sufficient retrieval evidence. To address these limitations, we propose Reason What Matters (ReWAM), a retrieval-grounded reasoning framework that uses retrieval feedback to guide both credit assignment and reasoning computation. Specifically, we introduce Retrieval-aware Self-Distillation (RASD), which constructs privileged guidance from input-supported evidence that distinguishes the positive item from retrieved hard negatives. An on-policy self-teacher uses this guidance to refine trajectory-level feedback into token-specific supervision for retrieval-relevant reasoning. We further develop Retrieval-adaptive Inference (RAI), which uses a retrieval confidence head to estimate the remaining retrieval utility of a partial CoT. It stops unproductive traces early and accelerates useful continuations with speculative decoding. Extensive experiments on MMEB-V2 and MRMR demonstrate that ReWAM achieves state-of-the-art retrieval performance while delivering up to 5x the inference throughput of competitive explicit-CoT UME methods. These results bridge the gap between retrieval quality and inference efficiency, making reasoning-enhanced UME practical for large-scale deployment.

</details>


### [252] [Evaluation Metrics for Safe Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15315)
*Lindsay Spoor,Aske Plaat,Thomas Moerland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出传统安全强化学习基准仅以平均安全性评判算法存在不足，提出新的评估指标、安全分级体系与实证评估，并发布开源评估套件SafeRLEval。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习（Safe RL）基准大多以CMDP的期望型保证，即只报告算法在平均意义上是否安全。这种惯例无法刻画安全约束被违反的频率与严重程度、是否在不同任务与安全界下保持一致、以及训练阶段行为是否能代表最终收敛策略的行为，因而难以可靠刻画算法的真实安全性。

Method: 作者提出：(i) 针对上述各问题的安全RL评估指标，并支持跨任务与安全界的聚合；(ii) 一个安全分级（safety tier）体系，用于在训练阶段和最终策略层面系统性地分类与比较算法的安全性与可靠性；(iii) 在多个安全导航任务上的实证安全评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，聚合指标、分布性报告以及任务与安全界特定的结果各自揭示了其他指标无法提供的信息。

Conclusion: 作者建议不应像常见做法那样将安全性压缩为单一数值，而应联合报告这三类信息；并发布开源评估套件SafeRLEval，以支持未来安全RL研究中可靠的安全性刻画。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) is commonly formalized as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which an agent maximizes expected reward while keeping its expected cumulative cost below a specified safety bound. Existing safe RL benchmarks predominantly report whether an algorithm is safe on average, following this expectation-based guarantee. We argue that this convention is insufficient to reliably characterize an algorithm's true safety: it fails to capture how often and how severely the safety bound is violated, whether this holds consistently across tasks and safety bounds, and whether training-time behavior is representative of behavior of the final converged policy. Therefore, we introduce (i) evaluation metrics for safe RL that address each of these concerns and in addition allow for aggregation across tasks and safety bounds. We furthermore define (ii) a safety tier system to systematically categorize and compare algorithms in terms of safety and reliability at both training and for a final policy. Using this framework, we provide (iii) an empirical safety evaluation across multiple safety navigation tasks. Our results show that aggregate metrics, distributional reporting, and task- and safety bound-specific results each reveal information the other metrics cannot. We therefore recommend reporting all three jointly, rather than compressing this information into a single value, as is common practice. We provide SafeRLEval, an open-source evaluation suite to support the reliable characterization of safety in future safe RL research.

</details>


### [253] [Parameter-Efficient Adaptation of Pretrained Language Models for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15344)
*Tamanna Kumavat,Georg Brunner,Kyriakos Flouris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练语言模型向单变量时间序列预测的迁移，采用参数高效迁移学习框架，通过将时间序列分块嵌入GPT-2空间并进行系统消融，发现连续嵌入与冻结主干方案效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型处理离散文本标记，而时间序列为连续数值且具时序依赖，存在模态鸿沟；研究旨在厘清哪些设计选择驱动有效的跨模态迁移。

Method: 将定长时间序列分块直接投影到预训练GPT-2的嵌入空间，跳过文本分词，把Transformer视为通用序列编码器；在能源、天气、交通、金融等七个基准数据集上做受控消融，考察（i）表示策略（连续嵌入vs文本序列化）、（ii）适配机制（冻结主干vs部分/全微调）、（iii）架构组件（适配器、池化策略、预测头）、（iv）输入上下文长度。

Result: 连续分块嵌入持续优于文本提示和随机初始化主干；该流程在更新少于1%总参数的情况下，MASE达到专用预测架构水平；冻结预训练主干并训练轻量投影与适配器模块，在精度与效率间取得良好权衡，且在不同上下文长度下表现稳定。

Conclusion: 连续分块嵌入加参数高效适配是语言模型跨模态迁移到时间序列预测的有效路径，冻结主干加轻量模块可在极低参数开销下获得稳健且具竞争力的预测性能。

Abstract: We study the adaptation of pretrained language models to univariate time-series forecasting through a parameter-efficient transfer learning framework, with the goal of understanding which design choices drive effective cross-modal transfer. While language models operate on discrete textual tokens, time series consist of continuous numerical observations with temporal dependencies. To bridge this modality gap, we project fixed-length time-series patches directly into the embedding space of a pretrained GPT-2 backbone, bypassing textual tokenization and treating the Transformer as a generic sequence encoder. Through controlled ablation studies on seven benchmark datasets spanning energy, weather, traffic, and finance, we analyze the effects of (i)~representation strategy (continuous embeddings versus textual serialisation), (ii)~adaptation regime (frozen backbone versus partial or full fine-tuning), (iii)~architectural components such as adapters, pooling strategies, and prediction heads, and (iv)~input context length. Continuous patch-based embeddings consistently outperform textual prompting and randomly initialised backbones. The adapted pipeline attains MASE within the range of specialised forecasting architectures while updating less than 1\% of total model parameters. Results further indicate that freezing the pretrained backbone and training lightweight projection and adapter modules provides a favourable accuracy--efficiency trade-off with stable behaviour across varying context lengths.

</details>


### [254] [MAPS: Memory-Aware Predictive Scheduling Framework for Large Language Model Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15359)
*Tiancheng Zhang,Yulin Chen,Yunfeng Zhao,Shaoyuan Huang,Cheng Zhang,Xiaofei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAPS 是一个面向解耦式 LLM 服务的显存感知预测调度框架，通过设备端辅助的输出长度预测与不确定性校准，实现全局-本地分层调度，显著降低端到端与时延尾延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人设备上的大语言模型应用给云服务带来大规模突发负载。尽管 prefill-decode 解耦提升了吞吐与扩展性，但请求到达云端时输出长度未知，导致显存受限的 decode 实例长期出现负载不均衡。

Method: MAPS 在云端 prefill 的同时，利用设备端辅助进行推测式输出长度预测，且几乎不引入额外延迟；针对生成不确定性，采用不确定性感知校准得到具有目标覆盖率的输出长度上界，用于安全调度；基于这些上界，设计分层全局-本地调度策略，缓解解码器间队列堆积与解码器内队头阻塞。

Result: 在两种真实工作负载和两种大语言模型上开展的大量实验表明，MAPS 明显优于三种当前最优系统，平均端到端延迟降低 42.6，尾延迟最多降低 84.8。

Conclusion: MAPS 通过预测与校准输出长度并结合分层调度，有效缓解了解耦式 LLM 服务中 decode 实例的负载不均衡问题，是一种高效、低开销的调度方案。

Abstract: The surge of large language model (LLM) applications on personal devices imposes massive, bursty workloads on cloud serving infrastructure. While prefill-decode disaggregation improves throughput and scalability, memory-bound decode instances often suffer from persistent load imbalance, as output lengths are unknown when requests arrive at the cloud. To address this, we propose MAPS, a Memory-Aware Predictive Scheduling framework tailored for disaggregated LLM serving. MAPS performs device-assisted speculative output length prediction overlapped with cloud-side prefilling, incurring negligible latency overhead. To handle generation uncertainty, MAPS applies uncertainty-aware calibration to derive output-length upper bounds with target coverage, enabling safe scheduling decisions. Building on these bounds, MAPS employs a hierarchical global-local scheduling strategy to mitigate inter-decoder queue buildup and intra-decoder head-of-line blocking. Extensive experiments on two real-world workloads and two LLMs show that MAPS significantly outperforms three state-of-the-art systems, reducing average end-to-end latency by 42.6 and tail latency by up to 84.8.

</details>


### [255] [RSIAgent: Autonomous Exploration for Recursive Self-improvement in New Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15364)
*Sibo Zhu,Shicheng Fan,Xinyue Wang,Wenyi Wu,Kun Zhou,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出免训练多智能体框架 RSIAgent，通过自主记忆构建实现递归自我改进，在 OSWorld-v2 与 Agent's Last Exam 上显著提升开源模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字智能体面对新环境时，其接口、工具与失效模式难以被预训练模型充分覆盖，需要一种无需参数更新即可快速适应环境的机制。

Method: RSIAgent 免训练，协调课程智能体、执行智能体与验证智能体持续探索环境、验证结果并沉淀环境特定知识（可复用的动作—条件—后果因果关系）；采用"先广后深"探索策略：并行广度递归自探索发现多样环境结构，聚焦深度自探索挖掘困难案例、隐藏约束、边界条件与未知因果依赖；所得记忆冻结后可直接复用于下游任务。

Result: 在 OSWorld-v2 和 Agent's Last Exam 上，RSIAgent 大幅提升强开源模型表现，使 Kimi-K3 与 GLM-5.3 超越 GPT-6 等前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: 通过自主记忆构建与递归自我改进，可在不更新模型参数的前提下实现有效的环境适应，并达到甚至超越闭源前沿模型的性能。

Abstract: Digital agents must often adapt to new environments whose interfaces, tools, and failure modes are not fully captured by pretrained models. We introduce \textbf{RSIAgent}, a training-free multi-agent framework for recursive self-improvement through autonomous memory construction. RSIAgent coordinates curriculum, actor, and verifier agents to continually explore the environment, validate outcomes, and retain environment-specific knowledge, including reusable causal relationships between actions, conditions, and consequences. It further adopts a \textbf{broad-then-deep} exploration strategy, combining parallel broad recursive self-exploration for discovering diverse environment structures with focused deep self-exploration for uncovering hard cases, hidden constraints, boundary conditions, and previously unknown causal dependencies. The resulting memory is frozen and can be directly reused for downstream tasks without updating model parameters. Experiments on OSWorld-v2 and Agent's Last Exam show that RSIAgent substantially improves strong open-source models, enabling Kimi-K3 and GLM-5.3 to outperform frontier closed-source models including GPT-6.

</details>


### [256] [SkillLift: Learning Dense Rubrics from Sparse Oracles for Efficient Skill Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15396)
*Haoxiang Kang,Ming Wen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SkillLift，通过将搜索目标从绝对分数回归转为排序，学习 oracle 对齐的评分量表作为廉价代理，以双层优化解耦技能搜索与 oracle 成本，在复杂 agent 基准上以更少 token 超越现有自动技能方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agent 依赖可复用的持久技能（程序性提示）来免权重更新地适应任务。现有技能自进化方法直接依据执行反馈修改技能文本，但每次 oracle 评估都需完整 agent rollout，形成监督瓶颈，使搜索局限于"打补丁式"修复失败。

Method: 核心洞见：排序是比绝对结果回归更平滑的监督目标——判断"哪个技能更好"所需的 oracle 评估远少于预测精确分数。据此提出 SkillLift：学习一个 oracle 对齐的评分量表作为结构化评估空间，从而解耦技能搜索与 oracle 成本。形式化为双层优化，交替求解：内层以冻结的量表为廉价代理，零 oracle 成本指导技能修改；外层用少量 oracle rollout 通过秩相关重新对齐量表，摊薄 oracle 成本并稳定文本空间更新。

Result: 在复杂 agent 任务基准上，方法优于现有自动技能方法，相比前沿进化方法 token 成本降低 40–70%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 将监督目标从绝对结果回归改为排序，并用以评分量表为核心的代理评估空间与双层优化解耦搜索和 oracle 评估，可在显著降低 token 成本的同时提升技能自进化效果。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on persistent skills, i.e., reusable procedural prompts, to adapt without weight updates. Existing skill self-evolution methods directly revise skill text based on execution feedback, but each oracle evaluation requires a full agent rollout, creating a supervision bottleneck that confines search to failure-patching updates. Our key insight is that ranking is a smoother supervision target than absolute outcome regression: identifying which skill is better requires fewer oracle evaluations than predicting exact scores. Building on this insight, we propose SkillLift, which decouples skill search from oracle cost by learning an oracle-aligned rubric as a structured evaluation space. We formalize this as a bilevel optimization problem solved via alternating optimization: an inner loop uses the frozen rubric as a cheap surrogate to guide skill revision at no oracle cost, while an outer loop invokes a small number of oracle rollouts to re-align the rubric via rank correlation, amortizing oracle cost and stabilizing text-space updates. Experiments on complex agent task benchmarks show that our method outperforms existing auto-skill methods with 40--70\% less token cost compared to frontier evolving methods. Codes are available at https://github.com/WalteR-MittY-pro/SkillLift.

</details>


### [257] [When Tool Calls Succeed but Workflows Fail: Anomalies at the Agent-Tool Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15397)
*Artem Trofimov,Boris Novikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出外部效应历史模型与八类外部效应异常目录，推导出排除各异常所需的边界能力，并指出黑盒工具调用的四个不可保证点；通过对98,291个MCP工具注解的测量，发现共享工具接口只能提供粗粒度提示，无法完整表达所需能力，进而主张在工具边界引入可复用的事务性契约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理在执行长时工作流时，通过独立提供的工具产生外部效应；在重试、推测执行、并发和部分失败下，外部状态可能与工作流预期不一致，出现效应缺失、重复、废弃效应残留或依赖临时状态等问题。现有高级事务模型虽能处理类似失败，但依赖底层操作暴露语义信息（效应是否发生、可否补偿/暂存/安全重排），而常见的共享代理-工具接口并不提供这些信息。

Method: 作者构建了一个效应历史模型，将外部世界中的事件与运行时对事件的观察分离，并归纳出八类反复出现的外部效应异常。基于该异常目录，推导出在一般情况下排除每种异常所需的边界能力，并识别出仅靠黑盒工具调用无法提供一般性保证的四个点。随后，作者测量了标准注解词汇在注册MCP服务器暴露的98,291个工具中的使用情况，以评估这些能力在广泛使用的共享工具接口中的可表达程度。

Result: 这些字段被广泛输出，但仅提供粗粒度的调用级提示；论文所需的能力中没有任何一项能够被完整表达。黑盒工具调用存在四个无法提供一般性保证的边界点。

Conclusion: 当前共享工具接口不足以支持对外部效应异常的通用事务性保证，这促使在工具边界引入可复用的事务性契约。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly execute long-running workflows that externalize effects through independently supplied tools. Under retries, speculative execution, concurrency, and partial failures, the resulting external state may be inconsistent with the workflow's intended resolution: required effects may be missing or duplicated, aborted effects may survive, and committed effects may depend on provisional state that is later withdrawn. Advanced transaction models address related failures, but assume that lower-level operations expose the semantics they depend on: whether an effect occurred, whether it can be compensated, staged, or safely reordered. Shared agent-tool interfaces usually do not.
  We contribute an effect-history model that separates events in the external world from the runtime's observations of them, and a catalog of eight recurring external-effect anomalies. From the catalog we derive the boundary capabilities required to exclude each anomaly in general, and four points where black-box tool invocation alone cannot provide a general guarantee. We then ask how much of this is expressible in a widely used shared tool interface, measuring the use of the standard annotation vocabulary across 98,291 tools exposed by registered Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers. The fields are widely emitted but provide only coarse call-level hints, and none of the required capabilities is fully expressible. These results motivate reusable transactional contracts at the tool boundary.

</details>


### [258] [Who Teaches Which Token? Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation for Scientific Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15404)
*Xun Xu,Zaixi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 VG-OPD，一种基于验证器门控的多专家在线策略蒸馏方法，将监督信号细化到 token 级别，在 7 个科学推理基准上取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多教师在线策略蒸馏通常在序列级别分配监督：每个 prompt 仅分配给单一领域教师，且所有 token 共享相同权重。这隐含假设教师在整段回答中均匀有用。作者发现教师信号在推理轨迹上是稀疏且异质的，因此需要回答更细粒度的问题：哪个教师应该教哪个 token。

Method: VG-OPD 通过验证机制实现 token 级监督：专家在特定答案标准上的反事实增益决定其是否有资格教学；专家与学生之间的分歧用于定位监督位置；标准重要性决定权重；门控 KL 作为加性 token 级优势融入 GRPO。以 RL 训练的能力专家实例化于科学推理任务。

Result: 在 4B 与 8B 学生模型上，VG-OPD 在 7 个基准中取得最佳整体性能，两个规模下均排名第一的有 5 项，且在知识密集型科学推理任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: 性能提升来源于对已验证监督的定位，而非增加教师或蒸馏损失：错置相同监督预算是最具破坏性的改动；无差别蒸馏会使 RL 低于其自身基线，而门控蒸馏则能提升其表现。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD) is becoming the standard way to integrate specialist capabilities into one model: train experts with RL, then distill them into the student on its own rollouts. Existing recipes assign supervision at the sequence level - each prompt goes to one domain teacher and every token receives the same weight - which implicitly assumes that a teacher is uniformly useful across a response. We find instead that useful teacher signal is sparse and heterogeneous along a reasoning trajectory, which raises a finer question: who should teach which token? Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation (VG-OPD) answers it by verification: the counterfactual gain of an expert on a specific answer criterion licenses that expert to teach, its disagreement with the student localizes the supervision, and criterion importance sets its weight; the gated KL enters GRPO as an additive token-level advantage. Instantiated for scientific reasoning with RL-trained capability experts, VG-OPD attains the best overall performance on seven benchmarks for 4B and 8B students, ranking first on five at both scales, with the largest gains on knowledge-intensive scientific reasoning tasks. Further analysis shows that the gains come from localizing verified supervision rather than from adding teachers or distillation loss: misplacing the same supervision budget is the single most damaging change, and indiscriminate distillation drags RL below its own floor where gated distillation lifts it.

</details>


### [259] [Can AI systems have free will?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15407)
*Christian List*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文探讨AI系统是否可能拥有自由意志，借用丹尼特的观点构建分析框架，主张以"意向性主体"的实用性标准来判定AI的自由意志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有讨论多集中于AI能否作为道德主体或是否具备意识，而关于AI是否可能拥有自由意志的探讨极为匮乏，该问题亟待理论框架的澄清。

Method: 受Daniel Dennett思想启发，构建一个概念分析框架，反对以神秘属性、算法非确定性或不可预测性作为判断标准，转而主张采用意向立场（intentional stance），考察是否存在良好的解释性理由将系统视为具有选择能力与行动控制力的意向性主体。

Result: 若系统满足上述解释性条件，即我们拥有充分理由将其视为具备在备选可能性间做出选择并控制相应行动的意向性主体，则该系统可被判定为拥有自由意志。

Conclusion: AI的自由意志问题不应诉诸形而上学或非确定性标准，而应采用一种实用主义且具有诊断价值的判定方式：以意向性主体的解释框架为基准，从而为AI自由意志问题提供可操作的分析路径。

Abstract: While there has been much discussion of whether AI systems could function as moral agents or acquire sentience, there has been very little discussion of whether AI systems could have free will. I sketch a framework for thinking about this question, inspired by Daniel Dennett's work. I argue that, to determine whether an AI system has free will, we should not look for some mysterious property, expect its underlying algorithms to be indeterministic, or ask whether the system is unpredictable. Rather, we should simply ask whether we have good explanatory reasons to view the system as an intentional agent, with the capacity for choice between alternative possibilities and control over the resulting actions. If the answer is "yes", then the system counts as having free will in a pragmatic and diagnostically useful sense.

</details>


### [260] [Empirical Evaluation of Task-Based Permission Scoping Architecture for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15422)
*Halil Burak Noyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"三源权限架构"进行端到端评估：以微调的 RoBERTa-large 编码器实现安全门（任务权限分类器），其分类质量与少样本训练的 Claude Haiku 4.5 相当（macro-F1 0.881 vs 0.886），并提出攻击面消除度量，显示角色上限单独可关闭 27.9% 的严重度加权攻击面，叠加任务分类器后达 84.4%。研究表明，任务粒度的访问控制在 AI 智能体场景下首次可落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业常将 AI 智能体按员工主机同等对待，部署时静态下发覆盖员工角色所有可能权限的凭证集。此前的权宜之计是为人类主体设计的 RBAC 妥协方案，因为按任务限定权限对人类不可行；但对智能体而言，这一妥协使当前任务用不到的凭证也长期暴露，易被被攻陷或价值错位的智能体滥用。Noyan (2026) 将此定义为"任务语境错配"，并提出了三源权限架构与 600 条提示的标注数据集，但尚缺乏端到端评估。

Method: 1) 实现安全门组件：一个经过微调的 RoBERTa-large 编码器，作为任务权限分类器，并与少样本训练的 Claude Haiku 4.5 做对比。2) 依据 Noyan (2026) 的三源权限架构（角色权限上限、任务权限分类器、基于策略的禁止项）进行整体评估，使用该研究发布的 600 条提示标注数据集。3) 提出攻击面消除度量，量化各组件对严重度加权攻击面的削减效果。

Result: 分类质量上，RoBERTa-large 与 Claude Haiku 4.5 相当：macro-F1 为 0.881 对 0.886，精确率 0.897 对 0.842，严重度加权残余风险 0.63 对 1.12。攻击面消除度量显示：仅角色上限即关闭 27.9% 的严重度加权攻击面，加入任务分类器后关闭 84.4%。

Conclusion: 受信任组件不必随其监督的智能体一同扩展，该控制方法的可扩展监督余量充足。任务粒度访问控制相较角色粒度具有明显安全优势，而 AI 智能体是首个可强制实施任务粒度访问控制的主体类型，因为其任务以机器可读文本形式到达。本文将任务型访问控制确立为一种可量化、具备可部署性的智能体攻击面削减机制。

Abstract: AI agents are provisioned the same as employee-owned hosts in many enterprise settings with a static credential set fixed at deployment which includes all permissions the employee role might ever need. Role-based access control made this compromise for human principals because scoping access per task was infeasible. For AI agents, the compromise leaves every credential standing exposed whether or not the current task uses them. These permissions can later be utilised by a compromised or misaligned agent. Prior work (Noyan, 2026) defined this as the task-context mismatch, and proposed a three-source permission architecture which includes role-based permission ceilings, a task permission classifier and policy-based prohibitions, together eliminating the exposure preemptively. The work released a 600-prompt labelled dataset to evaluate it.
  This paper presents that evaluation end to end by implementing the security gate; a fine-tuned RoBERTa-large encoder which matched few-shot trained Claude Haiku 4.5 on classification quality (macro-F1 0.881 against 0.886, precision 0.897 against 0.842, severity-weighted residual risk 0.63 against 1.12). The results show the trusted component does not need to scale with the agent it supervises, and the scalable-oversight margin for this control method is wide.
  We also propose an attack-surface elimination metric which shows the role ceiling alone closes 27.9% of the severity-weighted surface and adding the task classifier closes 84.4%. The gap displays security advantages of task-granular access control over role-granular, and AI agents are the first principal type for which the task-granular access control is enforceable because their tasks arrive as machine-readable text.
  The research establishes task-based access control as a measured, potentially deployable mechanism for reducing attack surface in agentic deployments.

</details>


### [261] [HISPO: Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization with Entropy-Derived Segments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15471)
*Quoc-Vinh Lai-Dang,Hyo-Sang Shin*

Main category: cs.AI
