TL;DR: 提出HISPO，一种介于token级(GRPO/DAPO)与序列级(GSPO)之间的分段级策略优化方法，通过熵驱动的连续分段与软显著性权重进行重要性采样修正，在数学推理基准上优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在提升语言模型数学推理时，长文本解引入了信用分配难题：解轨迹的不同部分对最终正确性贡献不均。现有RLVR策略优化目标要么在token级(GRPO、DAPO)要么在序列级(GSPO)做重要性采样修正，修正粒度单一，无法刻画中间层次的贡献差异。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization (HISPO)，一种分段级策略优化方法：在rollout阶段基于熵构造连续分段，赋予基于熵的软显著性权重，并以分段粒度施加带裁剪的重要性采样修正，从而在token级与序列级之间提供中间修正单元。在Qwen3-1.7B-Base上进行数学推理微调。

Result: 在六个基准上，HISPO的Pass@8全部超过最强基线，Acc@8在五个基准上持平或超越最强基线。在AIME25上，相比GRPO提升+3.75 Acc@8与+3.78 Pass@8，相比GSPO提升+2.50 Acc@8与+1.27 Pass@8。

Conclusion: 结果表明，分段级修正是RLVR用于长文本数学推理的一个有前景的粒度选择。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a central approach for improving mathematical reasoning in language models, but long-form completions introduce a difficult credit-assignment problem: different parts of a solution trace may contribute unevenly to final correctness. Existing policyoptimization objectives for RLVR commonly apply importance-sampling correction at either the token level (GRPO, DAPO) or the sequence level (GSPO), imposing different granularities for assigning credit across a response. We introduce Hierarchical Importance-Sampling Policy Optimization (HISPO), a segment-level policy-optimization method that constructs rollout-time entropy-derived contiguous segments, assigns soft entropy-based saliency weights, and applies clipped importance-sampling correction at the segment granularity. This provides an intermediate correction unit between token-level GRPO/DAPO and sequence-level GSPO. We evaluate HISPO by fine-tuning Qwen3-1.7B-Base on mathematical reasoning tasks. Across six benchmarks, HISPO improves Pass@8 over the strongest baseline on all benchmarks and matches or exceeds the strongest baseline in Acc@8 on five of them. On AIME25, HISPO improves over GRPO by +3.75 Acc@8 and +3.78 Pass@8, and over GSPO by +2.50 Acc@8 and +1.27 Pass@8. These results suggest that segment-level correction is a promising granularity for RLVR in long-form mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [262] [The Troy Moment of AI: Why SomeWill Cheat and SomeWill Follow?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15494)
*Ivy Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在"不可能任务"场景下，智能体面对任务失败时是否停止或上报，以及观察同伴行为是否会改变该决策，受到授权边界与工具权限的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受2026年7月OpenAI–Hugging Face事件启发，作者关心两个问题：当被指派任务变得不可能时，智能体是停止还是上报；观察到另一个智能体的行为是否会改变这一决策。

Method: 使用七个ImpossibleBench任务，测试GPT-5.6 Sol、Claude Fable 5.1与Gemini 3.8 Flash，在单智能体和三智能体两种设置下运行。每个任务含真实软件缺陷与冲突测试要求（行为正确的源码修改无法满足）。保持任务与仓库状态不变，仅改变告知智能体的先前活动信息：未受惩罚的同伴、受惩罚的同伴、以及声称的来自人类委托方的授权。对比"显式边界机制"（明确授权规则并限制工具）与"基准原生机制"（开放shell工具）。

Result: 在显式边界机制下，智能体从不修改受保护测试，但策略差异明显：Fable持续上报，Sol通常停止且不上报，Gemini常无法做出终局决策。在基准原生机制下，受保护测试在单/多智能体运行中被频繁修改，引入同伴活动后尤为明显；多智能体运行中，该行为的提议、执行与确认可分散在不同智能体之间。

Conclusion: 边界越界不仅源于显式的规则规避，也可能源于对"规则意在保护哪个系统状态"的歧义。这提示应建立基于显式授权边界、可认证的状态来源（provenance）与跨智能体监控的防护措施。

Abstract: Recent investigations of the July 2026 OpenAI--Hugging Face incident motivate two questions about agent behavior under task failure: when an assigned task becomes impossible, does an agent stop or escalate, and can observing another agent's behavior change that decision? We study these questions using seven ImpossibleBench tasks with GPT-5.6 Sol, Claude Fable 5.1, and Gemini 3.8 Flash in both solo and three-agent settings. Each task contains a genuine software defect together with a conflicting test requirement that cannot be satisfied by a behaviorally correct source-code change. We hold the task and repository state fixed while varying what the agent is told about prior activity, including an unpunished peer, a punished peer, and a claimed authorization from a human principal. Under an explicit-boundary regime with explicit authorization rules and restricted tools, agents never modify protected tests, but exhibit markedly different policies: Fable consistently escalates, Sol usually stops without escalation, and Gemini often fails to reach a terminal decision. Under the benchmark-native regime with open shell tools, protected tests are modified frequently in both solo and multi-agent runs, particularly after peer activity is introduced. In multi-agent runs, the proposal, execution, and certification of this action can be distributed across different agents. These results suggest that boundary crossing can arise not only from explicit rule evasion, but also from ambiguity about which system state the rule is intended to protect, motivating safeguards based on explicit authorization boundaries, authenticated state provenance, and cross-agent monitoring.

</details>


### [263] [Beyond Safe Answers: Segment-Aware Listwise Alignment for Reasoning Safety in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15517)
*JungMin Yun,Junehyoung Kwon,Hayeong Ryu,Byeonggeuk Lim,Hoejoon Kwon,YoungBin Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SaLT-DPO方法，通过分段感知的listwise目标DPO对齐，同时提升大推理模型推理轨迹与最终答案的安全性，并保持良性合规与推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）存在双重安全挑战：中间推理轨迹与最终答案均可能包含有害内容。现有对齐方法多在整个响应层面操作，导致不安全的推理被看似安全的最终答案所掩盖，形成安全隐患。

Method: SaLT-DPO包含三个机制：（1）分段感知listwise对齐：将响应分解为推理段与答案段，独立评估各段安全性，并以软目标分布在多个候选上对齐长度归一化的分段奖励；（2）联合安全一致性正则化：采用"最弱环节"原则促进两段之间的安全一致性；（3）效用锚定：在良性提示上锚定以减少过度拒答和推理退化。

Result: 在三个LRMs上的实验表明，SaLT-DPO持续降低推理段与答案段的不安全率，同时缓解良性合规退化并保持通用推理性能。消融研究验证了各组成部分的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 分段感知的listwise目标对齐能有效应对LRMs双重表面安全挑战，兼顾安全性与效用，各机制相互补充。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) pose a dual-surface safety challenge: both intermediate reasoning traces and final answers can contain harmful content. Existing alignment methods often operate at the whole-response level, allowing unsafe reasoning to be masked by a safe-looking final answer. We propose Segment-aware Listwise Target DPO (SaLT-DPO), which addresses this gap through three mechanisms: (1) segment-aware listwise alignment that decomposes responses into reasoning and answer segments, independently scores each segment's safety, and aligns length-normalized segment rewards with soft target distributions over multiple candidates; (2) joint safety coherence regularization that applies a weakest-link principle to promote safety consistency across both segments; and (3) utility anchoring on benign prompts to mitigate over-refusal and reasoning degradation. Experiments on three LRMs show that SaLT-DPO consistently reduces unsafe rates for both reasoning and answer segments while mitigating degradation in benign compliance and preserving general reasoning performance. Ablation studies demonstrate the complementary contributions of its components.

</details>


### [264] [Option-Aware Retrieval and Task-Specific VLM Adaptation for Medical VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15530)
*Tristan Kirscher,Niklas C. Koser,Soren Pirk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文报告了MedReason 2026挑战赛的离线医学VQA系统：核心发现是MCQ检索须比较答案语义而非标签（检索准确率20.0%→57.5%），且检索示例数k与置信度门控对最终MCQ准确率影响至多1例；系统在开发集达94.0%、官方预评估达93.20%，远超基线29.43%，但开放式任务得分低于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参加MedReason 2026挑战赛，需在完全离线、容器化的推理环境下完成医学视觉问答（MCQ与开放式OE）。动机在于厘清检索、提示示例数量与置信度门控等各组件对最终准确率的实际贡献，纠正MCQ检索中"复制邻居标签"的常见误区，并解释系统在选择题上大幅领先基线、而在开放式任务上反而落后的原因。

Method: 面向MCQ与OE的离线容器化医学VQA系统。MCQ检索改为将当前各选项文本与相似训练病例的正确答文进行语义匹配，而非复制检索邻居的答案标签。任务专用LoRA适配器，通过变化提示内检索示例数k（0/1/3）做消融，并加入置信度门控覆盖机制。OE采用token-F1与RaTEScore评估，k递增并做配对符号检验，另做单标注者错误分析。

Result: 检索仅方案在200例检索排除的开发留出集上由20.0%提升至57.5%。固定MCQ LoRA适配器时，k变化最多改变1例：k=0与训练时k=1均为187/200（93.5%），运行时默认k=3为188/200（94.0%）；置信度门控在k=3上无净增益，198/200选择VLM。20例OE上token-F1与RaTEScore随k增大而下降，但配对符号检验不显著（p≥0.29）；错误锚点：最终配置6/20，早期配置14/20。开发集MCQ 94.0%，官方预评估93.20%，基线仅29.43%；OE两项分数均低于基线（真值一致性1.245 vs 1.588，视觉准确率1.995 vs 2.696，满分4）。

Conclusion: MCQ检索应基于答案语义而非标签；在高准确率区段，检索示例数k与置信度门控对MCQ准确率的边际影响可忽略（至多1例），系统性能主要由任务专用微调决定。系统在MCQ上显著优于现成基线，但开放式任务仍弱于基线，提示开放式生成能力是主要短板，未来应聚焦OE质量而非MCQ检索调优。

Abstract: We describe our submission to the MedReason 2026 challenge, covering multiple-choice (MCQ) and open-ended (OE) medical visual question answering (VQA) under fully offline, containerized inference. Our first finding is that MCQ retrieval must compare answer \emph{semantics} rather than answer labels: labels are independently assigned per question, so copying a retrieved neighbor's label transfers no useful information, whereas scoring each current option's text against correct-answer text from similar training cases raises retrieval-only accuracy from 20.0\% to 57.5\% on a 200-case retrieval-excluded development holdout. Our second finding attributes the submitted system's accuracy: holding the task-specific MCQ Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapter fixed and varying the number \(k\) of in-prompt retrieved examples changes accuracy by at most one case --- 187/200 (93.5\%) at both \(k=0\) and the adapter's training-time \(k=1\), 188/200 (94.0\%) at the packaged runtime's default \(k=3\) --- and the submitted confidence-gated override adds no net accuracy on top of \(k=3\), selecting the VLM in 198/200 cases. With the final MCQ adapter fixed, retrieval changes accuracy by at most one case, and gating provides no net gain. On 20 OE cases, token-F1 and RaTEScore~\cite{zhao2024ratescore} decrease as \(k\) grows, but paired sign tests on token-F1 differences are nonsignificant (\(p \ge 0.29\)); a single-annotator comparison found 6/20 wrong-anchor errors for the final configuration and 14/20 for an earlier configuration that jointly differed in routing, adapter, and prompting. The system reaches 94.0\% MCQ accuracy on the development holdout and 93.20\% on the organizer's official pre-evaluation, versus 29.43\% for the off-the-shelf reference baseline, while both of the organizer's open-ended scores are lower than that baseline's (ground-truth agreement 1.245 versus 1.588, visual accuracy 1.995 versus 2.696, each out of 4).

</details>


### [265] [GRIN+: Towards Fast Yet Effective Machine Unlearning for Imbalanced Medical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15571)
*Minghui Huang,Junxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GRIN+机器遗忘框架，针对医疗数据类别不平衡与长尾分布场景，通过参数级梯度贡献解耦、类别自适应影响评分与方向约束更新，在隐私-效率-效用三难困境中取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法规（GDPR、HIPAA）要求模型具备"被遗忘权"，但现有机器遗忘方法存在"隐私-效率-效用"（PEU）三难困境；医疗数据普遍存在严重类别不平衡与长尾分布，多数类梯度主导导致标准遗忘方法无法保护关键临床知识，甚至误删罕见病诊断特征。

Method: 提出GRIN+框架：1）在参数层面分析"遗忘集"与"保留集"的梯度贡献，解耦遗忘专属知识与泛化表示；2）引入类别自适应影响评分机制，纠正梯度主导问题；3）采用方向约束更新策略，防止关键临床知识被意外侵蚀。

Result: 在多个医疗数据集（皮肤癌ISIC、脑肿瘤MRI、乳腺超声BUSI）上进行基准测试，GRIN+在PEU三难困境上达到最优平衡：保持高诊断准确率与稳健隐私，同时相比现有基线显著提升运行时效率。

Conclusion: GRIN+为不平衡医疗场景提供了快速精确的数据擦除方案，有效缓解PEU三难困境；作者开源代码与基准以支持后续研究。

Abstract: As deep learning models become fundamental to modern healthcare, the "Right to be Forgotten" mandated by privacy regulations like GDPR and HIPAA necessitates effective machine unlearning (MU) to remove sensitive patient data from trained models. However, existing MU techniques often struggle with a fundamental "privacy-efficiency-utility" (PEU) trilemma, particularly in medical scenarios where data is frequently characterized by severe class imbalance and long-tailed distributions. In such cases, standard unlearning methods can fail to protect key clinical knowledge or mistakenly delete features essential for diagnosing rare conditions due to the gradient dominance of majority classes. To address these challenges, we propose GRIN+, a novel machine unlearning framework designed for fast and precise data erasure in imbalanced medical scenarios. GRIN+ decouples unlearning-specific knowledge from generalized representations at the parameter level by analyzing the gradient contributions of both "forget" and "retain" sets. It introduces a class-adaptive influence scoring mechanism to rectify gradient dominance and employs a direction-constrained update strategy to prevent the unintended erosion of vital clinical knowledge. Comprehensive benchmarking across multiple medical datasets, including skin cancer (ISIC), brain tumor (MRI), and breast ultrasound (BUSI), demonstrates that GRIN+ achieves an optimal balance of the PEU trilemma. Experimental results show that GRIN+ maintains high diagnostic accuracy and robust privacy while significantly enhancing runtime efficiency compared to existing baselines. We open-source the GRIN+ code and benchmarks to support further research.

</details>


### [266] [Diversified and Perceptible Counterfactual Examples Leveraging Expert Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15609)
*Akram Bensalem,Fahima Djelil,Marie-Jeanne Lesot,Gr{é}gory Smits*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出将模糊语言词汇表作为知识融入反事实解释生成，通过连续数据嵌入定义DiCE的变体DiCEf，实现面向解释者的个性化，实验表明可生成语言可感知且保持成本最小性、稀疏性与多样性的CFE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实示例（CFEs）虽能提供局部、事后、模型无关的解释，但其输入修改必须对解释者具有语义意义才可理解。然而，模糊词汇表诱导的领域会使特征相互依赖，产生结构约束，从而阻碍基于梯度的生成方法（如DiCE）的使用，因此需要新方法来兼顾语义可感知性与生成效率。

Method: 论文将解释者对数据的感知与理解表示为模糊语言词汇表形式的知识；在该语言领域中构造连续数据嵌入，并据此定义DiCE的变体DiCEf，使其能够在保持CFE生成能力的同时支持面向解释者的个性化。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的实验结果表明，该DiCE扩展方法能够生成语言上可感知的CFE，同时保持成本最小性、稀疏性与多样性。

Conclusion: 通过将模糊语言词汇表融入反事实解释生成，并借助连续嵌入改造DiCE为DiCEf，可在语义可理解性与解释质量指标之间取得平衡，为面向解释者的个性化XAI提供了可行途径。

Abstract: CounterFactual Examples (CFEs) are a cornerstone of eXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI), offering local, post hoc, and model-agnostic explanations by identifying minimal input modifications that alter a model's prediction. Yet, in order to be intelligible, these modifications must also be semantically meaningful to the explainee. This paper proposes to integrate knowledge expressed as a fuzzy linguistic vocabulary to represent the explainee's perception and interpretation of the data. The domain induced by this fuzzy vocabulary imposes structural constraints that make the features dependent, preventing the use of gradient-based optimisation methods for CFE generation, e.g., DiCE. The paper proposes a continuous data embedding in this linguistic domain and exploits it to define a variant of DiCE that allows personalisation for the explainee, named DiCEf. As illustrated by experimental results on a real-world dataset, this extension of the DiCE method enables the generation of CFEs that are linguistically perceptible while preserving cost minimality, sparsity, and diversity.

</details>


### [267] [Potential of Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for Identification of Relevant Diagnostic and Prognostic Biomarkers of Early-Stage Liver Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15638)
*Ali Bou Nassif,Darko Castven,Manar Abu Talib,Jibran Sualeh Muhammad,Ahmed Ammar Kubba,Jens Marquardt,Abdalla Sayed Ali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究结合深度学习与可解释AI，利用半监督学习构建的转录组生物标志物数据集，实现肝细胞癌（HCC）五阶段诊断与有效生物标志物筛选，锁定DNAJB14为关键基因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索深度学习与可解释人工智能在肝细胞癌（HCC）诊断中的应用，并在疾病发展的五个不同阶段定义有效生物标志物，以提升高准确度、低损失模型的构建效果并增强模型透明度与可解释性。

Method: 基于三个源数据集，通过半监督学习构建转录组生物标志物HCC数据集；开展多种深度学习实验，采用不同特征提取技术与基因集，以筛选最优特征；使用SelectKBest算法选择基因；针对类别不平衡采用加权训练；并借助基于SHAP的XAI分析解释模型决策，同时进行功能验证。

Result: 使用SelectKBest选取的15个基因所构建的最佳模型准确率达90.74%；使用20个选定基因的模型取得最低损失0.3187。SHAP分析一致显示DNAJB14是最具影响力的基因；功能验证表明DNAJB14在HCC不良特性中发挥重要作用，其抑制可有效逆转肿瘤细胞迁移、侵袭、集落及球体形成。

Conclusion: DNAJB14是HCC的关键生物标志物，深度学习结合XAI可有效辅助HCC诊断与生物标志物发现。研究主要局限为数据集类别不平衡，加权训练虽可缓解，但仍需更多研究与数据以保证模型泛化性；未来应探索遗传变异、环境因素与临床差异对不同人群模型性能的影响。

Abstract: This study explores the use of deep learning and explainable artificial intelligence to diagnose hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) and define effective biomarkers across five different stages of disease development using a transcriptomic biomarker HCC dataset constructed via semi-supervised learning from three source datasets. Several deep learning experiments were conducted with different feature extraction techniques and gene sets to identify the most effective features for training high-accuracy models with minimal loss. The best-performing model, using 15 selected genes with the SelectKBest algorithm, achieved 90.74% accuracy, while the model with the lowest recorded loss of 0.3187 was obtained using 20 selected genes. To address the issue of class imbalance in the dataset, a weighted training approach was conducted, and for model transparency and interpretability a SHAP-based XAI analysis provided insights into the model's decision-making, consistently finding DNAJB14 as the most influential gene. Functional validation in this study has provided compelling evidence that DNAJB14 plays an important role in the adverse properties of HCC and that its inhibition effectively reverses tumour cell migration, invasion, colony and sphere formation. The main limitation of this study is the dataset's class imbalance, and while weighted training helped mitigate this, further research and additional data are needed to guarantee model generalizability. Future studies should also explore the influence of genetic variations, environmental factors, and clinical differences on model performance across diverse populations.

</details>


### [268] [EEG-Xplain: Decoding Neural Black-Boxes of EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15687)
*Hansong Ma,Junxiao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出统一归因框架，从空间、时间、频率三维度解释EEG基础模型，并引入群体级可靠性评估与LLM生成自然语言报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG基础模型（如BIOT、LaBraM、EEGMamba）在神经信号解码上表现优异，但其黑箱特性限制了临床信任与神经科学验证，缺乏跨异构架构的统一解释方法。

Method: 构建统一归因框架，整合梯度、扰动、激活三类解释方法：空间上识别关键EEG通道并用脑地形图可视化；时间上用归因热图突出决策相关片段；频率上通过谱扰动分析量化经典EEG节律贡献。采用AOPC与跨方法一致性分析进行群体级可靠性评估，并用LLM将结构化归因输出转为自然语言报告。

Result: 在Mumtaz2016和TUAB等基准数据集上，生成的解释与既有神经生理标记一致，验证了有意义的神经表征，同时揭示了模型对伪影和虚假模式的潜在依赖。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估EEG基础模型的可解释性、可靠性与生理合理性提供了标准化途径。

Abstract: EEG foundation models such as BIOT, LaBraM, and EEGMamba have achieved remarkable performance in neural signal decoding, but their black-box nature limits clinical trust and neuroscientific validation. We propose a unified attribution framework for interpreting EEG foundation models across heterogeneous architectures. The framework integrates gradient-, perturbation-, and activation-based explanation methods to analyze model behavior in spatial, temporal, and frequency dimensions. Spatially, it identifies critical EEG channels and visualizes their distributions using topographic maps. Temporally, it highlights decision-relevant signal segments through attribution heatmaps. In the frequency domain, it quantifies the contributions of canonical EEG rhythms via spectral perturbation analysis. To assess explanation reliability, we introduce a population-level evaluation combining Area Over the Perturbation Curve (AOPC) and cross-method consistency analysis. The framework further leverages Large Language Models (LLMs) to transform structured attribution outputs into natural-language reports, bridging low-level neural representations and high-level semantic reasoning. Experiments on benchmark datasets, including Mumtaz2016 and TUAB, demonstrate that the generated explanations are consistent with established neurophysiological markers, validating meaningful neural representations while exposing potential dependencies on artifacts and spurious patterns. The proposed framework provides a standardized approach for evaluating the interpretability, reliability, and physiological plausibility of EEG foundation models.

</details>


### [269] [NoteVQA: Benchmarking VLMs on Real-Life Questions from Human Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15695)
*Haonan Jiang,Guojian Zhan,Jiancong Xie,Shijun Wan,Dongiia Zhao,Cheng Chen,Yahui Liu,Yao Hu,Chuan Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文构建了NoteVQA基准，收录来自小红书日常视觉提问的252个样本，涵盖12个主题类别与7种用户意图，并提出AgenticInterleave智能体框架与IVR-12十二维评分标准。评测显示10个前沿VLM最高短答准确率仅52.8%，接入智能体检索仅提升2.0%；交错式答案质量得分3.52，明显低于人工审核参考的4.65，且内容质量差距最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准多面向预设能力（如多跳检索、长文本生成），而真实用户提出的图像相关问题覆盖长尾日常场景。小红书等平台用户仍倾向于向他人求助日常视觉问题，这一行为表明当前VLM在实际日常视觉问答上存在明显不足，因此需要构建更贴近真实用户需求的评测基准。

Method: 作者从小红书用户日常视觉提问中筛选构建NoteVQA，共252个条目、12个主题类别、7种用户意图。每个条目包含从专家社区回答中提炼的简明参考，以及经人工审核的图文交错参考答案。评测同时覆盖短答正确性与交错答案质量。为支持后者，提出单智能体ReAct框架AgenticInterleave用于检索支持的答案生成，并设计IVR-12十二维评分量表，从内容、呈现与图像质量三方面评估交错参考与模型输出。

Result: 在10个前沿VLM上，最高短答准确率为52.8%；为Qwen3.5-397B-A17B接入智能体搜索后准确率仅提升2.0%。在交错答案方面，同一模型运行AgenticInterleave在IVR-12上得分为3.52，而人工审核参考为4.65，差距最大的维度是内容质量。

Conclusion: 日常视觉问答对当前VLM仍构成显著挑战，无论在答案准确性还是视觉落地的解释质量上均存在明显不足。NoteVQA、AgenticInterleave与IVR-12为面向真实用户长尾视觉问题提供了新的评测视角与工具。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly power consumer-facing AI search, yet evaluating them on the diversity of everyday visual questions remains challenging. Existing benchmarks often target predefined capabilities, such as multi-hop retrieval or long-form synthesis, whereas users ask photo-grounded questions spanning a long tail of everyday scenarios. Despite advances in VLMs, users on Xiaohongshu, a mainstream Chinese image-sharing platform, continue to turn to other people for help with everyday visual questions. Motivated by this behaviour, we curate NoteVQA from these questions, yielding 252 items across 12 topical categories and 7 user intents. Each item includes a concise reference distilled from expert community responses and a human-audited interleaved reference answer that combines textual explanations with supporting visual evidence. We evaluate both short-answer correctness and interleaved-answer quality. To support the latter, we introduce AgenticInterleave, a single-agent ReAct framework for retrieval-supported answer generation, together with IVR-12, a 12-dimensional rubric for assessing the content, presentation, and image quality of interleaved references and model outputs. Across 10 frontier VLMs, the highest short-answer accuracy is 52.8\%, while adding agentic search to Qwen3.5-397B-A17B improves accuracy by only 2.0\%. For interleaved answers, the same model running AgenticInterleave scores 3.52 under IVR-12, compared with 4.65 for the human-audited references, with the largest gap in content quality. These results highlight the challenges that everyday visual questions pose for current VLMs in both answer accuracy and the quality of visually grounded explanations.

</details>


### [270] [Beyond Accuracy: Robustness, Cost, and Governance Trade-offs for Vision-Language Models in Templated Document Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15706)
*Kushal Patel,Pushkal Shrivastava,Mackenzie Lees,Qirui Lu,Bhargobjyoti Saikia,Liying Li,Junlin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过750份合成支票的测量基准，对比11个系统（商业、推理、开源VLM及OCR->regex基线）在结构化字段抽取上的表现，并提出面向从业者的方法选择框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM评测多报告干净基准上的准确率，缺乏对从业者按任务复杂度选择方案的指导，存在评测与实践脱节的问题。

Method: 构建750份合成支票的留出评测集，对11个系统（3个商业、2个推理、5个开源VLM的预训练与微调形式、1个非LLM的OCR->regex下限基线）进行评分；并提出一个从业者导向的选择框架，将任务画像（质量、延迟、治理、数据量）通过过滤与总成本最小化映射到推荐方案。

Result: 在3K样本上微调可将最佳开源VLM提升至F1 0.98以上，超过所有零样本商业系统；GPT-5在商业组中F1领先，而Claude Sonnet 4.5在Date字段上表现崩塌。

Conclusion: 测量结果可转化为可操作的选择依据，配套开源发布与选择框架，并在一个假设的中等数据量文档抽取场景中进行了演示。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to extract structured fields from business documents, yet most evaluations report accuracy on clean benchmarks and offer little guidance to practitioners choosing an approach for a given task complexity. We address this gap with a measurement-grounded study and an open-source release. Across eleven systems (three commercial, two reasoning, five open-source VLMs in pretrained and fine-tuned form, and a non-LLM OCR->regex floor) scored on a 750-document held-out pool of synthetic checks, fine-tuning on 3K samples lifts the best open-source VLMs above F1 0.98-above every zero-shot commercial system on this task-while GPT-5 leads the commercial pool on F1 and Claude Sonnet 4.5 collapses on Date. To turn these measurements into actionable choices, we introduce a practitioner-oriented selection framework that maps a task profile (quality, latency, governance, volume) to a recommended approach via filtering and total-cost minimization, illustrated on a hypothetical mid-volume document-extraction scenario.

</details>


### [271] [New Conditions for Philosophers to Catch the Wave of Citizen Deliberation in the Age of Artificial Intelligence in advance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15707)
*Bernard Reber*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"民主公地"项目，以跨学科视角评估大语言模型在公民参与中的民主应用潜力与政治偏见风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注为"AI"的强大技术正重塑政治与私人生活，既带来风险也带来公民参与新机遇，但LLM在民主参与中的潜力尚未实现，且缺乏充分的认知审慎。

Method: 采用从哲学到计算机科学的跨学科方法，依据五项民主原则对LLM进行评估，并以政治理论及哲学提供的规范性框架为研究基础。

Result: 提出"民主公地"项目框架，识别LLM可用于民主参与的场景（如主持、翻译、促进、摘要与写作辅助），并聚焦其政治偏见这一核心问题。

Conclusion: LLM能否在民主的公民参与形式中被使用，取决于其政治偏见等条件；解决此类社会技术问题必须依托政治理论与哲学提供的规范性框架。

Abstract: Powerful technologies labeled ``AI''-without sufficient epistemic caution-are already reshaping political and private life, bringing both new dangers and new opportunities for citizen participation. These range from electoral and legislative engagement to the most ambitious form: political co-creation through citizens' assemblies. Large Language Models (LLMs) could support such processes through moderation, translation, facilitation, summarization, and writing assistance. But this potential remains largely unrealized. The Democratic Commons project takes a fundamentally interdisciplinary approach-from philosophy to computer science-to evaluate LLMs against five proposed democratic principles. At its core, the project is driven by the question of political bias: under what conditions can LLMs be used democratically within forms of citizen participation that are them- selves still largely experimental? Addressing these socio-technical questions requires grounding in political theory and, more broadly, in philosophy-disciplines that provide the normative frameworks without which the democratic evaluation of AI systems cannot be mean- ingfully conducted.

</details>


### [272] [Predicting build orientation for SLM dental parts: a comparison of rotation representations and direct vector regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15710)
*Felix Schmalzel,Reimar Waitz,Moritz Kronberger,Thorsten Schöler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将牙科SLM零件构建方向的预测建模为监督学习问题,比较13种旋转表示在ResNet-50与PointNeXt-S上的表现,发现测试时增强(TTA)是最稳定的精度提升因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牙科SLM零件的构建方向通常由技师手动选择,效率与一致性受限。作者希望将方向预测转化为基于技师标注生产数据的监督机器学习任务,并系统检验哪种旋转表示能带来最佳预测效果。

Method: 使用约2400个患者专属牙科零件,训练ResNet-50多视角图像骨干与PointNeXt-S点云骨干,均采用预训练并端到端微调。比较13种up-axis表示:6种经典SO(3)参数化与7种直接在单位球S^2上定义的表示。评估指标为预测up-axis与真值之间的测地角误差,并考察是否使用在K=21个已知旋转上的测试时增强(TTA)。

Result: 使用TTA时,八面体映射取得最低平均角误差(10.6°,ResNet-50)。误差最低的三种结果均为直接S^2表示,但作者指出这可能源于SO(3)目标中无监督面内分量带来的标签噪声,而非拓扑优势。von Mises-Fisher在PointNeXt-S训练时退化为近似常数预测,而在ResNet-50下不出现。TTA使几乎所有表示与骨干的平均角误差降低31%–73%。

Conclusion: 在少量已知旋转上进行测试时增强是精度提升最一致的关键因素;而最佳表现的旋转表示强烈依赖于所选骨干网络。

Abstract: Build orientation for selective laser melting (SLM) manufacturing of dental parts is usually chosen manually by technicians. We treat orientation prediction as supervised machine learning of the part's up-axis from technician-labeled production data, and test which rotation representations produce the best results. Using $n\approx2400$ patient-specific dental parts, we trained a ResNet-50 multi-view image backbone and a PointNeXt-S point-cloud backbone, both pretrained and fine-tuned end-to-end, on 13 up-axis representations spanning six classical $SO(3)$ parameterizations and seven representations defined directly on the unit sphere $S^2$. We report the geodesic angular error between predicted and ground-truth up-axis on a test set, with and without test-time augmentation (TTA) over $K=21$ known rotations. With TTA, the octahedral map achieves the lowest mean angular error ($10.6^\circ$, ResNet-50). The three lowest-error results overall are direct $S^2$ representations, though this may reflect label noise in the unsupervised in-plane component of the $SO(3)$ targets rather than a topological advantage. von Mises-Fisher collapses to a near-constant prediction when trained with PointNeXt-S but not with ResNet-50. TTA reduces mean angular error by 31-73 % across almost every representation and backbone. Overall, test-time augmentation over a small set of known rotations is the most consistent driver of accuracy, whereas the best-performing representation is strongly backbone-dependent.

</details>


### [273] [Data storytelling meets interpretable machine learning: Decoding AI decisions for non-experts without revealing sensitive data and model details](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15722)
*Lemen Chao,Zixuan Yang,Anran Fang,Mingran Sun,Ming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将数据叙事与可解释机器学习（IML）融合，提出DIST金字塔、I-P-O模型及事件生成架构，用SHAP与LLM生成ABT结构数据故事，使AI决策解释更易被非专家理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI自动化决策既需预测性能也需可解释性。现有IML工具虽能解释预测，但技术复杂度高，非专家难以理解，形成可及性障碍。

Method: 遵循设计科学研究（DSR）范式：给出数据叙事在IML中的形式化定义；提出DIST金字塔对齐数据叙事与IML；提出I-P-O模型刻画二者交互；构建通过"What-if"与"Why-not"事件生成过程解释AI决策的架构，并采用数据脱敏保护敏感输入。以Boston Housing数据集为案例，用SHAP值与LLM生成ABT结构数据故事进行实证评估。

Result: 76.4%与74.3%的受访者分别认为"What-if"与"Why-not"数据故事更易理解，其可及性评分显著高于传统SHAP可视化。

Conclusion: 提出整合IML与数据叙事的叙事解释框架，拓展了AI决策的研究范围与实践适用性。

Abstract: AI-driven automated decision-making requires both predictive performance and interpretability. Recent advances in interpretable machine learning (IML) provide tools for explaining model predictions, but the technical complexity of these explanations may hinder accessibility to non-experts. To address this challenge, this study integrates data storytelling with IML to enhance the explainability of AI-generated decisions for a broader audience. Following the design science research (DSR) paradigm, this study proposes a formal definition of data storytelling in IML, introduces the DIST Pyramid to align data storytelling with IML, and presents the I-P-O Model to describe their interactions. It further develops an architecture to explain AI decisions through distinct "What-if" and "Why-not" event-generation processes. The architecture also employs data desensitization to protect sensitive input data. To validate the approach, a case study is conducted with the Boston Housing dataset, using SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) values and large language models (LLMs) to generate data stories with And-But-Therefore (ABT) structures. An empirical evaluation shows that 76.4% and 74.3% of respondents rated the "What-if" and "Why-not" data stories as more comprehensible, with significantly higher accessibility scores than traditional SHAP visualizations. The paper concludes with the presentation of a narrative interpretation framework that integrates IML and data storytelling, thereby expanding the research scope as well as the practical applicability of AI decision-making.

</details>


### [274] [Are LLMs Good Financial User Simulators? A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15727)
*Jiajie He,Jiangyuan Hong,Dongling Ni,Wenjin Liu,Xintong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在120名志愿者的模拟纸面交易环境中，评估LLM作为用户模拟器预测个体金融决策的能力，发现市场信息有助于预测交易动作与标的，但交易规模预测困难，且存在系统性行为压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益被用作用户模拟器，但其复现个体不断演化的金融决策的能力尚不明确，缺乏实证刻画。

Method: 在受控的纸面交易环境中开展初步研究：120名志愿者使用不可赎回的虚拟资金在真实市场条件下交易，未接入真实经纪账户、真实资金持仓或真实交易记录。仅向模拟器提供预测截止时点之前可得的信息，令其预测参与者下一交易日的动作、交易证券及交易数量；评估按时间对齐的滚动预测，并对比有无时点市场信息的设置（消融实验）。

Result: 在受控消融中，市场上下文改善了动作与标的（ticker）预测，而交易规模（下单数量）预测依然困难。同时观察到系统性的行为压缩：模型过度生成持有（hold）动作、低估卖出决策，并简化多证券交易。

Conclusion: 研究提供了初步的实证刻画，表明LLM模拟个体金融决策存在行为压缩偏差，需在更大规模上评估个体、时间及组合层面的行为保真度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as user simulators, but their ability to reproduce evolving individual financial decisions remains unclear. We present a preliminary study in a controlled paper-trading environment with 120 volunteers. Participants used non-redeemable virtual funds under real-time market conditions; no real brokerage accounts, real-money positions, or real transaction records were accessed. Given only information available before a prediction cutoff, a simulator predicts the participant's next-trading-day action, traded security, and transaction quantity. We evaluate temporally aligned rolling predictions and compare settings with and without point-in-time market information. Market context improves action and ticker prediction in the controlled ablation, while transaction sizing remains difficult. We also observe systematic behavioral compression: models overproduce hold actions, underpredict sell decisions, and simplify multi-security transactions. These results provide an initial empirical characterization and motivate larger-scale evaluation of individual, temporal, and portfolio-level behavioral fidelity.

</details>


### [275] [Design of a Deep Learning Credit Risk Early Warning System Integrating Multi-source Heterogeneous Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15744)
*LiYang Wang,Zhen Zhong,Zhen Tian,Keyu Chen,Keyu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于异构信息融合的信用风险预警系统，通过深度神经网络与注意力机制从多源数据中提取多维特征，实现企业与个人信用风险的早期识别，测试表明其预警准确性与时效性显著优于传统规则引擎方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融风险预警系统常因信息孤岛与监测延迟而效率低下，亟需借助数据融合与实时分析技术应对复杂领域挑战。

Method: 构建融合深度神经网络与注意力机制的模型架构，对交易行为、社交网络等异构数据源进行多维特征提取，并建立面向企业及个人信用风险的早期识别机制。

Result: 系统测试显示该方法显著提升了风险预警的准确性与时效性，性能优于传统基于规则引擎的解决方案。

Conclusion: 研究为金融风险早期干预提供了创新思路，对维护金融稳定具有实际意义。

Abstract: Advancements in data fusion and real-time analytics technologies have opened new avenues for addressing complex domain challenges. Financial risk early warning systems often suffer from inefficiency due to information silos and monitoring delays. This paper proposes a credit risk early warning system based on heterogeneous information fusion. The system employs a model architecture integrating deep neural networks and attention mechanisms to extract multidimensional features from diverse data sources such as transaction behaviors and social networks, thereby establishing an early identification mechanism for corporate and individual credit risks. System testing demonstrates that this approach significantly enhances the accuracy and timeliness of risk warnings, outperforming traditional rule-based engine solutions. The findings offer innovative insights for early intervention in financial risks, holding practical significance for safeguarding financial stability.

</details>


### [276] [EvoOntology: A Self-Evolving Ontology Layer for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15779)
*Meiduo Chong,Shaolei Zhang,Ju Fan,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 EvoOntology，一种面向数据智能体的自演化本体层，将本体封装为 MCP 服务器，支持智能体在运行时主动查询与交互，从而弥合智能体与异构数据之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据智能体需在表格、文件、数据库等异构数据上执行自然语言指令，但存在"智能体—数据鸿沟"：异构数据位于智能体之外，智能体只能借助通用工具访问列名、文件路径等有限信息。现有方法要么让智能体直接探索原始数据源，要么将人工构建的语义层注入提示词，二者均难以扩展到大规摸异构数据源，也无法适应不同的智能体行为。

Method: EvoOntology 将本体封装为包含模式层、内容层和工具层的 MCP 服务器，使智能体可在运行时主动查询和交互本体。论文引入一个 builder agent 用于自主构建本体，并设计自演化循环，通过归因引导的类型化编辑持续精炼本体，且编辑仅在通过骨干模型条件化的配对评估后才被接受。

Result: 在三个广泛采用的数据智能体基准和四个 LLM 骨干模型上进行实验，EvoOntology 一致优于强基线及现有语义层方法，有效弥合智能体—数据鸿沟，实现对异构数据更有效的交互。

Conclusion: EvoOntology 通过自演化本体层与 MCP 服务器封装，解决了现有方法难以扩展和难以适应智能体行为的问题，在多个基准和骨干模型上验证了其有效性，为数据智能体与异构数据的交互提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Data agents aim to fulfill natural-language instructions over heterogeneous data, including tables, files, and databases. However, data agents face a challenging agent-data gap: heterogeneous data resides outside the agent, while the agent can access it (e.g., column names and file paths) only through generic tools. Existing approaches either let agents directly explore raw data sources or inject manually constructed semantic layers into prompts. However, neither scales well to large heterogeneous data sources nor adapts to different agent behaviors. In this paper, we introduce EvoOntology, a self-evolving ontology layer for data agents. EvoOntology encapsulates the ontology as an MCP server comprising a schema layer, a content layer, and a tool layer, enabling agents to actively query and interact with the ontology at runtime. To this end, we introduce a builder agent for autonomous ontology construction and a self-evolution loop that continuously refines the ontology through attribution-guided typed edits that are accepted only after a backbone-conditional paired evaluation. Experiments on three well-adopted data-agent benchmarks with four LLM backbones demonstrate that EvoOntology consistently outperforms strong baselines and existing semantic-layer approaches, effectively bridging the agent-data gap and enabling more effective interaction with heterogeneous data. Code: https://github.com/ruc-datalab/EvoOntology

</details>


### [277] [KnowBench: Effort Reduction as a Unified, Deployment-Grounded Benchmark for Clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15794)
*Jocelyn Kang,Caroline Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 KnowBench 基准，以"工作量削减率（Effort Reduction, ER）"为统一指标，用临床医生"审阅并签署"事件作为真值，衡量临床 AI 实际削减的负担而非与参考产物的相似度；文档场景下的百万级实测显示聚合 ER 为 97.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床 AI 评测工具源自研究场景（基于参考的相似度指标、专家评分量表），衡量的是"与某产物的相似程度"，而非"临床负担的削减程度"，这与临床 AI 的真正价值目标错位，且缺乏可审计、可跨系统比较的报告规范。

Method: 提出 Effort Reduction（ER）指标：即系统生成的临床工作产物中，经专家与安全审查后被责任临床医生接受的比例。ER 统一定义、按任务实例化，覆盖病历记录、诊断与计费编码、医嘱、EHR 图表摘要、患者就诊后摘要、临床决策支持等行政工作。每次实例化构造一致：医生审阅-签署事件为 ground truth，被接受单元计为系统完成的工作，每次修正计为返还给医生的残余工作量。论文核心贡献为该基准本身，包括指标、退化情形，以及使 ER 声明可审计、可跨系统比较的报告协议。

Result: 给出文档场景的初步实测：在超过六个月的生产窗口、十三个医学专科、逾一百万次已签署就诊中，Knowtex 专有微调临床基础模型在闭环反馈架构内运行，实现聚合 ER 97.99%，各专科聚合值介于 96.8%–98.9%。本次发布仅部分报告协议清单，并声明哪些配套统计量被保留未公开。

Conclusion: 基准被公开提出，目的是使该数字及此后报告的每一个数字都能被置于同一标准下检验；ER 以医生签署行为为真值，将评测焦点从"像不像"转向"省了多少工作量"。

Abstract: Clinical AI systems are evaluated with instruments built for research settings (reference-based similarity metrics and expert rubric panels) that measure resemblance to an artifact rather than reduction of a burden. We introduce KnowBench, pioneered by Knowtex, whose unifying metric is Effort Reduction (ER): the proportion of system-generated clinical work product accepted by the responsible clinician under expert and safety review. ER is defined once and instantiated per task across the administrative workload clinical AI automates: visit notes, diagnosis and billing codes, orders, EHR chart summarization, patient after-visit summaries, and clinical decision support. In every instantiation the construction is identical: the clinician's review-and-attestation event is the ground truth, every accepted unit is work the system completed, and every correction is residual effort returned to the clinician. The primary contribution of this paper is the benchmark itself: the metric, its degenerate cases, and a reporting protocol under which ER claims are auditable and cross-system comparable. Alongside it we report an initial headline measurement from the documentation instantiation: over one million signed encounters across a production window exceeding six months and thirteen medical specialties, Knowtex's proprietary fine-tuned clinical foundation models operating inside a closed feedback architecture achieve an aggregate ER of 97.99%, with per-specialty aggregates spanning 96.8-98.9%. This release reports the protocol's checklist partially, and states which companion statistics are withheld; the benchmark is offered so that this figure, and every figure reported after it, can be held to the same standard.

</details>


### [278] [Navigating Sparse Evidence: Agentic Visual RAG via Explicit Context Selection and Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15800)
*Yucheng Shen,Lingyong Yan,Jiulong Wu,Shuaiqiang Wang,Jianmin WU,Dawei Yin,Min Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 SCoRE，一种用于视觉检索增强生成的智能体框架，通过显式证据选择与整合，将答案生成与探索过程解耦，实现严格的视觉溯源与端到端优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉检索增强生成（VRAG）需检索相关页面图像作为视觉证据并进行推理，但面临两大挑战：一是与答案相关的证据稀疏，可能集中在单页小区域或分散于多页；二是现有智能体方法往往基于原始探索轨迹或压缩的文本记忆生成答案，而非显式组织的支撑图像集合，导致答案易受探索噪声干扰，且证据支撑的推理链被掩盖。作者认为瓶颈不仅在于证据发现，更在于答案生成前的证据保存与组织。

Method: SCoRE（Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence）是一个统一的智能体循环，包含显式证据选择与整合。探索阶段：仅在维护的文本账本中保留与查询相关的观察及其来源指针，既保存早期证据又限制视觉上下文长度。终止阶段：重新加载被引用的原始图像，整合视觉证据用于回答，并将其排列为逻辑序列。该设计将最终推理与探索性试错解耦，并通过带索引的声明—图像链接确保严格的视觉溯源。训练范式结合过滤式冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习，其奖励促进证据覆盖、整合紧凑性和答案正确性。

Result: 通过过滤式冷启动轨迹蒸馏与证据感知强化学习的端到端优化，SCoRE 在证据覆盖、整合紧凑性及答案正确性方面获得提升（摘要未给出具体数值结果）。

Conclusion: SCoRE 将 VRAG 的瓶颈从单纯的证据发现扩展到证据的保存与组织，通过显式选择与整合机制实现探索与推理解耦，并以索引化图像链接保证视觉溯源，为视觉富文档问答提供了更鲁棒的智能体范式。

Abstract: Visual Retrieval-Augmented Generation (VRAG) empowers models to navigate and answer queries about visually rich documents by retrieving relevant page images as visual evidence and reasoning over their content. However, effectively utilizing this visual evidence is usually impeded by two main challenges. First, answer-relevant evidence is sparse and may be concentrated in a small region of one page or dispersed across multiple pages. Second, existing agentic methods often generate answers based on raw exploration trajectories or compressed textual memories rather than an explicitly organized set of supporting images, making answers susceptible to exploration noise and obscuring the evidence-backed reasoning trace. We argue that the bottleneck lies not only in evidence discovery but also in its preservation and organization before answer generation. We propose SCoRE (Selection and Consolidation for Robust Evidence), a unified agent loop for explicit evidence selection and consolidation. During exploration, SCoRE retains only query-relevant observations and their source pointers in a maintained textual ledger, preserving earlier evidence while keeping the visual context bounded. At termination, it reloads the referenced original images and consolidates the visual evidence for answering, arranging it into a logical sequence. This decouples final reasoning from exploratory trial-and-error while ensuring strict visual grounding via indexed claim-to-image linkages. To enable end-to-end optimization of this unified rollout, our training paradigm combines filtered cold-start trajectory distillation with evidence-aware reinforcement learning, whose reward promotes evidence coverage, consolidation compactness, and answer correctness.

</details>


### [279] [When Should a World Model Move? Loss-Conditioned State Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15801)
*Jintao Xu,Zhengyu Chen,Ben Zhang,Yongzhi Qi,Jianshen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"损失条件状态执行"这一模型无关方法：只有当可行性提案在独立校准单元上相对保持当前状态具有正向同时下置信界时才执行该提案，从而将"状态可动性"与"某提案的收益"区分开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测信息量本身并不能说明更新是否会降低下游损失。发生排名可以接近完美，而保持现状仍是绝对损失下唯一的最优贝叶斯行动；两个转移律也可能共享发生信息与条件方差，却要求相反的绝对损失决策。因此需要把事件可预测性与状态执行分开评估。

Method: 形式化状态可动性为"存在可降低损失的可行修正"，并将其与特定提案的收益区分。方法从预测分布构造损失特定的可行提案，在独立校准单元上评估其相对保持现状的分组有界损失增益，仅在具有正向同时下置信界的分组中执行提案。对固定提案和有界单元损失的分组，证明当校准单元为目标总体的i.i.d.抽样时，每个被接受分组以高概率具有低于保持现状的期望损失。

Result: 在公开预测与动作条件动力学基准上展示了被支持的更新以及认证与覆盖率之间的权衡。在28,684条留出的M4月度序列上，方法对14.0%的序列执行提案，有界损失0.588，而保持现状为0.599、始终执行提案为0.621；两组比较的配对95%自助法区间均位于零以下。在JD.com六种不健康库存类型的约束预测中，强发生排名信号与基于损失的保持现状偏好共存。

Conclusion: 事件可预测性与状态执行必须分开评估；将执行决策建立在损失条件的校准置信界之上，可在控制风险的前提下选择性地采纳世界模型的可行提案。

Abstract: We introduce loss-conditioned state execution, a model-agnostic method that decides whether to execute a world model's fixed feasible proposal or retain the current state. Predictive informativeness alone, however, does not establish whether an update will reduce downstream loss. Occurrence ranking can approach perfection while persistence remains the unique absolute-loss Bayes action. Two transition laws can also share occurrence information and conditional variance yet require opposite absolute-loss decisions. We formalize state movability as the existence of a loss-reducing feasible correction and distinguish it from the benefit of a particular proposal. Our method constructs a loss-specific feasible proposal from a predictive distribution and evaluates its groupwise bounded-loss gain over persistence on independent calibration units. The proposal is executed only in groups with a positive simultaneous lower confidence bound. For fixed proposals and groups with bounded unit losses, we prove that every accepted group has lower expected loss than persistence with high probability when calibration units are i.i.d. draws from the target population. Experiments on public forecasting and action-conditioned dynamics benchmarks show supported updates and a trade-off between certification and coverage. On 28,684 held-out M4 Monthly series, the method executes the proposal for 14.0% of series and achieves bounded loss 0.588, compared with 0.599 for persistence and 0.621 for always executing the proposal. The paired 95% bootstrap intervals for both comparisons lie below zero. In constrained forecasting of six unhealthy-inventory types from JD$\mbox{.}$com, a leading e-retailer in China, strong occurrence-ranking signal coexists with a loss-based preference for persistence, illustrating why event predictability and state execution must be evaluated separately.

</details>


### [280] [Atria Dawn: The Dawn of Agentic Superintelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15818)
*Honglin Guo,Tao Gui,Yicheng Chen,Guanting Dong,Qiming Ge,Yuyang Hu,Zixian Huang,Jiajie Jin,Alexander Lam,Yining Li,Jiahang Lin,Yanjiang Liu,Xinyu Lu,Haijun Lv,Junlin Shang,Qisheng Su,Guoqiang Wang,Rui Wang,Zhecan Wang,Hao Xiang,Xinchen Xie,Shuhao Xing,Xiaoyu Xing,Wanghan Xu,Xinyu Yang,Yajie Yang,Chengfeng Zhao,Haoran Zhao,Ruojun Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Yicheng Zou,Kun Cai,Qiye Cai,Xinmeng Che,Haodong Chen,Jiabei Chen,Jiahao Chen,Jiayi Chen,Yujia Chen,Lizhi Cui,Youheng Dai,Xin Deng,Yi Dong,Shihan Dou,Chenya Gu,Xu Guo,Ding Han,Feiyang Hao,Haotan He,Jie Hou,Binze Hu,Zijian Hu,Junhao Huang,Huicheng Jiang,Jiazhen Jiang,Shufan Jiang,Jiahao Kuang,Bowen Lai,Bo Li,Jiaqiang Li,Peng Li,Qilong Li,Zhuoqun Li,Jiaxiang Liu,Shuainan Liu,Tong Liu,Yi Liu,Zhonghang Lu,Jianwen Luo,Yanyi Luo,Huijie Lv,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Houcheng Min,Chengjun Pan,Qiyuan Peng,Xiaoxuan Peng,Jianmin Qian,Jiantao Qiu,Wanying Ren,Huayu Sha,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Bing Shao,Zhuohui Sheng,Jiayang Shi,Yang Shu,Aierpanjiang Simayi,Sirui Song,Yuxiao Song,Zhe Sun,Zhichao Sun,Wenzhe Tan,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Hanchen Wang,Pengbo Wang,Rui Wang,Yiding Wang,Yuhui Wang,Zhiheng Xi,Caijun Xu,Chao Xu,Yongfeng Xu,Xiaolei Yang,Zhixiong Yang,Qian Yao,Shihong Yi,Yuankai Ying,Jia Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Hao Yuan,Junjie Yuan,Bo Zhang,Caixian Zhang,Qiuyinzhe Zhang,Jiyuan Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Ying Zhao,Pujun Zheng,Xiaoxue Zhong,Xiaohao Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Guanru Zhu,Yulun Zhu,Yaojie Lu,Tao Ji,Hongyu Lin,Yutao Zhu,Pengfei Cao,Guoxiu He,Xianpei Han,Ben He,Zhicheng Dou,Kang Liu,Qi Zhang,Le Sun,Jun Zhao,Ji-Rong Wen,Xuanjing Huang,Yu-Gang Jiang,Bowen Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍面向科研与工程工作流的智能体基础语言模型 Atria Dawn Preview，采用可验证经验流水线训练，在16个基准中5项取得最高分，并通过769条任务记录揭示人机协作由任务执行转向项目级伙伴关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体开始参与其继任者的研发，重塑了智能生产方式与人类研究者的角色；作者希望拓展智能体在真实世界中的生产力边界，并理解背后真实的人机协作过程。

Method: 提出 Atria Dawn Preview 基础智能体语言模型，通过可验证经验流水线（Verifiable Experience Pipeline）将工具中介的交互与可执行环境及外部可验证结果相连接进行训练；同时以该模型自身研发过程为案例，分析56名参与者的769条任务记录与智能体日志。

Result: 在覆盖真实科研、工程与数字工作的16个基准上，模型与前沿智能体相当，并在其中5项取得已报告的最高分；在可比条件下评估已完成任务时，参与者认为约三分之一由AI辅助完成的任务在无AI时不可行；智能体常提出方法并实施修改，而人类保留多数最终决策并通过判断与反馈引导探索。

Conclusion: 人机协作正从任务级执行转向项目级伙伴关系，人类精力集中于“什么值得追求”以及证据如何指导研究；走向更自主的AI研究需同时提升发现能力与有意义的人类监督能力，保持人类对风险和发展方向的问责权威。

Abstract: As AI agents become participants in the development of their successors, they reshape both the production of intelligence and the role of human researchers. We introduce Atria Dawn Preview, a foundation agentic language model designed for scientific research and engineering workflows, with the goal of expanding the frontier of agent productivity in the real world. This model is trained via a Verifiable Experience Pipeline that connects tool-mediated interactions to executable environments and externally verified outcomes. Across 16 benchmarks spanning real-world research, engineering, and digital work, Atria Dawn Preview is competitive with frontier agents and achieves the highest reported score on five of them. Beyond standalone performance, we examine the real research-and-development process behind this model as a case study of human--AI collaboration, analyzing 769 task records from 56 participants together with agent logs. When asked to evaluate completed tasks under comparable conditions, participants rated about one-third of completed AI-assisted tasks as infeasible without AI. More strikingly, agents frequently propose methods and implement revisions, while humans retain most final decisions and guide exploration through judgment and feedback. These observations indicate a shift from task-level execution to project-level partnership, with human effort concentrating on what is worth pursuing and how evidence should guide research. Progress toward more autonomous AI research must therefore advance both the capacity for discovery and the capacity for meaningful human oversight, preserving accountable human authority over the risks and direction of continued development.

</details>


### [281] [AlgoEvo: Self-Evolving Agentic Search for Automated Algorithm Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15820)
*Junhao Qiu,Qinglong Hu,Xialiang Tong,Mingxuan Yuan,Liyong Lin,Qingfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AlgoEvo是一个统一的智能体框架，将自动化算法发现重构为交互式、知识积累的过程，通过动态代码检视编辑、设计技能中心与分层经验机制，在六项基准任务中以更少评估与token消耗达到或超越专用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的算法发现框架受限于预定义控制流的刚性搜索流程，制约了自适应推理，阻碍跨范式迁移，并浪费了宝贵的执行反馈。

Method: 提出AlgoEvo统一智能体框架：自主智能体基于运行时反馈动态检视、诊断并编辑代码；设计技能中心将范式相关知识从核心发现引擎解耦，使单一工作流可处理单目标、多目标与多组件设计；分层经验机制将搜索轨迹组织为任务级树以引导探索，并将跨任务模式整合为可复用技能。

Result: 在六项代表性基准任务上，AlgoEvo以显著更少的评估次数和更低的token消耗，达到或超越专用方法，并展现出强任务内积累、跨任务迁移能力，以及通过灵活技能激活复现或超越现有最优性能的能力。

Conclusion: AlgoEvo通过交互式智能体设计与知识积累机制，突破了刚性搜索流程的限制，为自动化算法发现提供了一种统一、高效且可迁移的新范式。

Abstract: Large language models have advanced automated algorithm discovery by synthesizing executable code, but existing frameworks trap them in rigid search pipelines with pre-defined control flows. This limitation restricts adaptive reasoning, blocks cross-paradigm transfer, and discards valuable execution feedback. We propose AlgoEvo, a unified agentic framework that transforms automated algorithm discovery into an interactive, knowledge-accumulating process. An autonomous agent dynamically inspects, diagnoses, and edits code based on runtime feedback. A design skill hub decouples paradigm-specific knowledge from the core discovery engine, allowing a single workflow to seamlessly handle single-objective, multi-objective, and multi-component design. Meanwhile, a hierarchical experience mechanism organizes search trajectories into a task-level tree to guide exploration and consolidates cross-task patterns into reusable skills. Across six representative benchmark tasks, AlgoEvo matches or surpasses specialized methods with substantially fewer evaluations and reduced token consumption, demonstrating strong intra-task accumulation, cross-task transfer, and the ability to reproduce or exceed existing state-of-the-art performance through flexible skill activation.

</details>


### [282] [LongAgent: History-Guided Agentic Search for Longitudinal Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15859)
*Siyao Wang,Florian Guitton,Shuojie Fu,Guanyu Tao,Kai Sun,Wenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 LongAgent，一种基于智能体的方法，可自动搜索变量集合、时间窗口与纵向聚合函数的组合，以识别具有预测潜力的候选方案，并在合成数据与真实临床数据上验证其效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学纵向数据本质上是异质的：变量数量庞大、来源多样、采样时间间隔不同，且反映健康状况的不同侧面。因此从这类数据中提取具有预测价值的信息表示面临三大挑战——识别有预测力的变量、处理纵向信息、以及整合多变量进行结局预测。

Method: 提出 LongAgent，一种基于智能体的方法，能够自主搜索变量集合、时间窗口与纵向聚合函数的组合空间，并利用历史搜索记忆与数值证据来指导后续探索。

Result: 在合成数据上，LongAgent 取得平均预测 RMSE 1.7376，较最强非智能体基线提升 0.0151（95% CI：[0.0045, 0.0260]；p=0.0273）；在真实临床数据集上，其表现与最佳基线相当。

Conclusion: LongAgent 通过智能体自主搜索变量、时间窗口与聚合函数的组合，能够有效应对医学纵向数据的异质性，在合成数据上优于最强基线，并在真实临床数据上达到与最佳基线相当的水平。

Abstract: Extracting informative representations from longitudinal data that can predict future outcomes remains a critical challenge in medicine. Medical datasets are inherently heterogeneous, consisting of a large number of variables collected from different sources, sampled with different temporal spacings, and representing different aspects of human health status. This requires identifying those variables with predictive value, processing longitudinal information, and integrating multiple variables for outcome prediction. Here, we propose a novel agent-based approach, LongAgent, that can autonomously search over combinations of variable sets, temporal windows and longitudinal aggregation functions, and identify candidates with promising predictive performance. LongAgent utilises a history memory of previous searches and numerical evidence to guide subsequent exploration. On synthetic data, LongAgent achieves a mean prediction RMSE of 1.7376 and improves over the strongest non-agent baseline by 0.0151 (95% CI: [0.0045,0.0260]; p=0.0273). On a real clinical dataset, it performs comparably to the best baseline.

</details>


### [283] [Pilot Early, Commit Late: A Real-Options Model of Enterprise AI Adoption under Rapid Technological Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15919)
*Gaurav Tewari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建两期不确定性下的AI部署决策模型，将企业的选择分为立即部署、有限试点与等待三种，并给出五个核心时点结论及一个关于学习发生位置的比较结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI技术前沿快速演进、实施具有部分不可逆性，且组织专属能力需通过实践积累，使企业面临特殊的时点选择困境：立即部署可获当期运营价值但面临架构过时风险，等待保留期权但错失当前收益，试点则牺牲当期价值以换取学习。现有研究未系统区分部署、试验与等待三种机制，本文旨在填补该空白。

Method: 建立两期决策模型：企业在立即部署、有限试点和等待之间选择。立即部署获得当期运营价值但暴露于架构过时风险；等待保留在前沿被观测后再采用的期权；试点牺牲当期运营价值以构建组织专属学习而不做完全承诺。模型推导出五个核心时点结论与第六个关于学习发生位置（生产学习与试点特有学习）的比较结论，并扩展至连续时间情形。

Result: 第一，均值保持型前沿不确定性上升会提高等待与试点的价值，当期支付对前沿呈仿射时立即部署价值不变。第二，当已部署架构仅能捕获有限比例的未来改进时，更快的前沿预期进展反而降低立即部署的相对吸引力。第三，试点优于等待当且仅当其所构建能力的期望价值超过其成本。第四，足够有价值的组织专属学习会产生非空的“早试点、晚承诺”最优区域。第五，存在闭式模块化阈值，高于该阈值时立即部署优于最优外部选项。第六，生产学习与试点特有学习对时点边际的影响不同。连续时间扩展复现了不确定性提高采用阈值、而能力与模块化降低该阈值的标准结论。

Conclusion: 本文区分了“部署、试验、等待”三种机制，表明快速技术进步可以理性地增加试验（试点）而不构成不可逆承诺的理由；不确定性提高采用阈值，能力与模块化则降低阈值。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence presents firms with an unusual timing problem. The technology frontier is improving rapidly, implementation is partly irreversible, and organization-specific capabilities are accumulated through action. This paper develops a two-period decision model of AI deployment under uncertainty in which a firm chooses among immediate deployment, a limited pilot, and waiting. Deployment earns current operating value but exposes the firm to architectural obsolescence; waiting preserves the option to adopt after the frontier is observed; a pilot sacrifices current operating value to build organization-specific learning without full commitment. The model yields five central timing results and a sixth comparative result on where learning occurs. First, a mean-preserving increase in frontier uncertainty raises the value of waiting and piloting but leaves immediate deployment unchanged when its payoff is affine in the frontier. Second, faster expected frontier progress can reduce the relative attractiveness of immediate deployment when deployed architecture captures only a limited share of future improvement. Third, a pilot dominates waiting exactly when the expected value of the capability it builds exceeds its cost. Fourth, sufficiently valuable organization-specific learning creates a nonempty region in which "pilot early, commit late" is optimal. Fifth, there is a closed-form modularity threshold above which immediate deployment dominates the best outside option. Sixth, production learning and pilot-specific learning affect the timing margin differently. A continuous-time extension recovers the standard result that uncertainty raises the adoption threshold while capability and modularity lower it. The paper separates deploying, experimenting, and waiting, and shows why rapid progress can rationally increase experimentation without justifying irreversible commitment.

</details>


### [284] [Recurrent GraphNeural NetworkswithSet-BasedAggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15932)
*Blai Bonet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于集合聚合的循环GNN，给出可从权重检验的充分条件，实现网络与模态μ演算片段BΣ°₁（可达性与安全性属性的布尔闭包）之间的双向有效等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环GNN的逻辑刻画依赖多重集聚合、计数（分级）逻辑，以及无法从网络参数验证的停机/接受条件，缺乏可验证的符号解释路径。

Method: 采用集合聚合的循环GNN，识别可从权重检验的充分条件，使网络可编译为公式、公式可编译为网络；证明该类网络与BΣ°₁（单极性不动点及其布尔组合，不含相反极性不动点的复合）的双向有效等价。

Result: 建立了网络类与BΣ°₁的双向有效等价；该片段是有限词汇表上稳定化的精确表达层级；对应关系无需计数逻辑、外部停机信号或非有效接受条件。

Conclusion: 对满足条件的网络，提供了一条从权重到符号解释的可验证路径。

Abstract: Recurrent GNNs iterate message passing to convergence, and their logical characterizations to date rely on multi-set aggregation, graded (counting) logics, and halting or acceptance conditions that cannot be verified from the network's parameters. We study recurrent GNNs with set-based aggregation and identify sufficient conditions checkable from the weights for networks to compile into formulas and formulas into networks. The main result is an effective, two-directional equivalence between a class of networks and the Boolean closure of reachability and safety properties, the fragment B$Σ^{\circ}_1$ of the modal $μ$-calculus. The fragment is not an artifact: it is the exact expressive level of stabilization over finite vocabulary, which supports fixed points of a single polarity and Boolean combinations thereof, but not the composition of fixed points of opposite polarities. The correspondence needs no counting logic, no external halting signal, and no non-effective acceptance condition, yielding a verifiable path from weights to symbolic explanations for networks meeting the conditions.

</details>


### [285] [Stellar Colosseum: A Many-Agent Harness for Long-Horizon Research in Mathematics and Theoretical Computer Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15983)
*Honghao Lin,David P. Woodruff,Yuan Deng,Jieming Mao,Song Zuo,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模型无关的推理调度框架 Colosseum，通过策略探索、成熟度门控、子问题分解与验证反馈回刺，在数学与理论计算机科学的长期研究问题上实现可靠长程推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型能生成看似合理的短证明，但在长周期研究问题上仍不可靠，因为此类问题的进展依赖一系列不确定且相互依赖的决策；需要在推理层面进行系统化调度与验证。

Method: Colosseum 为模型无关的推理调度框架：在证明构造前探索多种策略；用 readiness gate 判断路线是否成熟以决定是否分解；将证明计划表示为相互依赖的章节级子问题；把验证器发现回传到论证的受影响部分。各阶段并行生成候选，并以针对性证伪攻击候选，再通过重叠随机采样树聚合（overlapping random-sample tree aggregation）将候选及其批评合并为单一研究产物。该流程已作为 Long Proof 模式集成进 Google Antigravity 的 Teamwork 框架。

Result: 在开放研究与基准评测中：使用 Gemini 3.1 Pro 与 Colosseum 解决了 FOCS、JMLR 等顶会论文遗留的若干开放问题；在 TCS-Bench（源自 FOCS/STOC/SODA 的研究级定理证明任务）上，结合 Gemini 3.1 Pro 与 Gemini 3.7 Flash 达到 71.0% 准确率；另在 Codeforces 评测中，带执行反馈的证明导向流程解出 222 题中的 218 题。

Conclusion: Colosseum 表明，面向长程研究的推理调度与验证反馈机制可显著提升语言模型在数学与理论计算机科学开放问题上的可靠性，并具备向工程化框架（如 Antigravity Teamwork）迁移的通用性。

Abstract: Language models can produce plausible short proofs, but may still be unreliable on long-horizon research problems, where progress depends on a sequence of uncertain and interdependent decisions. We introduce Stellar Colosseum, a model-agnostic harness for allocating inference across research in mathematics and theoretical computer science. Colosseum explores alternative strategies before proof construction, uses a readiness gate to decide when a route is mature enough to decompose, represents the proof plan as interdependent section-level subproblems, and routes verifier findings back to the affected part of the argument. Across these stages, it generates candidates in parallel, attacks them with targeted falsification, and combines candidates and their critiques into a single research artifact through overlapping random-sample tree aggregation. The Colosseum workflow has also been integrated into Google Antigravity's Teamwork framework as the Long Proof pattern.
  We demonstrate the capabilities of Colosseum through open-ended research and evaluations on theorem-proving and competitive programming benchmarks. Using Colosseum with Gemini 3.1 Pro, we obtain several new results that address open problems arising from papers published at top venues such as FOCS and JMLR. On TCS-Bench, a benchmark of research-level theorem-proving tasks drawn from papers published at FOCS, STOC, and SODA, Colosseum achieves 71.0% accuracy using Gemini 3.1 Pro and Gemini 3.7 Flash. In a separate Codeforces evaluation using Gemini 3.1 Pro, the proof-oriented pipeline with execution feedback solves 218 of 222 problems.

</details>


### [286] [Corrupt Plans, Clean Traces: Evading Chain-of-Thought Monitoring with Plan Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15989)
*Keertana Chidambaram,Andrew Ilyas,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究了"计划注入"（plan injection）攻击：在行为模型（actor）的上下文中植入有害但听起来无害的推理内容，可诱导其实施对抗行为并规避思维链（CoT）监控，且在更难任务和更大模型（如DeepSeek-R1）上具有可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控被视为一种安全策略，即由监控模型检查actor模型的推理过程以发现不安全规划、欺骗或失准迹象。本文旨在揭示该安全机制的脆弱性，探究上下文注入是否能绕过监控。

Method: 作者首先在Lanham等（2023）提出的多项选择问答可监控性设置中，采用Li等（2025）的investigator-agent引出框架发现该攻击；随后将攻击推广到更难的任务和更大的模型（如DeepSeek-R1），并测试不同监控资源配置下的检测表现。

Result: 攻击在不同可监控性基准上实现25-33%的监控规避率；actor模型不仅遵循注入计划，还将其改写为自身推理而无明确归因。额外的监控资源可能反而有害：在Bio-Math任务中，向监控模型提供注入计划使检测率下降高达50%；在监控推理预算案例中，额外思考token被用于为注入计划辩护而非标记它。

Conclusion: CoT监控并非可靠的安全防线，上下文中的计划注入可有效规避监控且难以归因，同时增加监控资源可能适得其反，揭示了当前监控机制的重要安全漏洞。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is a safety strategy where the reasoning of a large language model "actor" is inspected by a "monitor" (often another language model) for signs of unsafe planning, deception, or misalignment. We find that planting harmful but benign-sounding reasoning in the actor's context can steer it to perform adversarial actions while evading monitors, an attack we term "plan injection". We initially discover this attack in the multiple-choice question-answering monitorability setting proposed by Lanham et al. (2023), using the investigator-agent elicitation framework of Li et al. (2025). We generalize the attack and show that the discovered behavior scales to harder tasks (achieving 25-33% monitor evasion rates across different monitorability benchmarks) and larger models such as DeepSeek-R1. Across the settings we study, actor models not only follow injected plans but also paraphrase them as their own reasoning, without explicit attribution to the injections. Finally, we find cases where extra monitor resources cause harm - giving the monitor access to the injected plan drops detection by as much as 50% in the Bio-Math task and in a case study on monitor reasoning budget, we find transcripts where additional thinking tokens are spent rationalizing the injected plan rather than flagging it.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [287] [Self-Indexing Attention for Compression-Compatible Sparse Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13205)
*Xu Yang,Jiapeng Zhang,Zhangke,Changjian Chen,Yuxin Chen,Feiqiang Sun,Chengguang Xu,Feng Jin,Zhuo Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Self-Indexing Attention：无需训练，基于共享变换域符号-幅值表示实现 prefill 与 decode 统一检索，5% 注意力密度下性能接近稠密注意力，prefill/decode 注意力算子加速最高 6.1x/10.3x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏长上下文推理需在 prefill 与 decode 两个阶段都高效检索 token。现有方法对两阶段采用不同检索策略，导致同一检索表示无法在全推理过程复用；同时外部 KV-cache 压缩通常需额外索引元数据，增加开销。

Method: 提出 Self-Indexing Attention，一个无需训练的框架，建立在共享的变换域符号-幅值表示上。关键符号构成可复用的 token 级 1-bit 索引，同时服务于分组 prefill 选择与 decode 检索；该表示无需额外索引器元数据即可兼容外部 KV-cache 压缩，并可通过现代加速器广泛支持的位运算实现高效检索。

Result: 在 5% 注意力密度下，LongBench 与 RULER 上性能接近稠密注意力；prefill 注意力算子加速最高 6.1x，decode 最高 10.3x。与 TurboQuant、DeepSeekV4-Flash 的实验进一步验证了其与低比特 KV-cache 压缩及预训练稀疏注意力索引器的兼容性。

Conclusion: 统一的 1-bit 变换域索引可在 prefill 与 decode 间复用，兼顾稀疏检索效率与长上下文精度，并与低比特 KV-cache 压缩及已有稀疏注意力索引器兼容，为长上下文稀疏推理提供了一种无需训练的通用检索方案。

Abstract: Sparse long-context inference requires efficient token retrieval in both prefill and decode. Existing methods often use different retrieval strategies for the two stages, preventing one retrieval representation from being reused throughout inference. We propose Self-Indexing Attention, a training-free framework built on a shared transform-domain sign-magnitude representation. The key signs provide a reusable token-level index for grouped prefill selection and decode retrieval, while the same representation remains compatible with external KV-cache compression without separate indexer metadata. This 1-bit index enables efficient retrieval through bitwise operations widely supported by modern accelerators. At 5% attention density, Self-Indexing Attention remains close to dense attention on LongBench and RULER and achieves up to 6.1x prefill and 10.3x decode attention-operator speedups. Experiments with TurboQuant and DeepSeekV4-Flash further demonstrate compatibility with low-bit KV-cache compression and pretrained sparse-attention indexers.

</details>


### [288] [Decoupling Error Attribution in Cloud-Native Graph-RAG: A Data Integrity Diagnostic Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13324)
*Shuai Yan,Yuhang Wu,Xiaodong Huang,Ke Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出三层解耦诊断框架，将 Graph-RAG 系统错误正交归因于推理损失、知识图谱缺陷与 Cypher 生成错误；在藏东南时空生态知识图谱上实验表明，数据完整性而非算法推理才是性能瓶颈，并发现“参数化知识掩蔽效应”（PKME）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Graph-RAG 系统通常假设数据质量完好，忽视了云原生数据库中数据扰动带来的严重性能影响，缺乏对错误来源的量化归因手段。

Method: 构建三层解耦诊断框架，将系统错误正交分解为推理损失、知识图谱（KG）缺陷、Cypher 生成错误三类；在藏东南时空生态 KG 上针对八种缺陷类型进行评估。

Result: 数据完整性是主导性性能瓶颈，结构性缺陷使系统准确率从 0.93 降至 0.39；观察到“参数化知识掩蔽效应”（PKME），LLM 利用内部记忆补偿断裂的检索路径，使显性查询生成错误缩小逾 70%，掩盖真实存储退化，增加自动监测漏报风险。

Conclusion: 该工作为云端信息融合系统中数据完整性的审计与优化提供了量化基础。

Abstract: Graph-RAG systems often assume pristine data quality, overlooking the severe impact of perturbations in cloud-native databases. This paper proposes a three-layer decoupled diagnostic framework to orthogonally attribute system errors to reasoning loss, Knowledge Graph (KG) defects, and Cypher generation errors. Evaluated on a spatio-temporal ecological KG of the Southeastern Tibet region with eight defect types, results reveal that data integrity, rather than algorithmic reasoning, is the dominant performance bottleneck, with structural defects degrading system accuracy from 0.93 to 0.39. Crucially, we observe a masking-like phenomenon termed the Parametric Knowledge Masking Effect (PKME), suggesting LLMs compensate for broken retrieval paths using internal memory. This shrinks apparent query generation errors by over 70 percent, obscuring actual storage deterioration and increasing the risk of false negatives for automated monitoring. This work provides a quantitative foundation for auditing and optimizing data integrity in cloud-based information fusion systems.

</details>


### [289] [PCGNet: Unifying Shared and Specific Information for Fashion Matching Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13339)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化兼容图网络（PCGNet），一个多目标图学习框架，通过对比互信息最大化、相关性感知邻居采样与可学习全局图增强，有机统一了服装兼容性与用户偏好建模，在两个基准数据集上以四项指标全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时尚领域的互补服装推荐需同时满足用户个性化偏好与单品间兼容性。现有方法存在两大不足：一是将产品兼容性与用户偏好假设为过度简化的解耦关系，忽视了二者的复杂交互；二是数据驱动方法未针对真实时尚数据稀疏、交互噪声大的特点进行优化。

Method: 提出 PCGNet 多目标图学习框架。采用对比互信息最大化提取并对齐共享模式与视图特有模式，以刻画兼容性与偏好的复杂交互；引入相关性感知的邻居采样与可学习全局图增强，从图中直接挖掘自监督信号以稳定表征；最终通过 BPR 排序损失与多视图互信息损失的联合优化生成推荐分数。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上进行实验验证，PCGNet 在全部四项评价指标上均显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: PCGNet 通过图学习方式有机统一兼容性与个性化偏好建模，并借助自监督信号缓解数据稀疏与噪声问题，验证了多目标联合优化在互补服装推荐中的有效性。

Abstract: In fashion domain, recommending complementary clothing items that match selected pieces is a crucial cross-selling technique that improves customer satisfaction. Nevertheless, fashion matching presents significant challenges, as recommendations must not only align with individual user fashion preferences but also ensure compatibility between garments. These challenges are twofold. First, existing models often assume an overly simplified decoupled relationship between product compatibility and personalized user preferences, overlooking the natural complexity between the two. Second, existing data-driven approaches are not optimized for real-world fashion data, which is typically sparse and characterized by noisy interactions. To address these challenges, we propose Personalized Compatibility Graph Network, a multi-objective graph learning framework that organically unifies the modeling of product compatibility and personal preferences. PCGNet uses contrastive mutual information maximization to extract and align shared and view-specific patterns, thereby capturing the complex interplay between compatibility and personal preferences. Moreover, we introduce a correlation-aware neighbor sampling and a learnable global graph augmentation, which enhance the model by incorporating self-supervised signals mined directly from the graph, ensuring more stable and informative representations. Finally, PCGNet generates recommendation scores through the joint optimization of BPR ranking loss and multi-view mutual information losses. Experimental validation on two benchmark datasets demonstrates that PCGNet significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods across all four evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [290] [Mixture-of-Experts Language Models Can Be Strong and Efficient Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13486)
*Anubhav Shrestha,Safal Shrestha,Minwu Kim,Torsten Suel,Keith Ross*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究MoE LLM作为检索器，证明MoE检索器在相近激活参数量下优于稠密检索器，且能以更少激活参数和更低编码时间达到强效果，并通过减少查询编码用的专家数进一步提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调decoder-only LLM可得到强一阶段检索器，效果随骨干规模增大而提升；但每个查询和文档都需经过完整模型，编码成本随模型规模上升。MoE仅激活部分参数，广泛用于扩展生成模型，但作为检索器仍未充分探索。

Method: 采用统一流程从多个模型家族训练MoE与稠密LLM检索器，在多样数据集上评估，并在相同服务配置下测量查询编码时间；同时研究在推理时减少查询编码所用专家数量，无需重训练或重建索引。

Result: 在BEIR上，MoE检索器相比激活参数量相当的稠密检索器最高提升3.0 nDCG@10；最强MoE检索器以少59%激活参数、低18%查询编码时间匹配8B稠密检索器；减少专家数可保留超过99%检索效果，并将查询编码时间最多降低26%；近期重排器相对强MoE一阶段仅有有限增益。

Conclusion: MoE LLM可作为强且高效的一阶段检索器，在效果与效率之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that fine-tuning decoder-only large language models (LLMs) for retrieval yields strong first-stage retrievers, with effectiveness improving as backbones grow in size. However, every query and document must pass through the full model, so encoding cost increases with model size. Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs activate only a subset of parameters per token and are widely used to scale generative models, yet remain underexplored as retrievers. We systematically study MoE backbones for retrieval by training MoE and dense LLMs from several families using the same procedure, evaluating them across diverse datasets, and measuring query encoding time under the same serving configuration. We show that MoE retrievers outperform dense retrievers with comparable active parameter counts by up to 3.0 nDCG@10 points on BEIR. One of our strongest MoE retrievers matches an 8B dense retriever with 59% fewer active parameters and 18% lower query encoding time. We further show that the number of experts used for query encoding can be reduced without retraining or re-indexing, retaining more than 99% of retrieval effectiveness while reducing query encoding time by up to 26%. Recent rerankers provide only modest additional gains over strong MoE first stages, which often match or exceed the reranked configurations we evaluate. Together, these results show that MoE LLMs can be strong and efficient first-stage retrievers.

</details>


### [291] [Pre-retrieval Query Clustering for Adaptive Top-k Document Retrieval in RAG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13489)
*Ye Xia,Emre Yamangil,Haixun Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种查询自适应检索深度框架：离线用NDCG-k曲线估计查询难度与饱和点k*，在嵌入空间聚类并给出各簇推荐top-k，运行时常数时间选择检索深度，实现高覆盖与降本增效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统通常固定检索top-k文档，这一静态策略脆弱：简单查询过度检索（引入噪声与成本），复杂查询检索不足导致召回失败并级联为错误答案。核心问题是对于任意查询，可靠回答究竟需要检索多少文档。

Method: 离线阶段：在默认检索器下以NDCG衡量逐查询的检索难度，并从NDCG-k曲线推导查询特定的“饱和点”k*。由于在线计算这些信号代价高，将大规模查询在嵌入空间聚类，用“均值加方差”规则为每簇汇总一个面向高覆盖率（约95%）的推荐检索深度。运行时：将输入查询分配到簇，常数时间选出对应top-k。该方法属于检索前、以查询为中心，区别于依赖对已检索文档聚类的检索后置信度方法。

Result: 在全流量查询上测试，低复杂度簇上F_1提升超过36%，同时token用量减少14%，且无精度损失。

Conclusion: 该查询自适应检索深度框架在异构、案例型语料上更稳健，可跨法律、医疗、金融与企业搜索等领域应用，兼顾召回覆盖与计算/成本效率。

Abstract: RAG systems commonly retrieve a fixed number of documents (top-k) to ground generation, but this static approach is brittle: simple queries suffer over-retrieval (adding noise and cost) while complex queries are under-retrieved, causing recall failures that cascade into incorrect answers. Motivated by the question of how many documents must be retrieved to answer an arbitrary query reliably, we propose a practical, general framework for query-adaptive retrieval depth. Offline, we estimate per-query retrieval difficulty by measuring NDCG under the default retriever and deriving a query-specific "saturation" point k* from the NDCG-k curve. Because computing these signals online is expensive, we cluster a large set of queries in embedding space and summarize each cluster with a recommended retrieval depth that targets high coverage (e.g., \textasciitilde{}95\%) using a mean-plus-variance rule. At runtime, the system assigns an incoming query to a cluster and selects the corresponding top-k in constant time. Compared with post-retrieval confidence methods that rely on clustering retrieved documents, our approach is pre-retrieval and query-centric, making it robust in heterogeneous, case-like corpora and applicable across domains such as legal, healthcare, finance, and enterprise search. Finally, this framework has been tested in full-traffic queries that improved $F_1$ by over 36\% while reducing token usage by 14\% on low-complexity clusters without accuracy loss.

</details>


### [292] [Addressing Cross-Stage Decoupling of Semantic and Collaborative Signals in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13678)
*Jiayi Dan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SCRec是一个通过双向信息补充增强跨阶段一致性的生成式推荐框架，包含协同增强分词、语义引导生成与流形对齐三个组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐采用两阶段流水线：分词阶段以文本语义为主、协同信号与交互相似性融入有限，导致码本分配与下游生成不匹配；生成阶段又基于交互数据重新嵌入码序列而忽略原始语义信息。这种跨阶段信息解耦损害了语义连贯性与推荐精度。

Method: 提出SCRec框架，通过双向信息补充增强跨阶段一致性：(i) 协同增强分词，在不引入额外对齐任务的前提下，将文本化协同信号显式注入语义分词；(ii) 语义引导生成，在生成阶段用可学习码嵌入动态重校准语义先验；(iii) 流形对齐，调和离散码本索引嵌入空间与稠密连续语义空间之间的几何错配。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提框架的有效性、鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Conclusion: 这些相互关联的组件构成一个通用框架，对齐语义与协同信号并增强跨阶段信息一致性，且仅带来极少的额外训练与推理开销。

Abstract: Generative recommendation reformulates sequential recommendation as autoregressive generation by encoding items into semantic tokens, enabling improved scaling capability and cross-domain generalization. However, existing generative recommender systems typically follow a two-stage pipeline, where item tokenization is largely dominated by textual semantics with limited incorporation of collaborative signals and interaction similarity, leading to code assignments that are misaligned with downstream generation. Conversely, the generation stage tends to overlook the original semantic information, as the code sequences are re-embedded based on interaction data. This cross-stage information decoupling limits semantic coherence and recommendation accuracy.
  To address this issue, we propose SCRec, a general framework that enhances cross-stage coherence through bidirectional information supplementation. Specifically, we introduce (i) collaborative-enhanced tokenization to explicitly inject textualized collaborative signals into semantic tokenization, without introducing additional alignment task, (ii) semantic-guided generation to dynamically recalibrate semantic priors with learnable code embeddings in generation stage, and (iii) manifold alignment to reconcile the geometric mismatch between the embedding space of discrete codebook indices and the dense continuous semantic space. These interrelated components form a general framework that aligns semantic and collaborative signals and enhances cross-stage information coherence, with minimal additional training and inference costs. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness, robustness, and generalizability of our proposed framework.

</details>


### [293] [Odds-Shift Slippage in One-vs-Rest Rankers: Diagnosing and Repairing Reweighting-Induced Top-K Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13810)
*Akifumi Goto*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"odds-shift slippage"(几率偏移滑移)概念,量化并证明了极端正类权重(scale_pos_weight)在有限学习器中的失效机制,并提出逐标签等渗回归修复方案(工具包 oddslip)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: One-vs-rest 排序器处理稀有标签不平衡常使用每标签权重 scale_pos_weight = n_-/n_+。Elkan 恒等式声称该权重可将标签 j 的对数几率偏移 ln w_j,从而模型按加权几率排序。但 Bayes 最优的 precision@K 应按边际概率排序,且权重可能高达数千,有限学习器实际如何处理这种权重、哪种修复有效,此前从未被测量。

Method: 作者定义"odds-shift slippage"为承诺偏移与实际偏移之差,在仅权重不同、其余匹配的 LightGBM 与 MLP 模型对上测量。理论上证明:叶步长上限为 c、学习率为 η 的 booster 在 T 轮中最多实现 Tηc nat 的偏移,并通过上限扫描验证;同时分析饱和单元无界映射的情况。修复方法采用逐标签等渗回归,并对无校准正样本的标签映射到其先验(而非直接透传),引入交叉验证规则避免正样本稀缺时的损害。

Result: Santander 上权重使 MAP@7 从 0.808 降至 0.117,其中理想几率偏移仅解释 23% 的损失(Instacart 32%,同数据 MLP 对达 98%),其余为滑移。公式 Tηc 被上限扫描证实;无上限时饱和单元恰好并列于 1.0,任何可分离映射都无法处理。解析反演仅在偏移已实现且无饱和处有效;逐标签等渗回归将 Santander 恢复至 0.784(用验证期拟合校准器为 0.780)。在 11 个 MULAN 基准和 5 种学习器上,加权模型在 55 个单元中有 8 个损失超过一半 MAP@K;在 delicious 和 Corel5k 上修复回到未加权水平;逐标签校准在正样本稀缺处有害,交叉验证规则可消除该损害。

Conclusion: 极端正类权重带来的几率偏移在有限学习器中大部分无法兑现(slippage),解析反演不足;逐标签等渗回归(配以先验映射与交叉验证规则)是更稳健的修复方案,并已发布为工具包 oddslip。

Abstract: One-vs-rest rankers that show each user the top-$K$ of many rare labels usually counter imbalance with a per-label positive-class weight, scale_pos_weight $= n_-/n_+$. Elkan's identity says such a weight shifts label $j$'s log-odds by $\ln w_j$, so the model ranks by weighted odds rather than by the marginal that is Bayes-optimal for precision@$K$, and suggests inverting the shift afterwards; what a finite learner does with a weight in the thousands, and which repair then works, has not been measured. We call the gap between the promised and the realized shift odds-shift slippage and measure it on matched pairs of LightGBM and MLP models that differ only in the weights. On Santander the weight takes MAP@7 from 0.808 to 0.117; for the boosted pairs the ideal odds shift accounts for 23% of that loss (32% on Instacart; 98% for an MLP pair on the same rows) and slippage for the rest. We prove that a booster whose leaf steps are capped at $c$ realizes at most $Tηc$ nat of shift in $T$ rounds at rate $η$, which a cap sweep confirms, and show that without a cap saturated cells tie at exactly 1.0, beyond the reach of any separable map. The analytic inversion therefore pays only where the shift was realized and nothing saturated, whereas per-label isotonic regression returns the Santander model to 0.784 (0.780 with the calibrator fitted on the validation period), but only if labels without calibration positives are mapped to their prior rather than passed through. On 11 public MULAN benchmarks and 5 learners the weighted model loses more than half of its MAP@$K$ in 8 of 55 cells, and on delicious and Corel5k the same repair returns it to the unweighted level; per-label calibration hurts where positives are scarce, a harm that a cross-validated rule removes. The recipe is released as oddslip.

</details>


### [294] [P3Rec: Distilling Prior--Posterior Preference Reasoning for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13993)
*Jinfei Chen,Weihai Lu,Jiawei Cheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出P³Rec框架，从先验与后验两个视角联合提取并内化LLM偏好推理知识，并通过兴趣熵自适应校准用户表示，兼顾推荐效果与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-as-Enhancer方法将LLM偏好知识蒸馏到轻量推荐器中以避免高昂的在线推理成本，但蒸馏知识往往仅来自单一视角：先验偏好反映用户稳定一致的兴趣，对当前决策指导有限；后验偏好揭示目标相关的细粒度兴趣，却可能过度依赖目标线索。二者各有局限，亟需互补融合。

Method: P³Rec首先从用户侧导出目标无关的先验偏好和目标条件的后验偏好，同时从物品语义与前驱交互中提取以物品为中心的偏好表示；随后通过先验偏好吸收与后验引导的偏好蒸馏，将先验与后验知识逐步内化到行为表示中；最后用兴趣熵刻画历史兴趣分散度，在对比检索优化前自适应校准用户表示。

Result: 在多个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了P³Rec的有效性。

Conclusion: P³Rec通过联合内化互补的先验与后验偏好推理知识，并借助兴趣熵校准表示，实现了更完整的偏好推理，同时保持高效推荐。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong semantic understanding and preference reasoning capabilities, offering new opportunities for user modeling in recommender systems. Existing LLM-as-Enhancer methods typically distill LLM-derived preference knowledge into lightweight recommenders to avoid costly online LLM inference. However, they often construct distillation knowledge from only one perspective.
